### [1] [Concord: A Video Relational Algebra for Cross-Modal Query Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05756)
*Sultan Muratbek,Charisse Ivana Yeung,Chanwut Kittivorawong,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Concord是一个用于表达和优化语义视频查询的系统，通过视频关系代数（VRA）和近似优化重写，大幅减少MLLM的使用量并提升结果质量，在真实视频上实现高达87%的成本降低和显著的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义视频查询结合自然语言提示与多模态大语言模型（MLLM）解释视频，越来越受欢迎。然而，其表达力代价高昂：MLLM可能需要处理数小时媒体才能返回数秒相关输出，导致朴素执行方式缓慢、昂贵且不准确。

Method: 论文提出三个贡献：(1) 引入视频关系代数（VRA），一种嵌套代数，涵盖视频、转录文本、帧和对象轨迹等常见语义视频操作；(2) 推导一组近似优化，重写VRA查询以减少MLLM使用并提高结果质量——对有旁白的视频，用转录替代视频或借助转录识别MLLM处理的视频片段；对无旁白的跨摄像头查询，用检测与跟踪将全视频MLLM连接替换为轨迹级关系连接；(3) 在真实视频上进行评估。

Result: 在4.59小时足球转播和3.92小时讲座视频上，转录到视频查询仅发送源视频时长的5.32%和2.47%给MLLM，MLLM成本降低高达87%。在两个五秒钟高速公路片段中（18辆车经人工裁决的跨摄像头匹配），Detect-Track-Join查询将F1从0.364提升至0.813，且不产生任何MLLM调用。

Conclusion: Concord证明了通过代数化的查询表达与优化重写，可以在保证语义视频查询结果质量的同时显著降低昂贵的MLLM计算成本，为语义视频数据查询提供了高效可行的系统方案。

Abstract: Semantic video queries let users embed natural language prompts and use multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to interpret the video. Such queries are increasingly popular for querying video data. However, their expressiveness comes at a steep cost: an MLLM may process hours of media to return only seconds of relevant output, making naive execution slow, expensive, and inaccurate.
  We propose Concord, a system for expressing and optimizing semantic video queries. We makes three contributions. First, we introduce Video Relational Algebra (VRA), a nested algebra over videos, transcripts, frames, and object tracks that captures common semantic video operations. Second, we derive a set of approximate optimizations that rewrite VRA queries to reduce MLLM usage while improving result quality. For narrated video, Concord either processes transcripts instead of video or uses them to identify video clips for MLLM processing. For cross-camera queries without narration, detection and tracking replace a whole-video MLLM join with a track-level relational join.
  Third, we evaluate Concord on real-world videos. Across 4.59 hours of soccer broadcasts and 3.92 hours of lectures, transcript-to-video queries send only 5.32% and 2.47% of source-video duration to the MLLM and reduce MLLM cost by up to 87%. In two five-second highway clips with 18 manually adjudicated cross-camera vehicles, a Detect-Track-Join query improves F1 from .364 to .813 while making no MLLM calls. See our project at https://concord-db.github.io/.

</details>


### [2] [sheval: An RDF data shapes evaluation tool and test-suite for recursive shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05944)
*Shqiponja Ahmetaj,Iovka Boneva,Jan Hidders,Maxime Jakubowski,Jose-Emilio Labra-Gayo,Wim Martens,Filip Murlak,Cem Okulmus,Ognjen Savković,Mantas Šimkus,Dominik Tomaszuk*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了sheval，一个用于评估和比较ShEx与SHACL两种RDF形状验证技术在递归形状定义下行为的框架，包含工具和测试套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ShEx和SHACL都支持递归形状定义，但ShEx对递归形状有明确的规范语义，而SHACL将递归形状的语义留给各引擎自行实现，导致不同SHACL引擎在处理递归形状时表现不一致，缺乏统一的比较和评估手段。

Method: 提出了sheval评估框架，包含一个工具和一个测试套件，用于对比不同形状技术在递归定义下的行为表现。该工具被应用于评估和理解ShEx与SHACL在递归形状实现上的差异。

Result: 该工具已成功用于评估和识别ShEx与SHACL在递归形状实现中的差异，能够帮助发现不同形状引擎之间的不一致性和潜在问题。

Conclusion: sheval为形状验证技术的测试和比较提供了基础框架，有助于识别实现差异，为RDF数据形状验证领域的进一步研究和开发奠定了基础。

Abstract: Two different languages have been developed to validate RDF data based on the concept of a shape: ShEx and SHACL. In each language it is possible to define a shape that refers to itself, which is called a recursive shape. While in the case of ShEx, the semantics of recursive shapes is well defined and is part of the specification, in the case of SHACL, the semantics of recursive shapes is left to the implementation of the different SHACL engines. Consequently, the different SHACL engines show different behaviours when confronted with recursive shapes. In this paper we present sheval: an evaluation framework consisting of a tool and a test suite that can be used to compare the behaviour of different shapes technologies when confronted with recursive definitions. The tool has been used to evaluate and understand the differences in the implementation of recursive shapes in ShEx and SHACL. It provides a framework for testing and comparing the behaviour of different shape engines, helping to identify inconsistencies and potential issues, and providing a basis for further research and development in the field of shape-based validation of RDF data.

</details>


### [3] [Compiling Linear Datalog to SQL for Program Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06301)
*Amir Shaikhha,Anna Herlihy,Hung Ngo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将Datalog程序（特别是Linear Datalog片段）编译为等价的递归SQL查询的框架，通过中间语言Midlog桥接两者，并恢复函数依赖作为schema键以解锁数据库引擎的优化，从而让现有关系型数据库作为Datalog的评估后端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Datalog在静态程序分析中被证明非常有效，但近期的进展主要来自编程语言和编译器社区（如Soufflé），而现代关系型数据库引擎在递归SQL优化方面已取得显著进步。本文希望重新审视Datalog与关系型数据库之间的联系，利用成熟的数据库引擎来执行Datalog程序。

Method: 提出了一个编译框架，将Datalog程序（特别是Linear Datalog片段）翻译为等价的递归SQL查询。编译器通过中间语言Midlog来弥合Datalog与SQL之间的语义鸿沟，还从程序中恢复函数依赖并将其暴露为数据库schema键，从而启动数据库引擎的标准查询优化。

Result: 在Umbra后端上，该方法的性能比Soufflé引擎高出最多一个数量级，8线程时几何平均加速比为5.46倍；DuckDB在单线程下与Soufflé相当，8线程时较慢（几何平均加速比为0.68倍）。生成的SQL是可移植的，在七个数据库系统上无需修改即可运行。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，递归SQL可以作为Datalog评估的有效后端，同时揭示了关系型数据库引擎若要完全支持大规模程序分析的Datalog所需具备的条件。

Abstract: Datalog is a declarative query language that has proven highly effective for expressing static program analyses. Although Datalog has deep roots in database theory, most recent advances have largely emerged from the programming languages and compiler communities, with systems such as Soufflé. In contrast, modern relational engines have made significant progress in optimizing recursive SQL.
  This paper revisits the connection between Datalog and relational databases, advocating recursive SQL as a backend for Datalog evaluation. We present a compilation framework that translates Datalog programs, particularly those in the Linear Datalog fragment, into equivalent recursive SQL queries. To bridge the gap between Datalog and SQL, the compiler routes every program through an intermediate language called Midlog. The compiler additionally recovers functional dependencies from the program and exposes them as schema keys, unlocking the engine's standard query optimizations. This approach enables existing database engines to execute a broad class of program analyses, outperforming the Soufflé engine by up to an order of magnitude on the Umbra backend. Umbra achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 5.46$\times$ at 8 threads, whereas DuckDB is competitive with Soufflé single-threaded and is slower at 8 threads (geometric-mean speedup of 0.68$\times$). Furthermore, the generated SQL is portable; it runs on seven database systems without any engine modification. Our results highlight what the relational engines require to fully support Datalog for large-scale program analysis.

</details>


### [4] [X-DigCheck: Co-Evolving Application Profiles and Knowledge Graphs, Demonstrated on the RTI Documentation of Rupe Magna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07694)
*Celian Ringwald,Huseyin Erdogan,Valentina Presutti,Cristiano Putzolu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文演示了X-DigCheck，一个领域无关的环境，用于在应用概要文件与其描述的数据共同演化时构建和维护这些概要文件。X-DigCheck将概要文件构建视为一个持续的"本体-数据"共同演化循环：数据根据概要文件被提升为RDF，通过能力问题和SHACL进行检查，生成的报告联合驱动本体、映射、约束和图表的修订。该工具在文化遗产领域得到验证，通过构建RupeMagna-RTI——首个RTI（反射变换成像）专用的CHS-ODP特化版本，并与CIDOC-CRM/CRMdig等标准对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对固定本体开发的应用概要文件会随着时间推移与它们本应捕获的Schema产生漂移，缺乏一种支持概要文件与数据持续共同演化的构建和维护环境。

Method: 提出X-DigCheck，将概要文件构建实现为一个持续的本体-数据共同演化循环。数据依据概要文件被提升为RDF，通过能力问题和SHACL进行验证检查，产生的覆盖率报告反馈驱动本体、映射、约束和图表的联合修订。该循环对领域和产生图表的管道均不敏感。

Result: 在文化遗产领域验证了该工具，成功构建了RupeMagna-RTI（首个RTI特化的CHS-ODP概要文件），与CIDOC-CRM/CRMdig、ArCo、CHAD-KG、Getty AAT对齐，并集成了可移植的共同演化环境与可复用的RTI应用概要文件。

Conclusion: X-DigCheck提供了一个便携式概要文件工程共同演化环境，使概要文件能够与其描述的数据同步演进，识别建模空白和过时假设，并通过实时演示验证了其可用性。

Abstract: We demonstrate X-DigCheck, a domain-independent environment for building and maintaining application profiles as they co-evolve with the data they describe. Profiles developed against a fixed ontology quickly drift from the schema they were meant to capture. X-DigCheck treats profile construction as a continuous ontology-data co-evolution loop: data are lifted into RDF against the profile, checked through competency questions and SHACL, and the resulting reports jointly drive revisions of the ontology, mappings, constraints, and graph. The loop is agnostic to the domain and to the pipeline that produces the graph. We validate and demonstrate the tool in the cultural heritage domain, on the construction of RupeMagna-RTI, the first Reflectance Transformation Imaging (RTI) specialisation of the Cultural Heritage Survey ODP (CHS-ODP), aligned with CIDOC-CRM/CRMdig, ArCo, CHAD-KG, and Getty AAT, with semRTI as the lifting pipeline of this use case. The demonstration lets visitors run one full turn of the loop -on the shipped Rupe Magna (Grosio, Italy) RTI survey, or on a profile and graph of their own -executing the competency-question and SHACL checks live and reading the bidirectional coverage report that flags modelling gaps and stale assumptions. The result is a portable co-evolution environment for profile engineering, together with a reusable RTI application profile produced through it. A screencast of the demonstration is available at https://zenodo.org/records/22210609.

</details>


### [5] [Preserving contextual information in cultural heritage metadata through multidimensional knowledge graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07695)
*Lyndon Nixon,Valentina Presutti,Celian Ringwald,Andrea Schimmenti*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出多维知识图谱（MKGs）的概念基础，用于解决文化遗产对象（CHOs）元数据中语句上下文依赖有效性问题，旨在统一建模和查询跨社会、文化和政治维度的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识图谱无法捕捉语句的上下文依赖有效性，这对文化遗产元数据尤为关键，因其常需容纳演变或冲突的观点（如殖民与后殖民视角、科学共识变迁）。现有方法（溯源、限定符、具体化）虽支持基本情境化，但缺乏统一框架来跨多个社会、文化和政治维度建模与查询。

Method: 引入多维知识图谱（MKGs）的概念基础，作为统一框架来建模和查询跨多维度（社会、文化、政治）上下文的文化遗产对象元数据。

Result: 论文提出MKGs概念框架，能保留文化遗产元数据中复杂、多层次的上下文信息。

Conclusion: MKGs有潜力弥补现有知识表示方法在多元情境建模上的不足，为文化遗产领域提供更语义丰富的上下文感知表示方案。

Abstract: Using Knowledge Graphs (KGs) to describe Cultural Heritage Objects (CHOs) supports semantic richness and interoperability. However, standard KGs fail to capture the context-dependent validity of statements. This limitation is critical for cultural heritage metadata, which must often accommodate evolving or conflicting viewpoints, such as colonial versus post-colonial perspectives or shifting scientific consensus. While current knowledge representation methods address basic contextualization via provenance, qualifiers or reification, they lack a unified framework to simultaneously model and query data across multiple social, cultural, and political dimensions. To bridge this gap, we introduce the conceptual foundations of Multi-dimensional Knowledge Graphs (MKGs) and discuss how they preserve complex, multi-layered contexts in CHO metadata.

</details>


### [6] [TrajectoryDB: A New Database for Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07782)
*Yunjia Zheng,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI代理执行轨迹视为一种独特的数据类型，并构想了一个轨迹原生的数据管理系统TrajectoryDB，该系统协同设计了数据摄取、存储和查询处理，以高效管理和分析代理执轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理产生的丰富执行轨迹数据（包含与LLM、工具和外部环境的交互）对下游任务如记忆提取、模型微调、运行时优化、安全和成本监控越来越有价值。然而，当前轨迹数据分散在文件、数据库和可观测性系统中，缺乏一个围绕其独特结构和访问模式而设计的持久化数据管理系统。

Method: 作者主张将轨迹视为一种独特的数据类型，并基于其三个关键特性（层级执行结构、大量需要语义推理的文本、事件与衍生工件之间的丰富依赖和血缘关系）提出新需求：摄入阶段需重建并保留执行结构和血缘；存储阶段需高效组织大规模但高度冗余的上下文并维护记录间关系；查询阶段需联合推理结构、时序、语义和血缘。因此构想TrajectoryDB，一个轨迹原生的数据管理系统，协同设计这三个环节。

Result: 该论文目前是一个愿景/立场论文，尚未展示实验性结果，主要贡献在于正式提出轨迹作为独立数据类型的论点、分析其在数据生命周期各阶段引入的新需求，并提出TrajectoryDB的架构愿景。

Conclusion: 轨迹数据需要专门的轨迹原生数据管理系统TrajectoryDB来处理，通过协同设计数据摄取、存储和查询处理，才能高效支撑AI代理生态中日益增长的轨迹管理和分析需求。

Abstract: AI agents generate rich execution trajectories that capture their interactions with large language models, tools, and external environments. These trajectories are increasingly valuable for downstream tasks such as memory extraction, model fine-tuning, runtime optimization, and security and cost monitoring. Yet trajectory data today is fragmented across files, databases, and observability systems, with no persistent data management system designed around its unique structure and access patterns.
  We argue that trajectories should be treated as a distinct data type. A trajectory combines hierarchical execution structure, large volumes of text whose analysis often requires semantic reasoning, and rich dependencies and lineage among events, intermediate states, and derived artifacts. These properties introduce new requirements throughout the data lifecycle. Ingestion must reconstruct and preserve execution structure and lineage; storage must efficiently organize large but highly redundant contexts while maintaining relationships among records; and query processing must jointly reason over structure, temporal order, semantics, and lineage.
  We therefore envision TrajectoryDB, a trajectory-native data management system that co-designs ingestion, storage, and query processing to efficiently manage and analyze agent execution trajectories.

</details>


### [7] [Generalized DBLog: A Verified Contract for Interleaving Database Rows with a Change Log](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08160)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文对Generalized DBLog进行了形式化验证，扩展了Netflix提出的DBLog算法，该算法解决了变更数据捕获(CDC)中"复制到日志交接"问题。作者证明了在不同变体（经典水印、Debezium的多种模式、Flink CDC的并行块、以及引擎一致性备份）下，只要满足特定条件，按发出顺序应用复制行和日志变更就能正确重建源数据库行状态。该理论在Isabelle/HOL中机器验证，核心部分在Lean 4中独立验证，协议还通过TLA+有界模型检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDC系统需要将数据库变更日志流式传输到下游系统（缓存、搜索索引、数据仓库）。当引导、添加表或修复数据时，必须复制现有行，但将复制与活跃日志合并会产生"复制到日志交接"问题：变更可能落入间隙，或旧复制状态覆盖新日志更新、复活已删除行。DBLog解决了此问题但缺乏严格的正确性证明，且对算法变体的适用性不清楚。

Method: 论文形式化了DBLog算法及其推广，明确了源数据库和捕获实现必须满足的条件。证明过程覆盖多种变体：经典水印、Debezium信号表和只读模式、Flink CDC的并行块、绑定到精确日志位置的读取和转储、以及日志位置在已知边界内的引擎一致性备份。证明支持跨多个表和键范围，不要求单一数据库快照。验证通过三层手段：Isabelle/HOL中的完整机器检查、Lean 4中的核心独立验证、TLA+中的有界模型检查。

Result: 论文证明了在复制和协调完成后，即使各表和各键范围的行在不同时间被读取，结果仍然成立，且不要求单数据库快照用于复制。进一步的日志变更会逐事件推进重建状态。这些保证覆盖了文中列举的所有算法变体。所有理论均经过机器验证。

Conclusion: Generalized DBLog提供了CDC复制-日志交接问题的严谨理论基础，确立了DBLog算法及其各种变体的正确性条件，填补了此前该算法缺乏正式证明的空白，并通过多种形式化工具交叉验证了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: Change-data capture (CDC) feeds downstream systems like caches, search indexes, and data warehouses from a database's log of committed row changes. When bootstrapping, adding a table, or repairing downstream data, a pipeline must also copy existing rows. Merging this copy with the active log introduces the copy-to-log handoff problem. Changes must not fall through a gap, and older copied state must not overwrite a newer logged update or resurrect a deleted row. DBLog, developed at Netflix, addressed this problem by reading tables in chunks and interleaving those reads with the live log. Watermarks identify the changes that overlap each read, and the log wins when a copied row is stale. Debezium and Flink CDC have since adapted this design. Earlier work proved that applying the original algorithm's copied rows and logged changes in their emitted order reconstructs the source's rows, including the effect of every logged insert, update, and delete processed. Generalized DBLog asks when the same result holds for variants of that design. We state the conditions the source and capture implementation must satisfy. Once copying and reconciliation are complete, we prove that the result holds across all selected tables and key ranges even when their rows were read at different times. A single database snapshot is not required for the copy. Further logged changes advance the reconstructed state one event at a time. We establish these guarantees for classic watermarking, Debezium's signal-table and read-only modes, Flink CDC's parallel chunks, reads and dumps tied to exact log positions, and engine-consistent backups whose log position lies within known bounds. The complete theory is machine-checked in Isabelle/HOL, its core independently verified in Lean 4, and the protocols are also examined by bounded model checking in TLA+.

</details>


### [8] [UnespDataLens-RM: A Reference Model for Analytical Data Engineering with Governance, Quality, Provenance, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08184)
*Ronaldo Celso Messias Correia,Douglas Francisquini Toledo,Camila Tolin Santos da Silva*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出UnespDataLens-RM，一个技术无关的参考模型，用于整合分析数据工程中的技术操作流程与横切能力（治理、质量、溯源、可追溯性、可复现性），以解决分析数据生命周期中的方法论碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析流程和基于证据的决策越来越依赖数据工程，但分析资产的可靠性不仅取决于数据处理能力，还取决于治理、质量保证、溯源、可追溯性、版本管理和可复现性等机制。这些职责通常由不同的模型、框架和操作实践分别处理，导致分析数据生命周期中的方法论碎片化问题。

Method: 采用Design Science Research（设计科学研究）方法开发UnespDataLens-RM参考模型。该模型由8个技术操作模块、8个横切模块、补充维度和一套形式化的工件、指标与验证标准组成，将治理、质量、溯源、可追溯性和可复现性从设计阶段就纳入统一结构中。

Result: 形成了UnespDataLens-RM模型的完整规范，包含技术操作模块、横切模块及相关的工件、指标和验证标准，为后续实例化和实证评估提供了概念与方法论框架。

Conclusion: UnespDataLens-RM为分析数据工程提供了统一且技术无关的参考模型，支持分析管道的规格化、组织与演进，旨在使分析管道更具可治理性、可文档化、可追溯性、可审计性和可复现性。

Abstract: The growing reliance on data in analytical processes and evidence-based decision-making has reinforced the importance of Data Engineering in building pipelines capable of integrating, transforming, validating, and delivering data from heterogeneous sources. However, the reliability of analytical assets depends not only on data processing capabilities but also on mechanisms for governance, quality assurance, provenance, traceability, versioning, and reproducibility throughout their lifecycle. These responsibilities are commonly addressed by different models, frameworks, and operational practices, resulting in methodological fragmentation across the analytical data lifecycle. To address this gap, this article proposes UnespDataLens-RM, a technology-independent reference model that integrates technical-operational processes and cross-cutting capabilities within a unified structure for Analytical Data Engineering. The model aims to support the specification, organization, and evolution of analytical pipelines by incorporating governance, quality, provenance, traceability, and reproducibility from the design stage. Developed following the Design Science Research approach, UnespDataLens-RM comprises eight technical-operational modules, eight cross-cutting modules, complementary dimensions, and a formalized set of artifacts, metrics, and validation criteria. The resulting specification offers a conceptual and methodological framework for future instantiations and empirical evaluations of analytical pipelines designed to be more governable, documented, traceable, auditable, and reproducible.

</details>


### [9] [SmartANN: Object Causal Modeling Boosts Approximate Nearest Neighbor Diagnosis and Auto-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08240)
*Yutong Zhou,Guoxin Kang,Lei Wang,Xueya Zhang,Qinwei Yang,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SmartANN 是一个基于对象因果模型（OCM）的 ANN 瓶颈归因与自动重设计框架。它将 ANN 工作流表示为八个有序、可替换的对象，通过顺序的诊断-替换循环定位瓶颈，并从可插拔动作库中组合兼容动作，生成优化的端到端 ANN 设计。在八个真实数据集上，Recall 提升 0.24–74.20%，QPS 提升 28.8–256.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ANN 算法在索引构建和查询执行中存在相互依赖的阶段，上游性能损失会传播到下游，影响执行行为和可测量输出。现有组件级分析仅比较孤立的设计选择，端到端基准只报告聚合指标，两者都无法追踪跨依赖阶段的损失传播，阻碍了根因归因和自动重设计。

Method: SmartANN 基于 OCM 将 ANN 工作流抽象为八个有序、可替换的对象，采用顺序诊断-替换循环。每轮迭代找出首个偏离预期行为或输出的对象作为瓶颈；因上游瓶颈可能遮蔽下游瓶颈，SmartANN 用测试 oracle 或更优实现替换瓶颈后继续下游诊断。最后从诊断结果和失败原因中，从可插拔动作库组合兼容动作，生成优化的端到端 ANN 设计。实例化覆盖 IVF-PQ 和 HNSW 两类 ANN 家族。

Result: 在八个真实数据集上，SmartANN 将 Recall 提升 0.24–74.20%，在可比 Recall 下 QPS 提升 28.8–256.5%，且诊断与自动设计开销较低。

Conclusion: SmartANN 有效解决了 ANN 系统中跨阶段瓶颈传播难以归因的问题，实现了自动化瓶颈诊断与端到端设计优化，且在不同 ANN 家族上均具泛化能力。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) algorithms achieve high efficiency through interdependent phases across index construction and query execution. This coupling allows upstream performance loss to propagate downstream, affecting execution behavior and measurable outputs. Existing component-level analyses mainly compare isolated design choices, while end-to-end benchmarks report aggregate metrics; neither traces loss propagation across dependent phases, hindering root-cause attribution and automated redesign.
  We present SmartANN, a framework based on the object causal model (OCM) for ANN bottleneck attribution and automated redesign. SmartANN represents an ANN workflow as eight ordered, replaceable objects and diagnoses them with a sequential diagnose-and-replace loop. At each iteration, it identifies the first object deviating from expected behavior or output as a bottleneck. Because an upstream bottleneck can obscure downstream ones, SmartANN replaces it with a test oracle when available, or with an implementation producing a better outcome, then continues downstream diagnosis. From the diagnosed bottlenecks and failure causes, SmartANN composes compatible actions from a pluggable action library to generate an optimized end-to-end ANN design. We instantiate SmartANN for IVF-PQ and HNSW, covering partition-and-quantization and graph-based ANN families.
  Experiments on eight real-world datasets show that SmartANN improves Recall by 0.24--74.20%, and increases QPS by 28.8--256.5% at comparable Recall, with low diagnosis and auto-design overhead. The code is available at https://github.com/zhouyutong20/SmartANN.

</details>


### [10] [WiDiff: Extracting Changes from Wikidata's Edit History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08508)
*Carolina Cortés,Lisa Ehrlinger,Lorena Etcheverry,Felix Naumann*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: WiDiff是一个从Wikidata完整编辑历史中提取变化并提供统一接口的工具，支持对知识图谱演化进行大规模分析查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱的演化研究对下游应用可靠性至关重要，但Wikidata作为最复杂的开放知识图谱案例，虽有多种变更数据暴露格式（如定期转储和实时事件流），却缺乏支持对完整编辑历史进行分析查询的工具。

Method: WiDiff工具从Wikidata的完整编辑历史中提取所有变更，并提供一个统一的接口，用于支持对完整编辑历史的大规模分析查询。

Result: WiDiff提供了对Wikidata超过十年、120多亿实体编辑历史进行大规模分析查询的能力，弥补了现有格式无法支持完整历史分析查询的空白。

Conclusion: WiDiff为研究知识图谱演化（包括真实世界更新、错误修正和破坏性噪音）提供了基础工具支持，可帮助保障下游应用的可靠性。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become a key resource for integrating heterogeneous data and powering downstream tasks such as question answering, entity linking, and semantic search. They are built and maintained incrementally, either (i) fully automated, e.g., YAGO, (ii) semiautomatically with community oversight, e.g., DBpedia, or (iii) manually through collaborative editing, e.g., Wikidata. Understanding the evolution of knowledge graphs is essential as changes may reflect real-world updates, error corrections, or noise introduced by vandalism, all of which affect the reliability of downstream applications. Among openly available knowledge graphs, Wikidata is the most challenging case to study evolution, with over 120 million entities edited by humans and bots and an edit history spanning more than a decade. Although Wikidata exposes change data in various formats (e.g., periodic dumps and real-time event streams), none support analytical queries over the complete edit history. Therefore, we present WiDiff, a tool that extracts changes from Wikidata's complete edit history and provides a unified interface for large-scale analytical queries over it.

</details>


### [11] [SQLMorph: Query Mutation and Fine-Grained Metrics for Text-to-SQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08950)
*Mohammadhossein Malekpour,Mohamed Riahi,Maxime Lamothe,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SQLMorph是一个面向Text-to-SQL系统的评估框架，通过查询变异技术（Join Query Expansion和Textual Query Augmentation）自动生成和扩展评测集，并引入执行级细粒度指标（Execution Precision、Execution Recall和F1）来替代传统的二值指标（如Execution Accuracy），从而更好地捕捉系统在复杂结构和高语言变体下的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL评测面临两大瓶颈：公开基准无法反映企业级schema的复杂性，而构建私有评测集成本高且不确定性强，导致结果难以复现。同时，传统的二值执行准确率指标过于粗略，无法揭示系统在过度预测和欠预测方面的细微差异。

Method: SQLMorph提出两种自动生成评测集的技术：（1）Join Query Expansion (JQE)通过系统性地添加有效的join操作来逐步增加查询的结构复杂度；（2）Textual Query Augmentation (TQA)生成可控的自然语言扰动以测试系统对语言变体的鲁棒性。此外，SQLMorph引入执行级指标Execution Precision (EXP)和Execution Recall (EXR)，并通过F1进行统一评分，以提供比二值指标更细粒度的分析。

Result: 实验表明，JQE增加了查询覆盖率并揭示了随着join数量增加系统准确率下降的现象；TQA显示重度缩写引起的语言脆弱性可使准确率下降高达17%。新的细粒度指标（EXP/EXR/F1）能够揭示二值指标无法区分的系统间差异，支持对过度预测和欠预测进行精细分析。

Conclusion: SQLMorph的查询变异技术和细粒度指标共同支持系统调试，并使Text-to-SQL的评测实践更贴近真实世界部署的需求。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems translate natural language queries into executable SQL, democratizing access to structured data. Despite recent advances driven by large language models (LLMs), evaluation remains a major bottleneck: public benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of enterprise schema, while building private evaluation sets is costly and nondeterministic, making evaluation results difficult to reproduce. To address this issue, we present SQLMorph, a framework for Text-to-SQL evaluation via query mutation. SQLMorph introduces two techniques to automatically generate and expand evaluation sets: Join Query Expansion (JQE), which systematically increases structural complexity through valid join additions, and Textual Query Augmentation (TQA), which generates controlled natural language perturbations to assess robustness to linguistic variation. JQE and TQA create targeted choke points to challenge specific system components. When applied to state-of-the-art systems, JQE increases query coverage and reveals accuracy degradation as the number of joins grows. Meanwhile, TQA shows that linguistic brittleness induced by heavy abbreviation can reduce accuracy by up to 17%.
  Beyond evaluation sets, SQLMorph introduces a family of execution-level metrics that address the limitations of current binary measures, such as Execution Accuracy. We define Execution Precision (EXP) and Execution Recall (EXR) to quantify the fraction of correct and recovered results, respectively, and combine them via F1 for unified scoring. Our experiments show that these relaxed metrics enable fine-grained analysis of over- and under-prediction, revealing differences across systems that binary metrics obscure. Together, SQLMorph's query mutation and fine-grained metrics support debugging and better align Text-to-SQL evaluation practices with real-world deployments.

</details>


### [12] [Factorized and Vectorized Execution: Optimizing Analytical and Semantic Queries over Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09002)
*Sunny Yasser,Anas Dorbani,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FFX是一个支持任意因子分解方案的全向量化流水线查询引擎，通过packed factorized vectors及配套算子保持缓存友好的紧凑布局，还通过将因子分解中间结果序列化为LLM提示来协同优化语义算子，大幅降低token消耗和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多对多连接在分析和语义工作负载（如欺诈检测、网络分析、推荐）中常常产生爆炸性增长的中间结果，数量级上可能远超输入。因子化表示通过利用属性间的条件独立性来更紧凑地编码中间结果，在部分情况下可将输出规模渐近地降到最坏输出规模以下。然而，在现代向量化查询处理器中采用因子化面临根本性矛盾：因子化表示是层次化的，而向量化执行建立在扁平、块导向的处理之上。已有方法要么依赖全物化，要么仅支持受限的因子化布局，无法同时兼顾因子化与向量化的全部优势。

Method: FFX设计了packed factorized vectors和相应算子，在保持缓存友好和连续内存布局的同时支持任意因子化模式。这是首个支持任意因子化方案且保持完全向量化的流水线引擎。此外，FFX还通过将因子化中间结果序列化为紧凑的LLM提示来协同优化语义算子（如大语言模型推理），以降低token使用和推理成本。

Result: FFX实现了高效的join密集型分析查询执行，包括增强语义算子的查询。在保持输出质量的同时大幅降低token使用和推理成本，某些情况下甚至还能提升输出质量。

Conclusion: FFX证明了因子化表示与全向量化执行并非互斥，其统一的流水线设计能够同时获得因子化压缩中间结果和向量化高速处理的双重优势，并拓展到了LLM语义算子的协同优化场景中。

Abstract: Many-to-many joins are central to analytical and semantic workloads such as fraud detection, network analysis, and recommendation, where insights arise from relationships between entities. These workloads often suffer from an explosion of intermediate results, sometimes orders of magnitude larger than the inputs. Factorized representations address this problem by exploiting conditional independence among attributes to encode intermediates more compactly. In some cases, they can reduce the output size asymptotically below the worst-case output size. However, adopting factorization in modern vectorized query processors remains challenging: factorized representations are hierarchical, whereas vectorized execution is built around flat, block-oriented processing. Prior approaches either rely on full materialization or support only restricted factorization layouts, sacrificing much of the benefits of both factorization and vectorization.
  We present FFX, a novel engine for Fast Factorized eXecution. FFX is the first pipelined engine to support arbitrary factorization schemes while preserving full vectorization. The engine introduces packed factorized vectors and operators that maintain cache-friendly, contiguous layouts. Beyond analytics, FFX also co-optimizes semantic operators by serializing factorized intermediates into compact prompts for large language models (LLMs), substantially reducing token usage and inference cost while maintaining output quality and, in some cases, improving it. Together, these contributions enable efficient execution of join-heavy analytical queries, including queries augmented with semantic operators.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [13] [The 2M Multiplication Algorithm for Complex Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05419)
*Peter Caday*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将经典的3M复数矩阵乘法算法（需3次实数GEMM）降为2M算法（仅需2次同尺寸实数GEMM），适用于整数实部与虚部的矩阵；对于浮点矩阵，2M与Ozaki-II方案结合，得到实用高性能的复数GEMM算法，耗时约为同尺寸实数GEMM的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复数矩阵乘法需4次实数GEMM，3M算法可降至3次但仍有二次时间的前后处理开销。作者希望进一步减少实数GEMM次数（从3次降至2次），从而提升复数矩阵乘法的计算效率。

Method: 针对整数实部和虚部的矩阵，提出2M乘法算法（仅需2次实数GEMM，加二次时间前后处理）。针对浮点矩阵，将2M乘法与Ozaki-II误差补偿方案自然结合，形成实用高性能的复数浮点GEMM算法。作为推论，还导出了内部使用完整矩形GEMM的对称秩k更新（SYRK/HERK）新算法。

Result: 复数GEMM从3次实数GEMM降为2次；浮点场景下可在约为实数GEMM两倍的时间内计算复数浮点GEMM；并得到新的SYRK/HERK算法。

Conclusion: 2M算法显著降低了复数矩阵乘法的实数GEMM调用次数，且与Ozaki-II结合后为浮点复数GEMM提供了实用高效的高性能实现路径。

Abstract: Complex matrix multiplication is typically computed using 4 real matrix multiplications (GEMMs) of the same size. The well-known 3M multiplication algorithm reduces this cost to 3 real GEMMs, together with quadratic time pre- and post-processing steps.
  In this paper, we reduce 3M to 2M for matrices with integer real and imaginary parts, performing complex GEMM with only 2 real GEMMs of the same size, along with quadratic time pre- and post-processing. For floating-point matrices, 2M multiplication combines naturally with the Ozaki-II scheme, yielding a practical, high-performance algorithm for computing a complex floating-point GEMM in roughly twice the time of a real GEMM of the same size. As corollaries, we derive new algorithms for symmetric rank-$k$ updates (SYRK/HERK) that internally use full rectangular GEMMs.

</details>


### [14] [Sustained 70B-Class AWQ Inference on a Single NVIDIA L20: Throughput, Stability, Energy, and Quality Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05420)
*Yin Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文技术报告评估了单块NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU能否支撑70B级量化模型的推理服务。使用vLLM和AWQ Marlin服务Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ，在24小时并发10的固定负载测试中完成36,740/36,740请求，零失败，维持108.84输出token/s，估计能耗7.92kWh（0.330输出token/J）。并发16条件平均127.22输出token/s。质量评测（MMLU 0.8130、CMMLU 0.8309、GSM8K 0.8082）表明该配置可作为吞吐导向的70B级端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战70B级语言模型必须依赖80GB加速器、张量并行多GPU系统或厂商托管推理配置的普遍认知，探索单块消费级48GB GPU能否承载有用的70B量化服务负载，以降低部署门槛和成本。

Method: 在单块NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU上，使用vLLM 0.8.5.post1和AWQ Marlin量化内核服务Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ。设计了24小时并发10的固定形状负载浸泡测试（约512输入token+256输出token），以及并发1/4/8/16的重复固定形状运行测试，并通过nvidia-smi采样GPU功耗估算能耗，同时进行MMLU/CMMLU/GSM8K等质量评估。

Result: 24小时浸泡测试中36,740/36,740个请求全部完成，无请求失败、无CUDA OOM或进程崩溃；维持108.84输出token/s，p95 TTFT为6.61s，p95端到端延迟23.54s；估计耗电7.92kWh，对应0.330输出token/J。并发16条件三次运行平均127.22±12.68输出token/s。质量评分：MMLU 0.8130、CMMLU 0.8309、GSM8K 0.8082，MT-Bench和8K LongBench子集均完成。

Conclusion: 精心配置的单块L20可以承载Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ作为吞吐导向的70B级端点（在测试的固定形状负载下）。但结果不证明无损AWQ质量保持、低延迟交互式服务、广泛的SLA覆盖或与BF16/FP16基线等价性，结论范围限定且谨慎。

Abstract: Serving 70B-class open-weight language models is usually associated with 80GB accelerators, tensor-parallel multi-GPU systems, or vendor-managed inference profiles. This technical report evaluates whether a single NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU can sustain a useful 70B-class quantized serving workload. We measure Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ served with vLLM 0.8.5.post1 and AWQ Marlin on one L20. Under a fixed workload of approximately 512 input tokens and 256 output tokens, a 24-hour concurrency-10 soak completed 36,740/36,740 requests with no request failures and no vLLM CUDA OOM, traceback, or killed-process signatures. The system sustained 108.84 output tokens/s, with p95 time-to-first-token of 6.61s and p95 end-to-end latency of 23.54s. GPU-board power sampled through nvidia-smi produced an estimated 7.92 kWh over the run, corresponding to 0.330 output tokens/J and 1.008 total tokens/J. Repeated fixed-shape runs at concurrency 1, 4, 8, and 16 completed 12/12 runs successfully; the concurrency-16 condition averaged 127.22 +/- 12.68 output tokens/s over three runs. The same AWQ endpoint also produced absolute quality scores of 0.8130 on MMLU, 0.8309 on CMMLU, and 0.8082 on GSM8K, plus 80/80 MT-Bench answer generations and a 60-item 8K LongBench subset. The evidence supports a narrow claim: a carefully configured single L20 can serve Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ as a throughput-oriented 70B-class endpoint under the tested fixed-shape workload. It does not prove lossless AWQ quality retention, low-latency interactive serving, broad production SLA coverage, or equivalence to a BF16/FP16 baseline.

</details>


### [15] [Toward a Formally Verified Optimality Certificate for OGR(29): A SAT-Encoding Methods Note with Small-Case Demos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05421)
*Tong Niu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑的CNF编码用于求解Golomb ruler最优问题GR(n,L)，编码规模为O(n²L)个子句，并在n≤12的小规模情形下生成了机器可验证的LRAT最优性证书。文章还针对OGR(29,L=757)给出编码规模估计，提出了cube-and-conquer分解方案用于Mallob风格的并行计算，验证流水线端到端完整。作者指出，若能为n>5的任一OGR值生成形式化验证的最优性证明，将是该领域的首次突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Golomb ruler最优问题是经典组合优化基准问题。OGR(29)正在分布式计算中，最近的上界757收紧了搜索窗口。作者希望提供一种紧凑且可验证的SAT编码，使OGR值的证明能通过形式化验证（LRAT证书），弥补此前分布式搜索仅给出结果而缺乏机器可检查证明的不足。

Method: 提出O(n²L)个子句的紧凑CNF编码来判定GR(n,L)决策问题；对n≤12进行小规模验证扫描，生成LRAT最优性证书；给出OGR(29,L=757)的编码规模闭式估计；提出cube-and-conquer分解策略以适配Mallob风格的并行求解；验证流水线为CaDiCaL(--lrat=true)生成证书，经结构性健全性检查后由drat-trim或cake_lpr进行形式化验证。

Result: 为n≤12的OGR值提供了机器可检查的LRAT最优性证书。给出了OGR(29)在L=757时的编码规模估计（含子句数和变量数的闭式表达式）。设计了可行的并行分解方案和端到端证书验证流程。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向形式化验证的Golomb ruler最优性证明的重要一步。若能扩展到大规模n（如n=29），将是该领域首个超越平凡情形(n≤5)的正式验证最优性证明，为其他分布式组合搜索结果的机器验证树立范式。

Abstract: A Golomb ruler of order~$n$ is an integer set $\{a_0<a_1<\cdots<a_{n-1}\}$ whose $\binom{n}{2}$ pairwise differences $a_j-a_i$ ($i<j$) are all distinct. The optimal Golomb ruler problem asks for $\mathrm{OGR}(n)=\min\{a_{n-1}-a_0\}$ and is a classical combinatorial benchmark. The values $\mathrm{OGR}(2),\dots,\mathrm{OGR}(28)$ are settled through distributed volunteer search (the Distributed.net OGR project); $\mathrm{OGR}(29)$ is in active computation, with verification expected in late 2026 or early 2027. The recent upper bound $\mathrm{OGR}(29)\le 757$ of Lee, Park, and Kim (arXiv:2510.0122, October~2025) tightens the search window. This note describes a compact CNF encoding of the decision problem $\mathrm{GR}(n,L)$ (``is there a Golomb ruler of order~$n$ with length exactly~$L$?'') with $O(n^2 L)$ clauses, together with a small-case verification sweep that emits machine-checkable LRAT certificates of optimality for $\mathrm{OGR}(n)$ at $n\le 12$. We then give closed-form encoding-size estimates for $\mathrm{OGR}(29, L=757)$ and propose a cube-and-conquer decomposition aimed at a Mallob-style parallel run on commodity multi-core hardware. The certificate pipeline (CaDiCaL with --lrat=true, a structural sanity-check, then formal validation by drat-trim or cake\_lpr) is end-to-end. A formally verified optimality proof for any single $\mathrm{OGR}(n)$ value beyond the trivial $n\le 5$ would be a first in the field.

</details>

*Florian Trimmel,Cynthia Marcelino,Thomas Pusztai,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出STORE，一个面向serverless函数的自供给存储架构。STORE将自供给逻辑移至平台层，自动选择最优存储后端，实现零接触、零配置的供给，无需开发者手动配置存储系统，显著降低实现工作量和配置错误风险。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless函数通常是无状态的，依赖外部存储服务来交换数据或支持有状态功能。然而，配置这些存储系统需要手动设置或编写声明式脚本，增加了复杂性、拖慢了开发速度，并增加了配置错误的风险。

Method: STORE通过在平台层面内嵌自供给逻辑，自动选择最优存储后端，实现零接触和零配置的供给方式，从而消除开发者的手动配置负担。

Result: 评估结果显示，STORE相比Terraform和Pulumi等成熟的IaC框架，可减少最多84%的实现工作量，同时在真实工作负载下保持低延迟和线性可扩展性，且不引入性能开销。

Conclusion: STORE通过平台级自供给架构有效解决了serverless存储配置的复杂性难题，在降低开发成本的同时维持了优异的性能表现，为serverless存储管理提供了更简洁高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Serverless computing provides on-demand elasticity, pay-per-use, and simplified deployment. However, serverless functions are typically stateless and depend on external storage services such as object stores or databases to exchange data or support stateful functions. Provisioning and configuring these storage systems still requires manual setup or declarative scripts, introducing complexity, slowing development, and increasing the risk of configuration errors. To address these challenges, in this paper, we introduce \textsc{STORE}, a self-provisioning storage architecture for serverless functions. STORE automatically selects the optimal storage backend and eliminates developer effort through zero-touch and zero-configuration provisioning, achieved by moving the self-provisioning logic to the platform level. Our evaluation results show that STORE reduces implementation effort by up to 84% compared to well-established Infrastructure-as-Code frameworks such as Terraform and Pulumi while maintaining low latency and linear scalability under realistic workloads, without introducing performance overhead.

</details>

*Yuchen Zhu,Longxiang Yin,Wanyu Wang,Jieke Lin,Guoqiang Zou,Zirui Cao,Yuling Yuan,Xiaolan Fan,Lifen Chen,Hao Zheng,Qizhang He,Hongyu Zhou,Chunhai Yu*

TL;DR: 本文面向具身智能计算中的感知-计算-控制一体化需求，在国产飞腾FT-2000/4处理器与寒武纪MLU370加速卡构成的异构平台上，提出NPU片上融合部署与全流程算子协同优化策略，实现了单帧18.7ms全流程延迟、相比NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier 2.89倍加速及82.6%的MLU利用率。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent computing integrates perception, computation and control. Traditional separate deployment of the three tasks leads to frequent data transmission, high latency and low hardware efficiency, failing to satisfy millisecond-level real-time requirements in dynamic scenarios. Besides, most operator optimization methods rely on foreign GPU platforms, while full-process collaborative optimization for domestic Phytium-Cambricon heterogeneous architectures is insufficient. This paper builds a domestic heterogeneous computing platform with Phytium FT-2000/4 processor and Cambricon MLU370 acceleration card, and proposes an NPU on-chip fusion deployment and full-process operator collaborative optimization strategy for perception, computation and control pipelines. Targeting embodied robot applications, modular optimization is conducted, including MLU hardware adaptation of motion blur correction operators for high-speed imaging, lightweight inference optimization of ViT models, and customized operator development for multi-DOF inverse kinematics solution. An on-chip data closed-loop and pipeline collaboration-based single-card solution is proposed to implement integrated execution of all perception-computation-control tasks on MLU370. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves a full-process single-frame latency of 18.7 ms and a speedup of 2.89 compared with NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier, with 82.6% MLU utilization and comparable accuracy to mainstream platforms. This work offers a practical reference for domestic engineering applications of integrated embodied intelligent computing services.

</details>


### [18] [Measurement-Driven Diagnosis and Mitigation of Host-CPU Co-location Interference in Single-GPU LLM Serving on a Multi-GPU Server](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05425)
*Guanjie Cheng,Guowei Li,Yingying Wen,Xinkui Zhao,Zhe Liu,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了单GPU LLM推理服务器中宿主CPU与外部CPU工作负载的共存干扰问题。作者发现核心问题并非GPU内核变慢，而是CPU工作负载放大了GPU任务提交前CPU侧服务阶段的尾部延迟。为此提出Core Path Tail Index (CPTI)和Core Tail Suppression (CTS)两个指标，并构建了CoTail诊断程序来选择操作系统级保护策略，实验验证其显著提升nginx吞吐量并降低TPOT，同时减少实时调度使用并降低共租户性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU服务器中的宿主CPU在LLM推理期间经常利用率不足，与外部CPU工作负载共存可提升资源利用，但会严重损害服务质量。现有工作主要改进LLM服务引擎或研究CPU-GPU边界延迟，对外部CPU负载如何影响服务路径以及运维人员如何选择保护策略缺乏指导。

Method: 1) 观察到主要问题不是GPU内核变慢，而是CPU工作负载放大GPU工作提交前CPU侧服务阶段的尾部延迟；2) 引入CPTI（核心路径尾部指数）和CTS（核心尾部抑制）指标量化该效应；3) 构建CoTail，一种测量驱动的诊断流程，包括筛选工作负载风险、剖析服务阶段尾部、选择OS级保护、验证decode SLO合规。

Result: 未保护时nginx共存降低吞吐量78.8%，TTFT增加429.5%，TPOT增加362.4%。CoTail引导的保护使nginx吞吐量提升最高4.4倍，TPOT降低4.5倍。在共同基线部署SLO下，CoTail满足全部12个oracle可行保留用例（对比Always-rt的10/12和Macro-only的11/12），并将RT使用从28降至22个用例，平均共租户性能下降从56.65%降至51.21%。

Conclusion: CoTail通过系统化诊断CPU侧服务阶段的尾部延迟并提供OS级保护选择，能够有效缓解外部CPU工作负载对LLM服务的干扰，在保证SLO的同时减少对实时调度的依赖，并降低对共租户负载的负面影响。

Abstract: Host CPUs in GPU servers are often under-used during LLM inference. Co-locating CPU workloads can improve resource use, but it can also seriously hurt serving quality. Existing work mainly improves LLM serving engines or studies CPU-GPU boundary delays. It gives limited guidance on how external CPU workloads affect the serving path and how operators should choose protection policies. This paper studies host-CPU co-location interference in single-GPU LLM serving. We show that the main observed problem is not slower GPU kernels. Instead, CPU workloads amplify long tails in CPU-side serving stages before GPU work is submitted. To capture this effect, we introduce the Core Path Tail Index (CPTI) and Core Tail Suppression (CTS). Based on these metrics, we build CoTail, a measurement-driven diagnostic procedure that screens workload risk, profiles serving-stage tails, selects OS-level protections, and validates decode SLO compliance. In our primary setup, unprotected nginx co-location reduces throughput by 78.8%, increases TTFT by 429.5%, and increases TPOT by 362.4%. CoTail-guided protections improve nginx throughput by up to 4.4x and reduce TPOT by 4.5x. Under a common-baseline deployment SLO, CoTail satisfies all 12 oracle-feasible held-out cases, compared with 10/12 for Always-rt and 11/12 for Macro-only. It also reduces RT usage from 28 to 22 cases and lowers mean co-tenant slowdown from 56.65% to 51.21%.

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Lemma Sharing -- Novel Parallelization Strategies for Property Directed Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05426)
*Verner Vlačić*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于共享的新型并行化策略（preemptive propagation 和 ARPOS），用于硬件模型检查器 rIC3 中的 Property Directed Reachability (PDR) 技术，并在异步 MPI 消息传递框架上与传统 lemma sharing 方法进行了性能对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDR 是自动化硬件模型检查中常用的技术，但高效并行化 PDR 仍然是一个重大挑战。现有的并行化方法（如 lemma sharing）在处理器数量增加时存在扩展性受限的问题。

Method: 提出了两种新型的基于共享的并行化策略：preemptive propagation（抢占式传播）和 ARPOS。同时开发了一个基于异步 MPI 消息传递的框架，并将其集成到当前最先进的 rIC3 硬件模型检查器中。

Result: 在 2025 年硬件模型检查竞赛基准测试上的实验结果表明，preemptive propagation 策略相比传统的 lemma sharing 方法带来了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: preemptive propagation 策略在 PDR 并行化方面表现出优于传统 lemma sharing 的性能，为硬件模型检查的并行化提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Property Directed Reachability (PDR) is a commonly used technique for automated hardware model checking, yet efficiently parallelizing it remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches, such as lemma sharing, often suffer from limited scalability as processor counts increase. In this work, we present two novel sharing-based parallelization strategies, preemptive propagation and ARPOS, and compare their performance with classical lemma sharing. To this end, we develop an asynchronous MPI-based message passing framework for the state-of-the-art rIC3 hardware model checker. Experimental results on the 2025 Hardware Model Checking competition benchmark demonstrate that our preemptive propagation strategy yields a significant performance boost over classical lemma sharing.

</details>


### [20] [Constella: A Novel Framework for Cost-Efficient Distributed AI Inference in LEO Space Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05427)
*Andrija Stanisic,Milos Gravara,Juan Luis Herrera,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Constella框架，用于在低地球轨道（LEO）卫星星座构成的空间数据中心中实现高效的AI推理任务分布式执行。该框架通过DNN拆分技术，结合离线资源配置器与在线调度算法，优化了处理器卫星与通信卫星的比例及数据路由策略，相比现有方法可将系统成本降低最多两个数量级，端到端推理延迟降低最多2.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座作为可扩展计算基础设施日益受到关注，可在轨运行AI工作负载。然而，管理异构卫星角色的星座面临成本优化挑战：过度配置处理卫星会增加系统成本，配置不足则会限制系统效率。如何为给定工作负载确定合适的星座规模和卫星角色比例是一个关键难题。

Method: Constella框架包含两个核心组件：1）离线资源标识器，用于确定处理器卫星与通信卫星的最优比例；2）在线分配算法，利用星座遥测信息自适应地在星座内部及向地面站路由数据。整体采用DNN拆分技术实现分布式AI推理。

Result: 在真实卫星数据集上，跨多种复杂度场景进行评估，系统成本降低最多两个数量级，端到端推理延迟较其他方法降低最多2.7倍，同时推理成功率保持不低于81.9%。

Conclusion: Constella能够有效实现在空间数据中心中以成本高效的方式执行AI推理工作负载，通过合理的资源配置和自适应路由策略显著优化系统成本与推理性能。

Abstract: Space data centers built from Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are gaining increasing attention as a scalable computing infrastructure. With access to abundant solar energy and high-throughput optical inter-satellite links, such constellations can run AI workloads directly in orbit, enabling new in-space application types while optimizing existing ones such as Earth observation. However, managing satellite constellations that combine heterogeneous satellite roles introduces a cost optimization challenge. Determining the appropriate constellation size and satellite role ratio for a given workload is challenging, as over-provisioning processing satellites increases system cost, while under-provisioning limits system efficiency. To enable cost-efficient execution of AI inference workloads in such space data centers, we present Constella, a novel framework that leverages DNN splitting for distributed AI inference in LEO satellite constellations. Constella comprises an offline resource identifier that determines the optimal ratio of processor-to-communicator satellites and an online assignment algorithm. The algorithm utilizes constellation telemetry to adaptively route data within the constellation and to ground stations. We evaluate Constella on a real-world satellite dataset across scenarios of increasing complexity. Results demonstrate a reduction in system cost by up to two orders of magnitude and lower end-to-end inference latency by up to 2.7x compared to other approaches, while maintaining no less than 81.9% inference success rate.

</details>


### [21] [Grid Checkpointing: an Innovative Rollback Support for Speculative Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05428)
*Andrea Mazzucchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"网格检查点"(Grid Checkpointing)的技术，用于推测性仿真中的应用程序透明增量检查点。该方法将内存更新操作的拦截与内存映射管理完全分离，通过在对象状态内存区域上使用叠加的网格布局，仅需极少量插桩指令即可追踪内存更新，无需了解对象状态中哪些块实际被分配/更新，也避免了记录被触及内存区域地址的开销。该技术已集成到开源PARSIR软件包中，实验表明其在实现增量检查点时运行开销几乎可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推测性仿真中，增量检查点技术对于状态保存与恢复至关重要，但现有方法通常需要追踪被修改内存区域的地址或深入了解对象状态的内存分配情况，导致较高的运行开销和实现复杂度。作者旨在提出一种更高效、应用透明的检查点机制，以最小化运行开销。

Method: 该方法的核心创新在于：(1) 将内存更新操作的拦截与内存映射管理完全分离，只需极少量插桩指令即可追踪模拟对象状态中的内存更新；(2) 在承载对象状态块的内存区域上设计叠加的网格布局，管理精简数据以实现增量检查点，无需事先知道哪些块真正被分配或更新到对象状态中；(3) 完全避免了像其他现有方案那样记录被触及内存区域的地址。(4) 将方案集成到开源PARSIR(并行仿真运行器)包中。

Result: 通过与文献中其他现有方案的实验对比，结果表明网格检查点方法能够通过内存更新追踪实现增量检查点，且运行时间开销几乎可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 网格检查点技术为推测性仿真中的增量检查点提供了一种高效且应用透明的解决方案，显著降低了内存更新追踪的运行开销，为并行仿真系统中的状态管理提供了更优的实践选择。

Abstract: We present a new technique for application-transparent incremental checkpointing in speculative simulation, referred to as {\em Grid Checkpointing}. With our proposal, we fully separate the interception of memory update operations from memory-map management. This enables tracing memory updates that occur in the state of a simulation object via an extremely limited set of instrumentation instructions. At the methodological level, our proposal exploits an overlaid grid layout on the memory area used to host the chunks of an object state, and manages concise data for enabling incremental checkpointing with no need for knowing which chunks are really allocated/updated into the object state. Additionally, grid checkpointing fully avoids logging the addresses of the touched memory zones, as instead takes place in other existing proposals. We embedded our solution into the PARSIR (PARallel SImulation Runner) open source package and report the results of an experimental study that compares grid checkpointing with other solutions available in the literature. The results demonstrate that our approach enables incremental checkpointing via memory update tracing with nearly negligible runtime cost.

</details>


### [22] [Accelerating Sparse Linear Solvers in OpenFOAM using RISC-V Vector Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05429)
*Gabriele Ceccolini,Federico Ficarelli,Filippo Barbari,Simone Bnà,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对CFD代码在RISC-V向量架构上的加速问题，通过在原型长向量EPAC加速器和商用短向量Sophon SG2044 CPU上，使用RISC-V向量内联函数优化SpMV并将其集成到自定义平滑器中，运行时将内部数据转换为向量友好格式。实验结果显示在EPAC上获得6倍平滑器加速，在Sophon SG2044 (MCv2集群)上获得1.5倍加速，证明传统CFD代码可在研究和商业新兴硬件上有效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenFOAM等CFD框架主要依赖MPI进行域分解级并行，对向量/SIMD架构支持有限（仅编译器自动向量化），且其内部矩阵数据格式不适配向量处理器所需的连续内存访问，导致向量架构在CFD中的应用受限。

Method: 针对两种不同的RISC-V架构（长向量EPAC加速器与短向量Sophon SG2044 CPU），使用RISC-V向量内联函数（intrinsics）优化SpMV核心计算，并将其集成到自定义平滑器中，通过运行时将内部数据格式转换为向量友好的连续内存布局。

Result: 在EPAC测试芯片上平滑器获得6倍加速；在配备Sophon SG2044处理器的Monte Cimone (MCv2)集群上实现1.5倍平滑器加速。

Conclusion: 通过显式向量化与数据格式转换，可以有效克服传统CFD代码在RISC-V向量架构上的性能瓶颈，证明遗留CFD代码能同时在研究型与商用新兴硬件上获得显著加速。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) relies heavily on the efficiency of linear solvers based on sparse linear algebra kernels. Widely used frameworks like OpenFOAM exploit parallelism primarily at the domain decomposition level via MPI. Support for vector/SIMD architectures is limited to compiler auto-vectorization. Furthermore, support for such architectures is limited by OpenFOAM's internal matrix data format, which is intrinsically ill-suited for the contiguous memory accesses required for efficient execution on vector processors. In this work, we focused on two very different RISC-V architectures: the prototype long-vector EPAC accelerator and the commercial short-vector CPU Sophon SG2044. On these platforms, we optimized the Sparse Matrix-Vector multiplication (SpMV) using RISC-V vector intrinsics and integrated it into a custom smoother, performing a runtime conversion of internal data into a vector-friendly format. Experimental results on the EPAC test chip show a 6x speedup for the smoother; benchmarks on Monte Cimone (MCv2) cluster with the Sophon SG2044 processor achieve a 1.5x smoother speedup, proving that legacy CFD codes can be effectively accelerated on both research and commercial emerging hardware.

</details>

Abstract: The effort to tokenize non-currency assets faces several hurdles, including the lack of a scalable decentralized computing infrastructure to manage asset-related metadata. While the centralized securities depository model has served the financial industry well for several decades, the vision of tokenization at a global scale requires new infrastructure that enables distributed control while protecting the integrity of asset-related metadata, regardless of where it is stored. In this paper, we discuss the decentralized artifacts metadata registry model for tokenized assets as a possible direction for the financial industry seeking to embrace tokenization. The artifacts metadata registries extend the function of the traditional CSD, and could in fact be a new type of service offered by CSDs around the world.

</details>


### [24] [COAST: Congestion-Aware Start-Time Recommendations for Carbon-Aware HPC Jobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05443)
*Weibin Feng,Abhishek Dasgupta,Zeynep Duygu Tekler,Sudha Ahuja,Jin Zheng,Xun Jiang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出COAST，一种拥塞感知的HPC作业启动时间推荐机制，通过将决策批次建模为精确势博弈，在碳节省与作业延迟及拥塞之间取得平衡。基于真实HPC能耗数据和电网碳强度轨迹的仿真显示，COAST可减少15.4%碳排放（增加1.5小时平均延迟），相比碳贪婪推荐降低了23.8%的峰值时段负载，同时保留93%的可实现碳节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳感知HPC作业若缺乏协调，会不约而同地集中迁移到低碳时段，导致推荐开始时间槽位拥塞，反而削弱了碳减排的实际效益。因此需要一种能够感知拥塞并协调多个作业启动时间的机制。

Method: COAST将每个决策批次建模为一个精确势博弈（exact potential game），通过最佳响应更新（best-response updates）使推荐稳定收敛。该方法在碳节省、额外作业延迟和推荐时间段拥塞三者之间进行显式权衡，实现拥塞感知的启动时间协调。

Result: 基于公共HPC能耗数据和历史电网碳强度数据的轨迹驱动仿真显示：COAST减少15.4%的估计碳排放，仅引入1.5小时额外平均启动延迟；相比碳贪婪推荐，COAST降低23.8%的峰值时段负载，同时保留93%的可实现碳节省。

Conclusion: 研究量化了拥塞感知的启动时间协调对灵活碳感知HPC作业的潜在收益，表明协调机制能有效缓解推荐时间槽拥塞，避免碳感知调度带来的次生问题。

Abstract: High-performance computing (HPC) workloads consume substantial amounts of electricity, and their carbon emissions vary over time with the carbon intensity of grid electricity. However, uncoordinated shifting of carbon-aware HPC jobs toward low-carbon periods can concentrate recommended start times in the same time slots, creating congestion and eroding the resulting carbon benefits. This paper proposes COAST, a congestion-aware start-time recommendation mechanism that coordinates HPC jobs by balancing carbon savings against additional job delay and congestion in recommended start-time slots. COAST formulates each decision batch as an exact potential game, enabling best-response updates to converge to stable start-time recommendations. Under an idealized realization model that assumes each job can start at its recommended time, we conduct trace-driven simulations using public HPC energy data and historical grid carbon-intensity traces. The results show that COAST reduces estimated carbon emissions by 15.4\% with 1.5 hours of additional average start delay. Compared with carbon-greedy start-time recommendations, COAST reduces peak time-slot load by 23.8\% while preserving 93\% of the achievable carbon savings. These results quantify the potential benefits of congestion-aware start-time coordination for flexible, carbon-aware HPC jobs.

</details>


### [25] [A Three-Layer Caching Architecture for Low-Latency LLM Web Search on Commodity CPU Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05463)
*Ayushman Bhattacharya,Nihal Gazi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OreoLook（原lixSearch），一个开源AI答案引擎，采用三层层级缓存架构解决会话上下文丢失、重复查询和重复嵌入计算等问题。系统在单台8-vCPU服务器上部署，实现了89.3%的Redis键空间命中率、0.1ms读取延迟和仅1.38MB的内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI搜索产品使用量增长，系统遇到三个问题：(1)会话上下文丢失；(2)等价查询触发冗余的LLM调用；(3)URL跨会话被重复嵌入。这些冗余工作导致资源浪费和系统性能下降。

Method: 提出三层层级缓存架构：(1)会话上下文窗口层：在Redis中维护消息的滚动窗口，自动溢出到Huffman压缩的磁盘归档；(2)语义查询缓存层：通过嵌入向量的余弦相似度捕捉查询改写，消除冗余LLM调用；(3)URL嵌入缓存层：跨会话去重嵌入计算。同时配备后台LRU淘汰守护进程，将空闲会话从Redis迁移到磁盘并在需要时重新加载。

Result: 在单台8-vCPU Intel Cascade Lake服务器（2 GHz，32 GB RAM）上运行30个Hypercorn工作进程、三个容器化副本，系统实现了89.3%的聚合Redis键空间命中率、0.1ms读取延迟和仅1.38MB的内存开销。

Conclusion: 该层级缓存架构能够在普通CPU硬件上高效支撑AI答案引擎，支持会话在数小时或数天后按保留策略恢复，证明了轻量级缓存策略对减少LLM冗余调用的有效性。

Abstract: AI-powered search products such as ChatGPT search, Google's AI Overviews, and Perplexity provide LLM-synthesized answers grounded in live web results. We developed OreoLook (formerly lixSearch), an open-source answer engine using automated browser agents and provider-routed LLM inference. Its local search, caching, session-management, and embedding stack runs on commodity CPU hardware; answer synthesis is performed by a remote inference provider. As usage grew, sessions lost context, equivalent queries triggered redundant work, and URLs were repeatedly embedded across sessions.
  We present a three-layer caching architecture: (1) a Session Context Window maintaining a rolling window of recent messages in Redis with automatic overflow to Huffman-compressed disk archives; (2) a Semantic Query Cache catches rephrasings via cosine similarity on embedding vectors, eliminating redundant LLM invocations; and (3) a URL Embedding Cache that deduplicates embedding computations across sessions. Deployed on a single 8-vCPU Intel Cascade Lake server (2 GHz, 32 GB RAM) running 30 Hypercorn worker processes across three containerized replicas, the evaluated system reported an 89.3% aggregate Redis keyspace hit rate with 0.1 ms read latency and just 1.38 MB of memory overhead. A background LRU eviction daemon migrates idle sessions from Redis to disk and re-hydrates them on demand, enabling conversations that can be resumed hours or days later under the configured retention policy.

</details>


### [26] [Toward Sustainable Distributed LLM Inference: A Systems Synthesis and Research Agenda for an Energy-, Carbon-, and Cache-Aware llm-d Control Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05565)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综合性综述论文，将LLM推理系统中零散的研究成果联系起来，探讨分布式推理控制平面（如llm-d）如何实现可持续推理。作者提出可持续推理控制平面（SICP）的概念，将延迟、能耗、碳排、缓存复用、成本和质量纳入路由与扩缩容决策，同时将TTFT/TPOT SLO作为硬约束，并给出基于SLO满意goodput每焦耳和每克CO2e的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可持续性问题正从训练阶段转向服务系统阶段。生产中能耗和碳影响取决于多种因素（工作负载形态、批处理、KV缓存复用、prefill/decode放置、硬件选择、地理碳强度等），而现有系统论文仅分别研究其中少数因素，缺乏将这些成果整合应用于分布式推理控制平面层面的系统性视角。

Method: 本文并非提出新的基准结果，而是采用综合研究方法：将现有文献归纳为重复出现的设计模式，基于这些模式为llm-d设计可持续推理控制平面（SICP）。控制平面在路由和扩缩容时综合考虑延迟、能耗、碳排、缓存复用、服务成本和质量，同时将TTFT/TPOT SLO设为硬约束。此外提出基于SLO满意goodput每焦耳和每克CO2e的评估框架及可复现实验方案。

Result: 主要观察结果（即综合结论）是：可持续LLM推理不太可能来自单一的"绿色"模型或单一加速器，而应自然地被视为一个跨模型、阶段、缓存、硬件、副本、区域和时间的控制问题。文中报告的性能、能耗、碳排和成本改进均来自所引用的论文和系统，本文贡献在于综合与框架设计。

Conclusion: 可持续LLM推理本质上是一个分布式控制问题而非单一硬件或模型选择问题。通过将文献成果综合为llm-d的SICP设计模式，并结合以效率（每焦耳/每克CO2e的SLO满意goodput）为核心的评估框架，可以实现从"点状优化"向"系统级绿色推理"的转变。该研究为推理控制平面的绿色调度提供了结构化的理论蓝图。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) sustainability is increasingly a serving-systems problem, not only a training problem. In production, energy and carbon impact depend on more than model size: workload shape, batching, key-value (KV) cache reuse, prefill/decode placement, model and accelerator choice, power state, geographic carbon intensity, and service-level objectives (SLOs) all matter. Recent systems papers study many of these factors separately. This paper connects those results and asks a practical engineering question: what do they imply when the decision point is a distributed inference control plane such as llm-d?
  The contribution here is synthesis, not a new set of benchmark results. Reported performance, energy, carbon, and cost improvements remain the results of the cited papers and systems. I group the literature into recurring design patterns and use those patterns to sketch a Sustainable Inference Control Plane (SICP) for llm-d. The proposed control plane would consider latency, energy, carbon, cache reuse, serving cost, and quality when routing and scaling, while keeping TTFT/TPOT SLOs as hard constraints. I also outline an evaluation framework based on SLO-satisfied goodput per joule and per gram CO2e, together with a reproducible experimental plan. The main observation from connecting the literature is that sustainable LLM inference is unlikely to come from one "green" model or one accelerator; it is more naturally treated as a control problem across model, phase, cache, hardware, replica, region, and time.

</details>


### [27] [Sharpedo: Dual-Mode Uncertified DAG-Based Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05580)
*Zeno de Angeli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sharpedo，这是首个双模式（dual-mode）非认证有向无环图（uncertified DAG）共识协议，旨在结合部分同步协议的高性能与异步协议的无进展保证，并通过动态调度机制进一步提升系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mysticeti和Mahi-Mahi虽然利用非认证DAG数据结构实现了显著的性能提升，但二者在网络假设上存在权衡：Mysticeti依赖时间界限假设（部分同步）以换取更低延迟，而Mahi-Mahi不依赖任何时序假设（异步）以换取更强的进展保证。虽然已有双模式协议存在，但尚未有协议能够充分利用非认证DAG这一新型数据结构来同时实现两种模式的优势。

Method: 研究首先追踪共识协议的演进历程，分析其对网络假设约束的处理方式，并探索现有的双模式协议。在此基础上，提出Sharpedo——首个双模式非认证DAG共识协议，能够动态适应不同网络假设（部分同步与异步）。此外，Sharpedo集成了一种新颖的动态调度机制，用于改进系统效率和性能优化。

Result: 本文呈现了Sharpedo共识协议的设计，该协议能够在部分同步环境下实现高性能，同时在异步环境下保证进展（liveness），实现了"两全其美"的目标。动态调度机制的引入进一步提升了系统整体效率与性能表现。

Conclusion: Sharpedo填补了非认证DAG共识协议领域双模式设计的空白，在保持异步环境下强进展保证的同时，能够获得类似部分同步协议的高性能，为共识协议的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Mysticeti and Mahi-Mahi represent leading approaches to consensus protocols, leveraging a novel uncertified Directed Acyclic Graph data structure to achieve substantial performance benefits compared to prior work. Mysticeti and Mahi-Mahi differ in their underlying network assumptions, where the former makes assumptions about timing bounds for communication while the latter does not. Mysticeti trades robustness for lower levels of latency in ideal conditions, whereas Mahi-Mahi favours increased latency in order to ensure stronger progress guarantees in asynchronous settings. Although dual-mode protocols exist (i.e., protocols that can adapt do different network assumptions), none have been implemented to take full advantage of the novel data structure seen in the aforementioned protocols. Thus, our research project aims to trace the progression of consensus protocols, analyse how they address network assumptions constraints, and explore dual-mode protocols. Then, we present Sharpedo, the first dual-mode uncertified Directed Acyclic Graph consensus protocols which aims to achieve the best of both worlds: the high-performance of partially-synchronous protocols, while providing guarantees of progress even in asynchronous settings. Furthermore, Sharpedo integrates a novel dynamic scheduling mechanism resulting in improved system efficiency and performance optimization.

</details>


### [28] [DejaVu: Unifying Memory Allocations to Eliminate Redundant Copies on Unified-Memory SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05635)
*Yuheng Zhu,Yanbo Zhao,Jiajia Li,Man-Ki Yoon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DejaVu是一个针对统一内存（UMA）边缘平台上GPU应用的优化系统，通过安全地消除CPU与GPU之间的冗余内存拷贝来提升性能。它提供两条路径：DejaVu-SR（编译时LLVM转换，针对开源代码）和DejaVu-DR（基于profile的二进制优化器，针对闭源部署）。在七个基准测试和三个NVIDIA Jetson平台上，拷贝密集型工作负载提速最高6.9倍，闭源端到端应用加速1.10-1.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在UMA（统一内存架构）边缘平台上，GPU应用仍沿用独立GPU时代的内存抽象——分配CPU和GPU两个缓冲区并在执行前后拷贝数据。由于UMA硬件上这些缓冲区位于同一物理DRAM中，这些拷贝浪费带宽、时间和能量且无实际数据迁移意义。然而简单地合并缓冲区并不安全，因为程序可能依赖两个缓冲区彼此独立，或依赖拷贝操作来排序CPU与GPU的访问顺序。

Method: DejaVu仅在程序不依赖上述副作用时才移除这些拷贝，采用两条互补路径：(1) DejaVu-SR：编译时的LLVM转换，证明安全性并就地重写被接受的拷贝对；(2) DejaVu-DR：针对闭源部署的profile引导二进制优化器，通过运行时profile和验证稳定的分配/拷贝模式，拦截匹配的调用以合并profile匹配的缓冲区对，同时保留被移除拷贝原有的排序效果。

Result: 在三个NVIDIA Jetson平台上的七个基准测试中，DejaVu的性能收益随基线时间中拷贝占比的增长而增大。拷贝密集型工作负载提速高达6.9倍，闭源端到端应用加速1.10-1.14倍。源码和二进制两条路径均能达到手动优化性能的99%以上。

Conclusion: DejaVu证明了在不依赖缓冲区分离或拷贝排序语义的程序中，可以安全且高效地消除UMA平台上的冗余GPU-CPU内存拷贝，且同时支持源码可用和闭源两种部署场景，接近手动优化的性能上限。

Abstract: GPU applications on unified-memory (UMA) edge platforms often inherit a discrete-GPU memory abstraction in which they allocate one buffer for the CPU, another for the GPU, and copy data between them before and after GPU execution. On UMA hardware these buffers reside in the same physical DRAM, so the copies consume bandwidth, time, and energy without moving data across a physical boundary. Despite the growing adoption of UMA platforms, this pattern remains common because production software stacks, libraries, and samples were written for portability across discrete GPUs. However, removing these copies is not as simple as merging the two buffers, because the original program may rely on the two buffers being distinct, or on the copy itself ordering CPU and GPU accesses.
  DejaVu removes these copies only when the program does not depend on the effects above. It does so along two complementary paths, depending on whether source is available. DejaVu-SR is a compile-time LLVM transformation that proves safety and rewrites accepted pairs in place. DejaVu-DR is a profile-guided binary optimizer for closed-source deployments that profiles and validates stable allocation/copy patterns and, at runtime, intercepts the matching calls to coalesce profile-matched pairs while preserving the ordering effects of removed copies.
  Across seven benchmarks on three NVIDIA Jetson platforms, DejaVu's benefit grows with the fraction of baseline time spent on copies. Copy-dominated workloads speed up by up to 6.9$\times$, closed-source end-to-end applications speed up by 1.10-1.14$\times$. Both source and binary paths achieve $\ge$99% of the performance achievable by manual optimization.

</details>


### [29] [CoCoFL_Continual_Computing_for_Federated_Learning_over_Intermittent_Satellite-Ground_Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05997)
*Yun Shen,Kun Guo,Xi Yang,Yaoqi Liu,Yisheng Zhao,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoFL框架，解决LEO卫星辅助联邦学习中部分设备参与导致的数据异构性和资源浪费问题。调度设备参与全局聚合，未调度设备继续更新本地模型（考虑模型陈旧性），并联合优化设备调度与本地训练轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在卫星辅助联邦学习场景中，间歇性的星地链路导致每个可见窗口内只有部分设备能参与全局聚合，未调度设备空闲导致其计算和数据资源利用率低。同时，部分设备参与下的数据异构性会使全局模型产生偏差，损害学习性能。

Method: 提出基于持续计算（continual computing）的联邦学习框架CoCoFL：调度设备参与全局模型聚合，未调度设备考虑模型陈旧性继续更新本地模型。基于收敛性分析，在可见窗口时间约束下联合优化设备调度以及调度/未调度设备的本地epoch数量。

Result: 实验结果表明CoCoFL相比基线方法实现了更快的收敛速度、更低的训练损失和更高的测试准确率。

Conclusion: 通过让未调度设备持续利用本地资源更新模型并合理优化调度策略，CoCoFL有效缓解了卫星联邦学习中部分参与带来的数据异构性偏差问题，显著提升了学习性能。

Abstract: Low earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations enable geographically distributed ground devices to collaboratively train a global model via federated learning (FL) without sharing raw data, with applications in environmental monitoring and disaster prediction. However, in satellite-assisted FL scenarios, intermittent satellite-ground links allow only a subset of devices to participate in global aggregation within each visibility window, leaving unscheduled devices idle and their local computational and data resources underutilized. Under partial device participation, data heterogeneity among devices may bias the global model toward certain devices, thereby deteriorating learning performance. In this regard, we propose a continual computing based federated learning framework, referred to as CoCoFL, in which scheduled devices participate in the global model aggregation, while unscheduled devices continue updating their local models taking into account model staleness. Guided by the convergence analysis of CoCoFL and subject to visible-window-related time constraints, we jointly optimize the device scheduling and the number of local epochs for scheduled and unscheduled devices. Experimental results demonstrate that CoCoFL achieves faster convergence, lower training loss, and higher test accuracy compared with baselines.

</details>


### [30] [Poseidon: DAG-Guided Parallelism Search for LLM Pre-Training on Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06086)
*Xiaosong Chen,Shaoheng Nie,Zhongmin Zhao,Zizhao Mo,Jiapeng Chen,Huanle Xu,Zeren Li,Weiwei Sun,ChengZhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Poseidon是一个面向异构加速器集群的高效可扩展LLM训练框架，通过基于有向无环图的显式训练时间模型，结合阶段级剪枝和层到阶段映射两种理论支撑策略，在不牺牲最优训练效率的前提下大幅缩减配置搜索空间，在异构集群上将训练吞吐量提升最高2.76倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着加速器技术快速发展，在异构加速器集群上预训练LLM对最大化硬件利用率至关重要。但现有系统存在两个主要缺陷：一是训练时间建模不准确，削弱了基于其上的并行化优化效果；二是庞大的配置搜索空间使得穷举探索不可行，被迫在搜索时间与训练效率之间做出权衡。

Method: Poseidon的核心是一个基于有向无环图（DAG）的显式训练时间模型。在此图模型基础上，采用两种高效的、有理论依据的策略：(1) 通过提前停止与部分估计进行阶段级剪枝；(2) 利用脊状分布模式进行层到阶段映射。这两种策略有效缩减搜索空间且不牺牲最优训练效率。

Result: 在异构集群上的实验表明，Poseidon相较现有最先进系统可将训练吞吐量提升最高2.76倍。

Conclusion: Poseidon通过精确的训练时间建模与高效的搜索空间缩减策略，成功解决了异构集群上LLM训练的并行化优化难题，在保证最优训练效率的同时实现了显著的吞吐量提升，展示了在异构环境下高效训练大规模语言模型的可行性。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of accelerator technologies, pre-training large language models (LLMs) on heterogeneous accelerator clusters has become increasingly crucial for maximizing hardware utilization. Existing systems, however, suffer from inaccurate training time modeling, which undermines the parallelization optimizations built upon it. Moreover, for current approaches, the vast configuration search space makes exhaustive exploration infeasible, forcing a trade-off between search time and training efficiency.
  To overcome these limitations, we introduce Poseidon, an efficient and scalable LLM training framework designed with heterogeneity awareness. Its core is an explicit training time model based on a directed acyclic graph. Building on this graph, Poseidon employs two efficient, theoretically grounded strategies: stage-level pruning via early stopping with partial estimation, and layer-to-stage mapping exploiting a ridge-like distribution pattern. These strategies reduce the search space without sacrificing optimal training efficiency. Experiments on heterogeneous clusters show that Poseidon improves training throughput by up to $2.76\times$ over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [31] [Diamond Agent: Agentic Control of Federated HPC Resources as a Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06181)
*Haotian Xie,Junlin Chen,Mingkai Zheng,Yifan Zhu,Minu Mathew,Max Burnette,Yadu Babuji,Volodymyr Kindratenko,Shivaram Venkataraman,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster,Zhao Zhang*
