TL;DR: 本文提出了Diamond Agent，一个智能体系统，通过类型化技能作为接口，实现跨异构集群的HPC工作流智能执行。它统一了跨站点资源发现、资源规范、数据移动、任务执行和结果检索，中心化实例无需在各登录节点分别部署即可操作多台超级计算机。实验表明在四台生产级超级计算机上，相比固定站点基线，中位额外完成时间从42秒降至4秒，降低10.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨异构集群聚合和编排HPC工作流计算力面临四个实际挑战：跨独立管理集群保留工作流上下文、站点间移动大数据集、推理站点特定环境和调度器策略、以及利用实时队列和资源状态进行高效任务调度。

Method: 设计Diamond Agent智能体系统，以类型化技能为接口。提供面向智能体的工作空间和技能，统一跨站点资源发现、资源规范、数据移动、任务执行和结果检索。中心化实例可操作多个超级计算机，将高级智能体动作翻译为有效的站点特定执行，通过Globus Transfer移动数据，利用实时系统能力和队列信息选择可行放置位置。采用事件驱动续接机制将智能体动作与长时批处理作业解耦，持久服务监控远程执行并在结果或决策相关事件可用时恢复智能体。

Result: 使用27小时遥测数据和19轮匹配的多站点提交轮次（含83个作业，跨越四台生产超级计算机）进行实验。相比固定站点基线，Diamond Agent将相对于最快观测放置位置的中位额外完成时间从42秒降至4秒，实现10.5倍减少。

Conclusion: Diamond Agent通过智能体化的架构设计和事件驱动的续接机制，有效解决了跨异构集群HPC工作流编排中的上下文保留、大数据移动、站点环境推理和实时调度等核心挑战，显著提升了任务完成效率。

Abstract: Efficiently aggregating and orchestrating computing power across heterogeneous clusters for HPC workflows faces four practical challenges: preserving workflow context across independently administered clusters, moving large datasets between sites, reasoning about site-specific environments and scheduler policies, and exploiting live queue and resource states for efficient task scheduling. To this end, we design Diamond Agent, an agentic system that enables intelligent execution of HPC workflows across heterogeneous clusters with typed skills as the interface. Diamond Agent provides an agent-facing workspace and skills that unify cross-site resource discovery, resource specification, data movement, task execution, and result retrieval. A centralized Diamond Agent instance can operate multiple supercomputers without being deployed separately on each login node. Diamond Agent translates high-level agent actions into valid site-specific executions, moves data through Globus Transfer, and uses live system capability and queue information to select feasible placements. Its event-driven continuation mechanism decouples agent actions from long-running batch jobs: persistent services monitor remote execution and resume the agent only when a result or decision-relevant event is available. We experiment with 27 hours of telemetry and 19 matched multi-site submission rounds comprising 83 jobs across four production supercomputers. Compared with a fixed-site baseline, Diamond Agent reduces the median additional completion time relative to the fastest observed placement from 42 seconds to 4 seconds, a 10.5x reduction.

</details>


### [32] [Water-network decisions share one hydraulic gradient, and it can now be computed exactly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06323)
*Tianwei Mu,Yue Wang,Mingzhe Yuan,Wenhong Wang,Qing Luo,Min Xiao,Jun Li,Hui Yang,Manhong Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出让供水管网(WDN)的全局梯度算法本身实现精确可微：前向传播完全复现参考引擎(EPANET 2.2)的离散设备、状态切换与低流量线性化，反向传播通过复用前向终结因子分解求解隐式伴随，仅需一次额外的稀疏求解即可同时获得所有参数的梯度，并可在GPU上跨场景批处理。在52个公开/合成/运营管网及8,140个模拟帧上全部达标，最大水头偏差1.137e-13 ft，25个完全一致；一次伴随求解替代906次有限差分模拟；泄漏反演训练每优化步463-470 ms(256场景)，快191倍；梯度校准中位595次模型调用收敛，而最强元启发式需8,060次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管网校准、泄漏定位与传感器布置本质上是连续参数决策问题，但液压引擎只返回解而不提供导数，实践中只能退回到无导数搜索或代理模型，后者误差会被答案继承。缺乏精确梯度严重限制了优化效率与可扩展性。

Method: 将全局梯度算法本身改造为精确可微：前向传播忠实复现参考引擎的离散设备、状态切换与低流量线性化；反向传播通过重用前向传播的终结因子分解来求解隐式伴随方程，一次稀疏求解同时获得全部参数的梯度，并支持GPU上多场景批处理。

Result: 在52个管网与8,140个模拟帧上全部满足验收标准，最大水头偏差1.137e-13 ft，25个与EPANET 2.2完全一致。一次伴随求解取代905管L-TOWN基准上906次有限差分模拟(粗糙度雅可比)；256场景的泄漏反演每优化步463-470 ms，比先前流程快191倍；梯度校准中位595次模型调用收敛，最强元启发式需8,060次且有两个在20,000次内无法达到。在554链接运营网络上，一次伴随即可逐管审计传感器可约束性并给出补点建议。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了与行业标准EPANET 2.2高度一致(几乎完全匹配)的精确可微液压引擎，使梯度下降成为管网校准、泄漏定位与传感器布置的实用首选方案，在效率上全面超越无导数启发式方法，并直接在真实运营模型上提供可操作的传感器审计能力。

Abstract: Calibration, leak localisation and sensor placement on water distribution networks (WDNs) are decisions about continuous parameters, yet the hydraulic engine that defines the physics returns a solution and no derivatives, so practice falls back on derivative-free search or on surrogates whose error the answer inherits. We make the global gradient algorithm itself exactly differentiable: the forward pass reproduces the reference engine's discrete devices, status switching and low-flow linearisation included, and the backward pass solves the implicit adjoint by reusing the forward pass's terminal factorisation, so one extra sparse solve returns every parameter's gradient at once, batched over scenarios on one graphics processor. Across 52 public, synthetic and operational networks and 8,140 simulation frames, every network meets the acceptance criterion, the largest head deviation from EPANET 2.2 is 1.137e-13 ft and 25 agree exactly. One adjoint solve replaces the 906 simulations a finite-difference roughness Jacobian costs on the 905-pipe L-TOWN benchmark, and a leak-inversion training loop runs at 463-470 ms per optimiser step for 256 scenarios, 191 times the prior pipeline. Gradient calibration reaches its endpoint within a median 595 model calls, where the strongest of five tuned metaheuristics needs 8,060 to match it on the training loss and two never do within 20,000. On a 554-link operating network, one adjoint pass audits, pipe by pipe, which roughness parameters the installed sensors can constrain and which sensors to add, on the model the utility already operates.

</details>


### [33] [Cops and Lethal Robber on Rings: A Distributed Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06388)
*Amanpreet Singh Saini,Ashish Saxena,Kaushik Mondal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在分布式设置中首次引入了"致命强盗"（lethal robber）概念，强盗不仅移动速度无界且能杀死移动到其所在顶点的警察。在静态环上证明n个警察不够（即使同起点出发），并给出从任意初始配置出发需要n+⌊log n⌋+4个警察的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Cops and Robber博弈及围捕变体主要在顺序设置中研究，且强盗本身不具杀伤力。现有研究虽涉及Intruder Capture（动态入侵者）和Black Hole Search（致命黑洞），但尚未有同时具备"动态"与"致命"双重属性的实体研究。作者希望填补这一空白，在分布式环境下引入更贴近现实威胁的模型。

Method: 在分布式环形网络中定义"cops and lethal robber"问题：强盗在奇数轮移动且速度无界，警察在偶数轮移动；若有警察移动进入强盗所在顶点则该警察被杀死；围捕（环绕）目标定义不同于传统捕获。作者在大小为n的静态环上研究了所需警察数下限与算法设计。

Result: 证明了n个警察即使全部从同一顶点出发也不足以围捕致命强盗；提出一个从任意初始配置出发的算法，最坏情况下需要n+⌊log n⌋+4个警察即可完成任务。

Conclusion: 该工作将Cops and Robber框架推广到带致命威胁的分布式场景，建立了与Intruder Capture和Black Hole Search问题的联系，并在环结构上给出了定量结果（上下界差距仅为对数级别的常数因子）。

Abstract: The Cops and Robber game is extensively studied in the sequential setting where the main goal is to capture the robber. Capturing the robber means at least one cop, and the robber will be at the same vertex together at some time. There are several variants, including variants where the goal of the cops is to surround the robber. Surrounding the robber means there is at least one cop in each of the neighboring vertices of the robber's position. In this paper, we introduce it in the distributed setting while empowering the robber by saying it can even kill cops. Specifically, in our model, the robber moves in odd rounds and has unbounded speed, cops move in even rounds, and if one or more cops move into a vertex where the robber is currently residing, all these cops get killed. We call this {\it lethal robber}. This also connects our work to the Intruder Capture and Black Hole Search problems by introducing an entity which is dynamic as well as lethal, a setting that, to the best of our knowledge, has not been studied.
  In this work, we introduce the lethal robber, define the {\it cops and lethal robber} problem in the distributed setting and study it on a static ring of size $n$. We prove $n$ cops are not enough, even if all start from the same vertex, and provide an algorithm starting from an arbitrary initial configuration that requires $n+\lfloor\log n \rfloor+4$ cops in the worst case.

</details>


### [34] [Sharing a Fabric with Collective Communication: Two Storage Penalties in Deep Learning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06506)
*Chen Wang,Wenzhao Wu,Hyojin Kim,Jae-Sung Yeom*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了HPC系统上分布式深度学习训练中，NCCL/RCCL集体通信与并行文件系统I/O共享网络结构所带来的两类代价，并提出通过节点本地NVMe暂存（DYAD）来消除这些影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC系统上的分布式DL训练通常让集体通信和并行文件系统I/O共享同一网络结构，这种共享会带来严重的性能问题，作者希望量化并解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 作者在Slingshot-11系统上使用真实的GNN训练工作负载进行实证分析，识别两类不同代价（DataLoader重尾停顿和流量类别争用），并提出DYAD方案——通过节点本地NVMe暂存，使存储I/O不经过共享网络。

Result: DYAD在完整训练epoch中对比直接Lustre读取实现7.4倍加速，对比VAST实现1.06倍加速；当本地缓存完全预热后（第二个epoch），DataLoader停顿被完全消除，DYAD相对VAST可达1.31倍加速。

Conclusion: 研究证明存储I/O通过共享网络传输是两类性能代价的共同根源，节点本地NVMe暂存方案能有效将存储I/O移出该路径，从而消除停顿与争用，显著提升训练性能。

Abstract: Distributed DL training on HPC systems often shares one network fabric between NCCL/RCCL collective communication and parallel-filesystem I/O. Using a real GNN training workload on a Slingshot-11 system, we show that this sharing imposes two distinct costs. The primary cost is heavy-tailed DataLoader stalls: the typical DataLoader wait is just 15 ms at steady state, yet spikes to multiple seconds in 28% of Lustre iterations and 12% of VAST iterations. The secondary cost is traffic-class contention on collective communication: Lustre I/O stalls the all-reduce by up to 145$\times$ in an isolated benchmark. The two costs arise from different mechanisms. I/O stall latency affects any storage path that traverses the shared fabric, whereas all-reduce network contention occurs only when storage and collective communication share the same traffic class. Their common root cause is that storage I/O traverses the shared fabric. This work shows that node-local NVMe staging via DYAD (Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/flux-framework/dyad) eliminates both effects by keeping storage I/O off that path. Across a full training epoch, DYAD achieves a 7.4 times speedup over direct Lustre reads and a 1.06 times speedup over VAST. By the second epoch, once the local cache is fully warmed, DataLoader stalls are eliminated entirely, allowing DYAD to reach a 1.31 times speedup over VAST.

</details>


### [35] [EStream: Fast and Memory-Efficient MoE Prefill through Expert Virtualization on Mobile NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06551)
*Junming Zhang,Zhenzhe Zheng,Fan Wu,Xiaoyao Huang,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EStream 是一个面向移动 NPU 的 MoE 预填充（prefill）推理系统。它通过"单一编译专家图 + 调用时绑定路由 token 和权重地址"的方式，将动态 MoE 执行完全放在 NPU 上运行，避免 padding 或 CPU/GPU 回退；同时采用专家虚拟化技术，将专家池存放在 UFS 闪存中并按组分页装入固定大小的 NPU 可寻址竞技场，加载隐藏在计算之后，使内存受限而非模型大小。在商用 Snapdragon 手机上对 7B–16B MoE 模型进行评估，相比最快基线实现 2.25–27.57× 的 TTFT 加速和 1.19–12.29× 的峰值物理内存降低，可扩展至 46.7B 参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端正越来越多地部署 LLM 用于多样化的 prefill-only 服务，但现有系统主要依赖稠密模型（其规则计算能高效映射到移动 NPU），导致能力更强的 MoE 模型未被充分利用。MoE 预填充与移动 NPU 不兼容存在两个核心矛盾：一是 NPU 图在编译时固定，而 MoE 在运行时才选择专家；二是一个请求会触及大部分专家，超过手机内存容量。作者旨在解决这两个矛盾，让 MoE 也能在移动 NPU 上高效运行。

Method: EStream 的方法包含三个关键部分：(1) 单一编译专家图——将所有专家共享为一个编译好的图结构，每个专家的路由 token 和权重地址在调用时绑定，从而让动态 MoE 执行完全运行在 NPU 上，无需 padding 或 CPU/GPU 回退；(2) 专家虚拟化——将专家池存放在 UFS 闪存中，按组分页装入固定大小的 NPU 可寻址竞技场（arena），加载过程隐藏在计算之后，使内存占用受限于竞技场大小而非模型大小；(3) 硬件感知配置算法——自动配置 UFS–NPU 流水线，最大化加载与计算的叠加（overlap）。

Result: 在商用 Snapdragon 手机上，涵盖三种 7B–16B MoE 模型和 256–4,096 token 提示、共 18 组对比设置中，EStream 相比各设置下最快的基线实现了 2.25–27.57× 的纯 prefill TTFT 加速，并将峰值物理内存降低 1.19–12.29×。此外，EStream 可扩展到参数规模高达 46.7B 的 MoE 模型。

Conclusion: EStream 证明了 MoE 模型可以在移动 NPU 上高效执行 prefill 任务，通过将"NPU 必须固定的"与"MoE 在运行时决定的"分离，结合专家虚拟化与硬件感知的流水线配置，既解决了 NPU 静态图与 MoE 动态专家选择的冲突，也克服了专家池超出手机内存的限制，实现了显著的性能提升和内存节省，将 MoE 的应用范围扩展到了移动端。

Abstract: Mobile vendors and application developers increasingly deploy LLMs on smartphones for diverse prefill-only services. Yet current systems rely mainly on dense models whose regular computation maps efficiently to mobile NPUs, leaving more capable MoEs underused. MoE prefill does not fit mobile NPUs: NPU graphs are fixed at compile time, yet MoE picks experts at runtime; and one request touches most experts, more than a phone can hold in memory. We present EStream, which resolves both by separating what the NPU must fix from what MoE decides at runtime. A single compiled expert graph serves every expert, with each expert's routed tokens and weight address bound at call time, so dynamic MoE execution runs entirely on the NPU without padding or CPU/GPU fallback. Expert virtualization keeps the expert pool in UFS flash storage and pages it through a fixed-size NPU-addressable arena, group by group, with loading hidden behind computation, so memory is bounded by the arena rather than by the model. It further introduces a hardware-aware configuration algorithm that automatically configures the UFS--NPU pipeline and maximizes loading--computation overlap. Across 18 comparative settings covering three 7B--16B MoEs and 256--4,096-token prompts, we evaluate EStream on a commercial Snapdragon smartphone. Compared to the fastest baseline at each setting, EStream achieves a 2.25--27.57X pure-prefill TTFT speedup and reduces peak physical memory by 1.19--12.29X. EStream further scales to MoE models with up to 46.7B parameters.

</details>


### [36] [Gutenberg: Taming Latency-Critical Cloud Services with Near-Data-Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06691)
*Qiushi Lin,Phillip B. Gibbons,Jovan Stojkovic,Yiwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Gutenberg，一种针对可变、延迟敏感的云服务设计的CPU+近数据处理(NDP)异构系统。它通过CPU端delta缓冲区暂存子页更新，避免热点可写页复制时的全页同步开销；利用CPU辅助核在NDP执行或副本维护代价高时协助请求处理；并通过在线控制器联合决策页放置、复制、执行位置和路由，同时保障服务间的隔离与公平资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟敏感的云服务面临内存压力，同时需要隔离性、公平性和可预测的QoS。近数据处理(NDP)虽能减少数据移动，但写入使副本维护代价高昂，且不均的计算与内存流量会导致部分NDP单元过载，增加尾部延迟。现有面向吞吐量的调度器无法充分解决共置云服务场景下的这些挑战。

Method: 1) 在CPU端引入delta缓冲区暂存子页更新，支持热点可写页复制而无需急切的全页同步；2) 部署CPU辅助核，在NDP执行或副本维护代价过高时协助请求执行；3) 在线控制器根据访问模式、队列压力和先前决策的反馈，联合决策页放置、复制、CPU/NDP执行及路由；4) 通过模型检验验证CPU-NDP协调协议的正确性。

Result: 在TailBench基准上使用ZSim（配以Ramulator校准的内存时序）评估，相比现有系统，Gutenberg将平均延迟和p99延迟分别最多降低80.4%和85.8%，同时改善了隔离性和公平性，并能自适应变化的工作负载行为。

Conclusion: Gutenberg证明了一个联合的、具有反馈感知的在线控制器加上CPU-NDP异构协作架构，能够有效解决可变、延迟敏感云服务在写副本维护、热点失衡和QoS保障方面的核心挑战，在显著降低延迟的同时维持隔离与公平。

Abstract: Latency-critical cloud services place growing pressure on memory while requiring isolation, fairness, and predictable QoS. Near-data processing (NDP) reduces data movement by executing requests close to memory, and prior systems further improve locality through caching and replication. However, writes make replica maintenance expensive, while uneven compute and memory traffic can overload a few NDP units and increase tail latency. Existing throughput-oriented schedulers do not fully address these challenges for co-located cloud services.
  We present Gutenberg, a CPU+NDP for mutable, latency-critical cloud services. Gutenberg stages subpage updates in a CPU-resident delta buffer, allowing hot writable pages to remain replicated without eager full-page synchronization. It also adopts CPU helper cores to assist request execution when NDP execution or replica maintenance becomes costly. An online controller jointly decides page placement, replication, CPU/NDP execution, and routing using access patterns, queue pressure, and feedback from prior decisions. The system further enforces isolation and fair resource allocation across services. We also model-check CPU--NDP coordination protocol for correctness.
  We evaluate on TailBench using ZSim with Ramulator-calibrated memory timing. Across evaluated services, Gutenberg outperforms prior systems, reducing average and p99 latency by up to 80.4% and 85.8%. It also improves isolation and fairness while adapting to changing workload behaviors.

</details>


### [37] [ForgeStencil: Automating Per-Case Stencil Specialization from Kernels to 100+ Real Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06694)
*Yaojian Chen,Yuxuan Li,Wubing Wan,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ForgeStencil提出了一种与主流"通用性"相反的思路：利用代码合成代理（Code-synthesis agents）为每个具体场景定制化地合成CUDA内核，而非沿用单一的人工编写方法。该方法通过Kernel Agent按配置生成专用算子矩阵，并通过App Agent将定制化原则扩展到整个应用层（探测热点、重写结构、验证集成），在100多个真实工业与科学代码上验证。f32几何平均达2.35x（对比逐案例SOTA），端到端应用中位数1.41x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU上最快的stencil内核高度依赖形状、精度和宿主应用，单一方法难以在所有场景最优。已有Stencil DSL、代码生成器和自动调优器追求通用性，但缺乏按案例专门化（per-case specialization）的可行路径，因为成本过高难以扩展。作者观察到代码合成代理已大幅降低该成本，使得可以为每个案例构建全新方案并端到端部署成为可能。

Method: 采用两级Agent架构。（1）Kernel Agent：为每个配置合成CUDA内核，锻造专化算子矩阵，目标匹配或超越各案例公开最强SOTA基线；（2）App Agent：扩展到完整应用——定位热点、重写应用结构、验证并集成每次改动。同时设计了测量完整性校验框架，将夸大加速比转化为系统性错误以杜绝虚假报告；对所有失败案例（正确性/测量/加速比不达标）如实记录为拒绝或降级。

Result: f32同精度对比各案例SOTA基线几何均值2.35x（fp16为1.95x，单独披露；A100平台）。端到端应用在100个代码上的加速中位数为1.41x（对比同架构GPU基线，含程序提供验证与计时）。大部分加速来自结构和宿主端重写而非纯stencil替换；加速幅度与基线已调优程度呈负相关，支持"专化收益"而非通用复用。

Conclusion: 代码合成代理使"按案例专化"成为可行的新范式，优于追求通用性的传统方法。定制化内核配合应用层结构重写能带来显著且可验证的加速；同时论文强调严谨的测量诚信机制（所有失败者记录为拒绝或降级）对可信的加速比报告至关重要。

Abstract: On modern GPUs the fastest stencil kernel depends on the stencil's shape, precision, and host application, and a kernel tuned for one case is rarely fastest for another. Stencil DSLs, code generators, and autotuners instead pursued generality: a single human-authored method reused across cases and validated mainly on microbenchmarks, because per-case specialization was too costly to scale. ForgeStencil starts from the opposite assumption. Code-synthesis agents have reduced that cost enough to build a fresh solution for each case and deploy it end-to-end in real software. A Kernel Agent synthesizes CUDA and forges a per-configuration matrix of specialized operators that matches or exceeds the strongest publicly available state-of-the-art (SOTA) baseline for each case. An App Agent extends the principle to whole applications: it locates hotspots, rewrites application structure, and validates and integrates each change across 100+ real industrial and scientific codes. Most of the measured speedup comes from structural and host-side rewrites, with pure stencil replacement in the minority; the gain also correlates negatively with how well the baseline was already tuned, consistent with gains coming from specialization rather than generic reuse. Every result is checked by a measurement-integrity harness that turns an overstated speedup into a system-level error. The forged kernels reach a same-precision f32 geometric mean of 2.35x against the per-case SOTA baselines (fp16 gains, 1.95x, disclosed separately; A100), and the end-to-end application median is 1.41x across 100 codes, all against same-architecture GPU baselines with program-provided validation and timing. Of 116 candidates, every one that failed the correctness, measurement, or speedup criteria was recorded as rejected or downgraded instead of written up as a speedup.

</details>

*Kass Bileski,Avery Miller*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究动态网络中黑洞定位问题。在1-bounded 1-interval connected动态图中，作者将所需agent数量从之前的2δ_BH+17降低到2δ_BH+3，几乎匹配已知的2δ_BH+1下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态图（1-bounded 1-interval connected）中协同定位黑洞是分布式计算的重要问题。Kaur等人之前证明2δ_BH+17个agent足以从分散配置完成任务，但这一数量与其下界2δ_BH+1之间存在较大差距，本文旨在缩小这一间隙。

Method: 作者提出了一种改进的算法/策略，使得在带端口标记的1-bounded 1-interval connected动态图中，仅需2δ_BH+3个同步移动agent即可安全定位黑洞，显著优于先前的2δ_BH+17。

Result: 证明了2δ_BH+3个agent足以解决该动态网络中的黑洞安全问题，几乎匹配Kaur等人（ICDCN 2025）给出的2δ_BH+1下界，间隙仅为2。

Conclusion: 在1-bounded 1-interval connected动态图中，黑洞定位所需的agent数量上界被收紧至2δ_BH+3，与下界2δ_BH+1的差距极小，基本达到最优。

Abstract: We consider a team of synchronous mobile agents operating in a port-labeled network. There is one node in the network, called a black hole, that permanently destroys any agent that visits the node. The team of agents must safely locate the black hole, i.e., at least one agent must survive, terminate its algorithm at a node adjacent to the black hole, and output the port number that leads to the black hole from its current position. In the setting where the network is a 1-bounded 1-interval connected dynamic graph, Kaur et al. (SSS 2025) showed that a team consisting of $2δ_{BH}+17$ agents is sufficient to solve the task from a scattered configuration, where $δ_{BH}$ denotes the degree of the black hole node. We show that $2δ_{BH}+3$ agents are sufficient, nearly matching the $2δ_{BH}+1$ lower bound provided in Kaur et al. (ICDCN 2025).

</details>


### [39] [Unified AI Gateway: A Framework for Joint Model Routing and KV Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06940)
*Jiaxun Lu,Xiang Zhang,Yunfeng Shao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了"统一AI网关"（Unified AI Gateway）这一系统架构概念，作为边缘部署的AI流量枢纽，协同管理模型路由、KV缓存管理和计算放置，以解决多模型LLM推理中面临的集成成本与KV缓存重建成本问题。通过工作负载级分析模拟，在八种典型工作负载下实现了1.25×–13.28×的首token延迟加速和1.20×–6.16×的输入成本收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理正从单一模型扩展到多种不同大小、能力、价格和提供商的模型，这给开发者带来了两类成本：(1) 在多模型间选择和切换的集成成本；(2) 当KV缓存不可用或与所选模型不兼容时，需要重建KV缓存的推理成本。

Method: 提出Unified AI Gateway系统架构，作为边缘部署的AI流量枢纽，协调端设备、边缘资源和云端模型服务之间的模型路由、KV缓存管理和计算放置。请求时，网关在任务质量、延迟、成本和资源约束下联合选择目标模型、执行站点和KV缓存动作；同时在后台执行缓存管理动作以优化KV缓存的放置、复制、检索和生命周期决策。论文综合了KV缓存复用、压缩、跨模型映射、分布式存储和传输的现有证据。

Result: 通过八种典型工作负载画像的工作负载级分析模拟，报告TTFT加速1.25×–13.28×，输入成本收益1.20×–6.16×。

Conclusion: 统一AI网关将模型路由、缓存管理和计算放置整合为一个系统，能显著降低多模型LLM推理的成本。文章同时指出了将这些能力集成到一个系统中的剩余挑战，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference increasingly spans models that differ in size, capability, price, and provider. This shift creates two costs for developers. One is the integration cost of choosing among and switching between many models. The other is the inference cost of rebuilding a KV cache when it is unavailable or incompatible with the selected model. We define and analyze the Unified AI Gateway as a system setting for an edge-deployed AI traffic hub. It coordinates model routing, KV cache management, and compute placement across end devices, edge resources, and cloud model services. At request time, the gateway jointly selects a target model, an execution site, and a KV cache action under task-quality, latency, cost, and resource constraints. In parallel, background cache-management actions optimize KV cache placement, replication, retrieval, and lifecycle decisions for subsequent requests. We synthesize existing evidence on KV cache reuse, compression, cross-model mapping, distributed storage, and transfer, and discuss the remaining challenges of integrating these capabilities into one system. Across eight typical workload profiles, our workload-level analytical simulation reports TTFT speedups of 1.25$\times$--13.28$\times$ and input-cost benefits of 1.20$\times$--6.16$\times$.

</details>


### [40] [TreeRedux: Separating Concerns in Spark's Distributed Tree Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06982)
*David A. G. Harrison,Ivan Cao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TreeRedux，一个对Apache Spark树聚合原语的最小扩展，通过在executor上执行终端finalize操作将聚合状态U映射为紧凑结果V，避免将可能很大的中间状态物化在driver上，解决了大规模聚合中driver的计算和内存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spark默认将树聚合根节点放在driver上，要求driver参与中间聚合状态的计算，对于大规模聚合（如精确分位数计算和重头项识别），中间状态可能远大于最终结果，导致driver内存不足（OOM）。即使新版本将根移至executor，完成的聚合仍须返回并物化在driver上。

Method: 提出TreeRedux，增加一个在executor上执行的终端finalize操作，将聚合状态U映射为紧凑结果V，使得V而非可能很大的U在driver上物化。将其应用于GK Select（精确分位数）和Space-Saving（重头项识别）等算法。

Result: TreeRedux消除了GK Select中driver的epsilon-n内存项，使driver内存需求与Spark的GK Sketch同渐近阶。默认GK Select在25亿元素时driver OOM；executor端聚合选项将极限扩展至约160-180亿但仍需driver物化最终状态；Redux Select在280亿元素无OOM完成。TreeRedux使Space-Saving sketch容量可达最大配置的32倍。

Conclusion: TreeRedux通过最小化扩展解决了Spark树聚合中driver作为单点的内存与计算瓶颈，显著提升了大规模精确分位数和重头项识别等聚合任务的扩展能力。

Abstract: By default, Apache Spark's tree aggregation primitives place the tree root on the driver, requiring the driver to participate in the same aggregation computation over intermediate aggregation state as executor nodes. For large aggregates, this can expose the single coordinator to substantial computation and memory requirements.
  Recent Spark versions optionally move the root to an executor, but the completed aggregate must still be returned to and materialized on the driver. We demonstrate this limitation using exact quantile computation and heavy-hitter identification, where the intermediate aggregation state can be substantially larger than the desired final result.
  We propose TreeRedux, a minimal extension that adds a terminal finalize operation, executed on an executor, that maps the aggregation state U to a compact result V, so that V rather than the potentially large U is materialized on the driver.
  For exact quantile computation, applying TreeRedux to GK Select removes the driver's epsilon-n memory term, reducing driver memory requirements to the same asymptotic order as Spark's GK Sketch. In our experiments, the default GK Select implementation encountered a driver out-of-memory error at 2.5 billion elements. Spark's executor-side final aggregation option extended this limit to approximately 16-18 billion elements but still required the final aggregation state to be materialized on the driver. Redux Select completed through 28 billion elements without a driver out-of-memory error. TreeRedux allowed Space-Saving sketches with up to 32x the capacity of the largest configuration that materializes a full sketch on the driver.

</details>


### [41] [An Efficient Out-of-Core Tomographic Imaging Framework for Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07249)
*Xuetao Chen,Cong Ma,Xiangyu Meng,Du Wu,Zhengyang Bai,Tao Luo,Zhaorui Zhang,Emmanuel Jeannot,Edgar Josafat Martinez Noriega,Xun Wang,Peng Chen,Amelie Chi Zhou,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出edgeFBP，一个面向Nvidia Jetson SoC边缘设备的高效CT重建框架，通过端到端流水线设计实现超出内存容量的图像重建，并利用半精度Tensor Cores加速反投影（BP）核心内核。在Jetson Nano上比RTK库快1.83倍，在Jetson AGX上快2.56倍；在25瓦严格功耗预算下，Jetson Nano的能效比Nvidia DGX A100高5-48倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT成像是重要的3D成像技术，但在边缘设备上执行CT成像面临计算能力、内存容量和能量预算的限制，需要一种能在约束条件下高效进行CT重建的方案。

Method: edgeFBP采用端到端流水线设计，实现高效的外核（out-of-core）图像重建以应对紧致的功耗和内存约束；同时利用混合精度策略，借助半精度Tensor Cores（TCs）来加速计算瓶颈——反投影（BP）内核。

Result: edgeFBP在Jetson Nano上相比广泛使用的RTK库获得1.83倍加速，在Jetson AGX上获得2.56倍加速；在严格的25瓦功耗预算下，Jetson Nano上的edgeFBP能效比Nvidia DGX A100高出5-48倍。

Conclusion: edgeFBP展示了在资源受限的边缘设备上实现数据中心级规模成像的可能性，大幅提升了边缘CT重建的性能和能效。

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) is an essential 3D imaging technology widely used in medical diagnostics and scientific research. However, performing CT imaging on edge devices is challenging due to limitations in computational power, memory capacity, and energy budget. This paper presents an efficient CT reconstruction framework, called edgeFBP, designed for Nvidia Jetson System-on-Chip (SoC) devices. edgeFBP adopts an end-to-end pipeline design for efficient out-of-core image reconstruction under tight power and memory constraints. edgeFBP utilizes a mixed-precision strategy leveraging half-precision Tensor Cores (TCs) to accelerate the bottleneck back-projection (BP) kernel. edgeFBP achieves a 1.83x speedup over the widely used RTK library on Jetson Nano and a 2.56x speedup on Jetson AGX. Under a strict 25-Watt power budget, edgeFBP on Jetson Nano achieves up to 5-48x higher energy efficiency than an Nvidia DGX A100, enabling datacenter-scale imaging on constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [42] [The Maximum Mutual Visibility Set on a Cactus Graph and the Self-stabilizing Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07253)
*Yonghwan Kim,Yuichi Sudo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了仙人掌图（cactus graphs）中的最大互视集合（Mutual Visibility Set, MVS）问题。首先确定了仙人掌图中最大MVS的大小，然后提出了两种自稳定算法来构造这样的集合：第一种基于单棵BFS树，在O(D)轮内收敛且每个进程平均使用O(log n)比特；第二种使用并行BFS树，在O(|C_max|+|T_max|)轮内收敛，并证明了该复杂度关于这两个参数是渐近紧的，即使在|C_max|+|T_max| = o(D)的图上也是如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MVS概念在图中具有重要的结构性质，但在一般图中确定最大MVS是NP完全的。前人工作要么聚焦于寻找极大MVS，要么局限于特定图类。仙人掌图作为树宽较小的基础图类，其最大MVS问题仍悬而未决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文首先从理论上确定了仙人掌图中最大MVS的大小；然后设计了两种自稳定算法：第一种利用单棵BFS树，在O(D)轮内稳定，平均每进程O(log n)比特；第二种利用并行BFS树，在O(|C_max|+|T_max|)轮内稳定，其中C_max和T_max分别对应图中最大环和最大树的某种参数。

Result: 给出了仙人掌图中最大MVS的大小确界；两种自稳定算法分别以O(D)和O(|C_max|+|T_max|)的轮复杂度收敛；并证明了第二种算法的收敛时间关于|C_max|+|T_max|是渐近最优的，即使当这两个参数之和远小于图的直径D（即o(D)）时也成立。

Conclusion: 本文解决了仙人掌图上最大MVS问题的公开难题，提供了理论上下界与两种实用的自稳定构造算法，并给出的复杂度下界表明第二种算法在相关参数意义下已是最优的。

Abstract: Given a graph $G=(V,E)$, let $S$ ($\subseteq V$) be a set of vertices. Two vertices are \emph{mutually visible} if there exists a shortest path in $G$ between them that does not contain any other vertex of $S$. A set $S$ is a \emph{Mutual Visibility Set} (\MVS) if every pair of vertices in $S$ is mutually visible.
  The concept of \MVS s in graphs has attracted significant attention since its introduction, as it provides an important structural property of graphs. However, determining a maximum \MVS\ in general graphs is computationally intractable; the decision problem of whether a graph admits an \MVS\ of size at least $k$ has been shown to be \emph{NP-complete}. Thus, prior work has focused on finding maximal \MVS s or restricting attention to specific graph classes.
  Cactus graphs form a fundamental low-treewidth class, yet the maximum \MVS\ problem for this class remains open. In this paper, we first determine the size of maximum \MVS~in cactus graphs, and introduce two self-stabilizing algorithms that construct such sets. The first algorithm uses a single BFS tree and stabilizes in $O(D)$ rounds with $O(\log n)$ bits per process on average; the second one uses parallel BFS trees and stabilizes in $O(|C_{\max}|+|T_{\max}|)$ rounds, which we show to be asymptotically tight as a function of these two parameters, even on graphs where $|C_{\max}|+|T_{\max}| = o(D)$.

</details>


### [43] [PLATOS: A Power and Latency-Aware Task-Oriented Scheduling Strategy for Healthcare IoT in Fog Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07318)
*Mohammed Alaa Ala'anzy,Zulfiqar Ahmad,Zhanar Mukash*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PLATOS（功耗与延迟感知的任务导向调度）策略，通过四阶段方法优化医疗物联网（HIoT）在雾计算环境中的功耗和延迟，实验表明相比现有方法可降低18.72%的能耗和8.65%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIoT技术虽推动了个性化医疗保健的实时数据收集与分析，但其快速扩展在雾计算环境中（尤其管理电池供电设备时）带来了延迟增加和能耗升高的挑战，亟需一种同时优化功耗和延迟的调度策略。

Method: PLATOS策略分四个阶段实现：(1) 将HIoT任务分类为优先级导向、存储导向和计算导向三类；(2) 延迟优化阶段，为每类任务识别执行延迟最低的雾计算资源；(3) 功耗优化阶段，选择能耗最小的资源；(4) 决策阶段，为高优先级任务分配高性能雾资源，其余任务基于延迟和功耗优化阶段生成的映射列表进行调度。

Result: 在iFogSim2仿真实验中，PLATOS相比最先进方法将能耗降低了18.72%，延迟降低了8.65%。

Conclusion: PLATOS通过任务导向的调度策略有效提升了HIoT系统的效率和响应能力，有助于实现更有效的患者护理和主动式医疗服务交付。

Abstract: Healthcare Internet of Things (HIoT) technology is revolutionising the healthcare industry by enabling real-time data collection and analysis for personalised patient care. However, the rapid expansion of HIoT technology introduces challenges such as increased latency and higher energy consumption in fog computing environments, particularly when managing battery-operated devices. To address these issues, this work proposes a novel scheduling strategy that optimises both power consumption and latency through task-oriented scheduling for HIoT tasks. The proposed strategy, named PLATOS (Power and Latency Aware Task Oriented Scheduling), is implemented in four sequential phases. In the first phase, HIoT tasks are categorised into three groups: priority-oriented, storage-oriented, and computational-oriented. The second phase focuses on latency optimisation by identifying the fog computing resources that yield the lowest execution delay for each task category. In the third phase, power optimisation is achieved by selecting the resources that minimise energy consumption. Finally, in the decision-making phase, high-performance fog resources are allocated to high-priority tasks while the remaining tasks are scheduled based on a mapped list derived from the latency and power optimisation phases. Simulation experiments conducted in iFogSim2 demonstrate that PLATOS reduces energy consumption by 18.72% and latency by 8.65% when compared to the state-of-the-art. These improvements enhance the efficiency and responsiveness of HIoT systems and contribute to more effective patient care and proactive healthcare service delivery.

</details>

*Yenan Wang,Jesper Kullberg,Fabian Paglianno Persson,Elad Michael Schiller,Timothé Albouy*

TL;DR: 本文实现了三种拜占庭可靠广播（BRB/MBRB）算法（Bracha、AFRT、Coded MBRB），在统一的 Go 代码库中进行系统化基准测试，涵盖 Shadow 模拟器、云平台和 FABRIC 测试床等多样化环境，评估了传输数据量、延迟、CPU 成本等性能指标及其操作权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BRB 和 MBRB 的实际运行行为不仅取决于规范和渐近通信界，还受到序列化、密码学、缓冲、编排、部署环境和故障注入语义等多种因素的影响。仅靠算法描述无法揭示这些隐藏的瓶颈和操作权衡，需要实现层面的证据来弥补这一差距。

Method: 在共享 Go 代码库中实现 Bracha、AFRT 和 Coded MBRB，采用统一的编排、插桩、基于解析器的规范检查和故障注入机制。评估使用 Shadow 网络模拟器中的单次广播、原生分析、Google Cloud Platform 部署和分布式 FABRIC 测试床，覆盖最高 30 节点、40 MB 载荷，共 92,190 次运行和 2,361,600 条解析器检查条目。

Result: Coded MBRB 在大载荷的云设置中减少了传输数据并改善了延迟，但将成本转移到密码学和编码计算上。Bracha 和 AFRT 在小载荷下 CPU 成本更低，但随着载荷增长，其全载荷传播增加了处理、分配和网络成本。在所有测试配置中，解析器未发现重复交付、冲突交付或与发送方载荷不同的交付。

Conclusion: 本文提供了实现层面的证据和一个可扩展的开源基准测试工件，用于将 BRB 和 MBRB 作为可执行的分布式系统组件进行基准测试，揭示算法描述本身所隐藏的瓶颈和操作权衡。

Abstract: Byzantine Reliable Broadcast (BRB) and Message-Adversary-Tolerant Byzantine Reliable Broadcast (MBRB) are reliable-dissemination abstractions for fault-tolerant distributed systems. Yet their operational behavior is shaped not only by specifications and asymptotic communication bounds, but also by serialization, cryptography, buffering, orchestration, deployment environment, and fault-injection semantics. This paper implements and evaluates Bracha [Information and Computation, 1987], AFRT by Albouy et al. [TCS, 2023], and Coded MBRB by Albouy et al. [OPODIS, 2024]. We implement the algorithms in a shared Go codebase with common orchestration, instrumentation, parser-based specification checks, fault injection, and an open-source reproducibility artifact. The evaluation uses single-shot broadcasts in the Shadow network simulator, native profiling, a Google Cloud Platform deployment, and a distributed FABRIC testbed, covering controlled experiments up to 30 nodes, payloads up to 40 MB, 92,190 runs, and 2,361,600 parser-checked entries. The results show that Coded MBRB reduces transmitted data and improves latency in the evaluated cloud setting for larger payloads, but shifts cost to cryptographic and coding computation. Bracha and AFRT incur lower CPU costs at smaller payloads, but their full-payload dissemination increases processing, allocation, and network costs as payloads grow. Across the tested configurations, the parser found no duplicate deliveries, conflicting deliveries, or deliveries of values different from the sender's payload. The paper contributes implementation-level evidence and an extensible artifact for benchmarking BRB and MBRB as executable distributed-system components, exposing bottlenecks and operational trade-offs that are hidden by algorithmic descriptions alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式MoE推理中，计算和通信CTAs竞争SM上的有限驻留容量，固定的资源分区无法适应输入大小、路由专家负载和内核配置的变化，可能导致通信积压或降低专家计算并行度。需要一种自适应的资源管理方法来解决CTA共驻冲突和波状执行问题。

Method: 提出波量化分析模型和启动时资源管理器：利用路由瓦片计数、内核占用率、GPU驻留约束和分裂级就绪依赖关系，在每次内核启动前选择通信CTA数量和资源分区，无需候选执行、逐工作负载剖析或内核重编译。

Result: 在15个真实p90工作负载上，分析选择器相对于实测oracle的平均遗憾为3.22%，平均求解器开销0.157微秒。相比COMET，GEMM2+GatherRS算子加速2.528倍，完整后路由器MoE层加速1.771倍，完整模型预填充加速1.185倍，最大分别达4.218倍、2.584倍和1.439倍。在所有可行的TP=2/EP=2序列长度≥4096下均优于COMET、Megatron core-TE和FastMoE TP+NCCL。

Conclusion: 通过波量化分析和启动时资源分配，能够有效解决MoE推理中计算-通信重叠时的CTA驻留竞争问题，实现自适应资源分区并显著提升端到端性能，同时在所有评估的并行策略下超越现有实现。

Abstract: Fine-grained computation--communication overlap in distributed Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference allows communication to begin as partial compute results become ready. However, cooperative thread arrays (CTAs) performing computation and communication contend for finite residency capacity on streaming multiprocessors (SMs). Because a resident CTA generally retains its allocated SM resources until completion, CTAs that cannot be co-resident must wait for resources, resulting in wave-like execution. A fixed resource partition cannot adapt to changes in input size, routed expert load, and kernel configuration, potentially causing a communication backlog or reducing expert compute parallelism.
  We present a wave-quantized analytical model and launch-time resource manager for dependency-coupled overlap pipelines. Using routed-tile counts, kernel occupancy, GPU residency constraints, and split-level readiness dependencies, it selects the communication-CTA count and resource partition before each launch without candidate execution, per-workload profiling, or kernel recompilation. We integrate the method into the public COMET A100 implementation in FLUX.
  We evaluate three MoE models on four NVIDIA A100 GPUs under several parallelism strategies at the GEMM2+GatherRS operator, complete post-router MoE layer, and complete-model prefill levels. Across 15 real-p90 workloads, the analytical selector achieves 3.22 percent mean regret relative to the measured oracle with a mean solver overhead of 0.157 microseconds. Over COMET, our method achieves geometric-mean speedups of 2.528x at the GEMM2+GatherRS operator, 1.771x at the complete post-router MoE layer, and 1.185x for complete-model prefill, with maxima of 4.218x, 2.584x, and 1.439x, respectively. At every feasible TP=2/EP=2 sequence length of at least 4,096, our implementation outperforms COMET, Megatron core-TE, and FastMoE TP+NCCL.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出了一种GPU加速的脉冲神经网络（SNN）映射流水线，将SNN建模为超图以利用神经形态硬件的组播和复制特性，通过多级划分和递归二分初始化及交换精细化完成映射，相比现有工具在延迟、能耗和速度上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在神经形态硬件上的系统性能高度依赖神经元到核（core）的映射质量，而网络规模扩展到百万神经元时，映射（包含超图划分与布局两个NP难问题）的高质量解越来越难以获得。

Method: 将SNN建模为超图以同时利用硬件组播与核内复制特性；提出GPU加速的映射流水线：多级划分方案围绕硬件约束设计，布局通过递归二分初始化，再用反复交换将强连通核聚拢来进行精化。

Result: 基于模型的实验显示比现有顺序工具降低超过16%的延迟和42%的脉冲传输能耗，并行映射器平均比现有工具快18-280倍。

Conclusion: GPU加速的SNN映射方法在保持映射质量的同时大幅提升速度，能够支撑大规模SNN在神经形态硬件上的高效部署。

Abstract: SNNs running on neuromorphic hardware use spikes to achieve sparse and energy-efficient communication over a mesh of cores. In turn, system performance heavily depends on the assignment of neurons to cores: the mapping. Since hardware features inter-core multicast and intra-core replication of spikes, we model SNNs as hypergraphs to exploit both opportunities for reducing communication traffic. Mapping thus comprises two NP-hard problems: hypergraph partitioning and placement on the lattice of cores. High-quality solutions to both are critical, yet increasingly difficult as networks scale to millions of neurons. Therefore, we propose a GPU-accelerated pipeline for SNN mapping: a multi-level partitioning scheme is devised around hardware constraints, while placement is initialized through recursive bisection, followed by refinement pulling together strongly connected cores through repeated swaps. Model-based experiments show upwards of 16% lower latency and 42% lower energy for spike movements over existing sequential tools, while our parallel mapper is on average 18-280x faster.

</details>

*Tijana Milentijević,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维近似一致协议，在拜占庭容错下提出最小包围球（MEB）有效性约束及其松弛版本c-MEB。核心贡献包括：自适应的MEB收缩算法（同步模型，n>(d+1)t），以及维度无关的膨胀定理（inflating radius by √(β/(β-1))保证公共交点），基于此实现最优韧性n>3t、常数MEB有效性和坐标自由的收缩算法。异步设置下也有相应扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多维近似一致问题在凸有效性下，韧性问题阈值随维度增长。作者希望改为研究最小包围球（MEB）有效性，从而摆脱维度对韧性阈值的影响，实现坐标自由的收缩协议并达到最优容错韧性。

Method: 1) 提出自适应MEB收缩算法（Adaptive MEB Contraction），同步模型n>(d+1)t时每轮半径收缩1/√2，满足√2-MEB有效性。2) 核心理论贡献：维度无关的膨胀定理——若有限族欧氏球中每β个球有公共点，则半径膨胀√(β/(β-1))后全体有公共交点。3) 将该定理应用于MEB安全区域的候选球，得到同步最优韧性n>3t算法，收缩因子√3/2，√6-MEB有效性。4) 扩展到异步设置，分别给出有无膨胀的两种方案。

Result: 同步模型：n>3t下实现收缩因子√3/2与√6-MEB有效性，是首个具有最优韧性、常数c-MEB有效性和坐标自由收缩的多维近似一致算法。异步模型：无膨胀时n>(d+2)t、收缩因子√(2/3)、√6-MEB有效性；有膨胀时n>4t、收缩因子√15/4、2√10-MEB有效性。与MDA算法相比，在韧性上严格更优且MEB有效性更强。

Conclusion: 通过膨胀定理突破维度对韧性的限制，实现了最优韧性下的常数MEB有效性，证明了MEB有效性比凸有效性更适合多维近似一致协议的设计，为后续研究提供了新思路和理论工具。

Abstract: Multidimensional approximate agreement requires $n$ processes with inputs in $\mathbb{R}^d$ to output vectors close to each other, despite up to $t$ Byzantine faults. Under convex validity, outputs must lie in the convex hull of the correct inputs, which leads to resilience thresholds that grow with the dimension. We instead study Minimum Enclosing Ball (MEB) validity and its multiplicative relaxation $c$-MEB validity. Our first contribution is Adaptive MEB Contraction, a coordinate-free algorithm that, in the synchronous model with $n>(d+1)t$ contracts the correct MEB radius by $1/\sqrt2$ per round for $α=1$ while satisfying $\sqrt2$-MEB validity. We also give an example showing that this bound is tight for our analysis.
  Our main technical contribution is a dimension-free inflation theorem: if every $β$ balls in a finite family of Euclidean balls have a common point, then inflating each radius by $\sqrt{β/(β-1)}$ guarantees a common intersection. Applying the theorem to the candidate balls defining the local MEB-safe areas results in a synchronous algorithm with optimal resilience $n>3t$, contraction factor $\sqrt3/2$ and $\sqrt6$-MEB validity. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first multidimensional approximate agreement algorithm with optimal resilience, constant $c$-MEB validity and coordinate-free contraction.
  We further extend the approach to the asynchronous setting. Without inflation we obtain resilience $n>(d+2)t$, contraction factor $\sqrt{2/3}$ and $\sqrt6$-MEB validity, whereas with inflation for $n>4t$ we get the contraction factor $\sqrt{15}/4$ and $2\sqrt{10}$-MEB validity. Finally, we compare our guarantees with existing algorithms, including Minimum-Diameter Averaging (MDA), for which we derive MEB-validity guarantees. Our algorithms achieve strictly better resilience while providing substantially stronger MEB-validity guarantees than MDA.

</details>

*Guangneng Chen,David Garcia Selfa,Pablo Quesada Barriuso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）在分布式多GPU训练场景下的可扩展性进行了系统性实证研究，在FinisTerrae III超算上使用最多8块NVIDIA A100 GPU（跨4个节点）通过PyTorch DDP进行评估，涵盖强扩展、弱扩展、通信开销和模型规模扩展四个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然KAN的逼近性质已受到广泛关注，但其在分布式、多GPU训练环境下的行为尚未被系统性表征。填补这一空白对KAN的实际部署至关重要。

Method: 在多节点、多GPU的HPC基础设施上开展数据并行KAN训练的可扩展性实证研究，从四个维度评估：强扩展、弱扩展、通信开销和模型规模扩展。实验在FinisTerrae III超算上进行，使用最多8块NVIDIA A100 GPU跨4个节点，采用PyTorch Distributed Data Parallel（DDP）。

Result: 1) KAN训练在8 GPU下达到74.7%的并行效率和5.97倍加速，与传统深度学习负载表现一致。2) 弱扩展呈现从单GPU到多GPU的初始吞吐下降后保持强稳定性。3) 通信开销呈非单调模式（1.3%-6.1%），主要由All-Reduce算法选择和节点间延迟驱动，而非KAN的边级梯度结构。4) 参数与内存比随模型规模增大而改善，但训练时间随规模增长表现不利。

Conclusion: 算子级与数据并行优化对KAN是互补的。作者提供了GPU拓扑和模型规模选择的部署指南，并讨论了合成回归评估的局限性，表明KAN在分布式训练中与传统深度学习负载具有一致的扩展特性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed activation functions and linear weights of Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) with learnable univariate functions on network edges, offering improved interpretability and, in some settings, competitive parameter efficiency. While the approximation properties of KANs have received considerable attention, their behavior under distributed, multi-GPU training has not been systematically characterized. This paper presents an empirical scalability study of data-parallel KAN training on multi-node, multi-GPU high-performance computing (HPC) infrastructure, evaluated along four dimensions: strong scaling, weak scaling, communication overhead, and model-size scaling.
  Experiments were conducted on the FinisTerrae III supercomputer using up to 8 NVIDIA A100 GPUs across 4 nodes with PyTorch Distributed Data Parallel (DDP). KAN training reaches 74.7% parallel efficiency at 8 GPUs with a 5.97x speedup, consistent with conventional deep learning workloads. Weak scaling shows an initial single-to-multi-GPU throughput drop followed by strong stability. Communication overhead follows a non-monotonic pattern (1.3%-6.1%), driven primarily by All-Reduce algorithm selection and inter-node latency rather than KAN's edge-wise gradient structure. The parameter-to-memory ratio improves with model size even as training time scales unfavorably.
  These results indicate that operator-level and data-parallel optimizations for KAN are complementary. We provide deployment guidelines for GPU topology and model-size selection, and discuss the limitations of a synthetic-regression evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [Interactive Debugger for Performance Portable Python HPC Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07912)
*Ivan Grigorik,Gabriel Kosmacher,George Biros,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PKDB是首个面向Python GPU及多线程底层内核的交互式调试器，支持断点、单步、变量查看等标准调试功能，并引入两项高级能力：内核暂停期间的实时代码求值，以及无需重启应用的运行时内核调用点替换。在Intel/AMD/NVIDIA CPU及NVIDIA/AMD GPU上评估显示开销有限，实用性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python在HPC领域被广泛使用（如PyKokkos将Python DSL翻译为运行于OpenMP线程CPU和各种GPU上的原生代码），但这类代码缺乏交互式调试支持。开发者只能依赖print语句、框架特定断言或仅限CPU执行（后者需修改程序/数据且可能掩盖设备特定bug）。

Method: PKDB利用Python和PyKokkos的动态特性，实现两大高级功能：(i) 实时代码求值——允许开发者在暂停的内核中间执行任意Python表达式或整个内核而无需重启进程；(ii) 内核调用点替换——允许运行时更新并重载正在运行的内核，仅需重新编译和重新执行内核，无需重启应用程序。同时保留实际设备上执行且无需修改源码。

Result: 在Intel、AMD、NVIDIA CPU及NVIDIA、AMD GPU上的性能评估表明，PKDB引入的开销有限，适合日常使用。

Conclusion: PKDB为Python HPC生态系统引入了关键的调试功能，填补了GPU/多线程低层内核缺乏交互式调试支持的空白，兼具标准调试功能与利用Python动态特性的高级调试能力，且性能开销可控。

Abstract: We propose PKDB, the first interactive debugger for GPU and multithreaded low-level kernels written in Python. Python is widely used in high performance computing (HPC), with frameworks such as PyKokkos translating Python-embedded domain-specific languages to native code that runs across OpenMP-threaded CPUs and various GPUs. Yet interactive debugging support for such code is absent: developers resort to print statements, framework-specific assertions, or CPU-only execution, the last of which requires altering the program or its data and can mask device-specific bugs. PKDB enables standard interactive debugging like breakpoints, stepping, and variable inspection while preserving actual on-device execution without source modification. Beyond these fundamentals, PKDB introduces two advanced capabilities that exploit the dynamic nature of Python and PyKokkos: (i) Live code evaluation, which lets developers execute arbitrary Python expressions or entire kernels in the middle of a paused kernel without restarting the process; (ii) Kernel call site substitution, which allows an actively running kernel to be updated and reloaded on the fly, so only the kernel is recompiled and re-executed without restarting the application. Our performance evaluation on Intel, AMD, and NVIDIA CPUs, and NVIDIA and AMD GPUs shows that PKDB introduces limited overhead and is practical for everyday use while introducing critical debugging features to the Python HPC ecosystem.

</details>


### [50] [MicroIntent: Intent-Based Placement Strategy for Microservice Application in the Compute Continuum Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07927)
*Koushikur Islam,Guilherme Da Cunha Rodrigues,Bahman Javadi,Rodrigo N. Calheiros*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在计算连续体中利用高层级用户意图（自然语言描述）进行微服务部署决策的架构。该架构使用生成式AI将用户意图翻译为低层级SLO，再结合基础设施描述决定微服务的部署位置，以实现SLO目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算连续体中微服务的部署位置对满足客户需求（如低延迟、存储需求、服务质量和可用性）至关重要。虽然SLO已被广泛用于部署决策，但低层级的SLO增加了用户使用门槛，阻碍了计算连续体的大规模采用，因此需要基于高层级业务词汇的部署决策方法。

Method: 提出一种架构，以自然语言表述的高层级用户意图作为输入，利用生成式AI（Generative AI）将意图翻译为低层级SLO，随后结合基础设施描述信息，决策应用中各微服务应部署的具体位置以满足SLO要求。

Result: 实现了该架构的原型系统并进行评估，验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过生成式AI将自然语言意图自动转化为低层级SLO，能够降低计算连续体用户的使用门槛，使基于高层级业务意图的微服务部署决策成为可能，从而推动计算连续体的大规模采用。

Abstract: The placement of microservices in the compute continuum plays a vital role in delivering services that comply with customers' needs, such as reduced latency, storage requirements, quality of service and availability. To achieve customers' needs in the geographically dispersed architecture of the compute continuum, Service Level Objectives (SLOs) have been largely used in decision-making to place microservices. However, because low-level SLOs increase the barrier to entry for continuum users, placement decisions based on high-level business vocabulary are required if the compute continuum is to be adopted at scale. This paper proposes an architecture for microservices placement decisions in the computing continuum utilizing high-level user intents described in natural language as input. The approach utilizes Generative Artificial Intelligence to translate the intents to low-level SLOs, which are used along with the infrastructure description to decide where different microservices that compose an application must be deployed so that SLOs are met. We implement and evaluate a prototype of the architecture to demonstrate the approach's feasibility.

</details>


### [51] [SemBridge: Compiling Consumer Observations into Cross-Stack Communication Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08231)
*Genlang Chen,Junyi Zhu,Yuanshan Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SemBridge提出了一种分布式张量系统与集合通信系统之间的语义桥梁，通过编译图和运行时事实为"消费者可见结果及其交付义务"生成类型化契约，弥合了跨厂商运行时（如CUDA/NCCL与CANN/HCCL）间无法共享原生通信器时双方抽象无法界定远程消费者观测内容的空隙。系统包含确定性降级器和符号检查器，并在多厂商环境中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式张量系统关注值的位置，集合通信系统优化操作的执行方式；但当跨厂商运行时边界无法共享原生通信器时，两种抽象都无法明确规定远程消费者必须观测到什么内容，由此产生了语义空白。

Method: SemBridge将图和运行时事实编译为类型化契约，捕捉来源、可替换性、完成性、权威性、需求性和原生域局部性。使用确定性lowerer生成后端无关的通信计划，并用符号检查器在CUDA/NCCL与CANN/HCCL执行前验证每个计划。另有独立布局规划器和字节最小化器作为对比分析。

Result: 布局规划器覆盖72种结构转换但仅建立54种完整义务；字节最小化器提出40个语义无效候选且全部被SemBridge拒绝。在9个真实边上生成观测感知方案，减少全部9个的启动次数及3个结果边的负载字节。跨CUDA/CANN实时运行实现对全logits重建、源投影和所有者令牌交付的执行。在两主机1GbE容量溢出部署中，源投影使结果流量削减超99.97%，吞吐提升8.92-80.20%。所有18对MiniMax重启（并发1/8/16）均偏好源投影。Qwen3-14B MLP切片验证了位精确激活分片交付与行并行部分结果的HCCL完成。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了"消费者观测"作为介于放置与集合执行之间的一层语义抽象，填补了分布式张量系统与集合通信系统之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Distributed-tensor systems specify where values reside, while collective systems optimize how requested operations execute. At a boundary between vendor runtimes that cannot share a native communicator, neither abstraction states what a remote consumer must observe. SemBridge fills this gap by compiling graph and runtime facts into a typed contract for the consumer-visible result and its delivery obligations. The contract captures provenance, substitutability, completion, authority, demand, and native-domain locality. A deterministic lowerer constructs backend-neutral communication plans, and a symbolic checker validates each plan before execution across CUDA/NCCL and CANN/HCCL. An independent layout-only planner handles all 72 structural transitions but establishes only 54 complete obligations; a byte-only minimizer proposes 40 semantically invalid candidates, all rejected by SemBridge. On nine real edges, SemBridge produces distinct observation-aware plans that reduce startups on all nine and payload bytes on the three result edges. A live CUDA/CANN run derives and executes full-logit reconstruction, source projection, and owner-token delivery from log-probability, token-only, and owner-scoped requests. On a measured two-host 1-GbE capacity-spillover deployment, source projection cuts result traffic by more than 99.97% and increases throughput by 8.92-80.20% across Dense, MoE, and MiniMax workloads. All 18 MiniMax restart pairs at concurrency 1, 8, and 16 favor source projection. A Qwen3-14B MLP slice additionally verifies bitwise activation-shard delivery and HCCL completion of row-parallel partials. These results establish consumer observation as a semantic layer between placement and collective execution.

</details>


### [52] [A Measurement Study of LLM Inference Trade-offs Across Edge Continuum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08307)
*Maysam Khatib,Moysis Symeonides,Demetris Trihinas,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对边缘连续体上自托管LLM推理进行了受控测量研究，评估了NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin和近边缘服务器（含CPU-only与GPU两种模式）上的多个开源LLM及量化变体，并与云端GPT-4o对比，揭示了计算侧指标（延迟、能耗）与端到端流式投递开销之间的权衡，表明仅凭计算侧推理指标可能导致延迟敏感的交互式Web服务放置决策次优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作智能Web服务的后端，但在边缘连续体上提供服务需要在质量、延迟、模型足迹和能耗之间取得平衡，目前缺乏系统的受控测量研究来指导模型与部署位置的决策。

Method: 在Jetson AGX Orin和近边缘服务器（含CPU-only与GPU-enabled两种推理模式）上进行受控基准测试，使用固定问答工作负载评估多个开源权重模型及量化变体，与GPT-4o作为云端参考对比，报告准确性、模型足迹、逐token解码延迟、预填充延迟和总执行能耗，并利用Pareto前沿分析研究不同流式投递开销下的部署决策变化。

Result: GPU-enabled服务器执行提供最低的计算侧延迟；Jetson Orin由于平台功耗较低而表现出更低的测量能耗；CPU-only执行在延迟方面始终被支配且有更高能耗；参数量和下载权重文件大小不能可靠预测准确性或延迟；在考虑流式token投递开销后，计算侧推理指标单独可能导致次优的部署放置决策。

Conclusion: 边缘连续体上的LLM部署决策需要考虑计算侧指标之外的端到端因素，特别是流式投递开销会显著改变最优部署位置，计算侧推理指标不足以指导延迟敏感的交互式Web服务的放置决策。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as backends for intelligent web services, but serving them across the edge continuum requires balancing quality, latency, model footprint, and energy. This paper presents a controlled measurement study of self-hosted LLM inference across edge and near-edge deployment nodes: an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin and a near-edge server with CPU-only and GPU-enabled inference modes. We evaluate multiple open-weight LLMs and quantization variants using a fixed question-answering workload, and compare them against GPT-4o as a cloud-hosted accuracy and latency reference. Our benchmarking pipeline reports accuracy, model footprint, per-token decoding latency, prefill latency, and overall execution energy. The results show that GPU-enabled server execution provides the lowest compute-side latency, while Jetson Orin shows lower measured energy, consistent with its lower platform power under our setup. CPU-only execution is consistently dominated in latency for our workload and shows higher measured energy. We also show that parameter count and downloaded weight-file size alone do not reliably predict observed accuracy or latency. Finally, using Pareto-frontier analysis, we study how deployment decisions may change under possible streamed-token delivery overheads, highlighting that compute-side inference metrics alone can lead to suboptimal placement for latency-sensitive interactive web services.

</details>


### [53] [Sample-Guided Exact Top-K Selection for Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08450)
*Siran Liu,Yang Xue,Theo Tang,Changxu Shao,Qian Cheng,Haimeng Ren,Donghua Jiang,Haipeng Ming,Lehua Ding,Zhonghan Lin,Shengying Wei,Wei Liu,Kai Liu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HPC-Ops Top-K，一种样本引导的精确Top-K选择器，用于不规则稀疏注意力分数行。它通过固定步长部分视图提议粗边界，用完整行遍历验证其充分性并形成候选集，在提交输出前处理欠填充提案，从而用采样控制常见路径开销但绝不牺牲正确性。在Hy4-Preview索引器分数上比最快的已验证外部精确基线快1.29-1.75倍，几何平均加速1.55倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力中精确Top-K阶段必须处理随上下文长度增长而增长的完整分数行，而现有的基数选择器在精确细化前需要先进行完整的行遍历才能发现第一个可操作的边界，导致需要第二次行规模遍历，开销过大。

Method: 利用"定位紧凑上尾所需分辨率远低于识别精确秩边界"的观察，固定步长部分视图保持与完整行秩的校准。方法包括：固定步长视图提出行局部粗边界；强制完整行传递验证充分性、形成候选集并初始化精确FP32细化；嵌套二级边界和精确恢复处理欠填提案；GPU实现融合完整行验证和候选形成，采用持久化、KV分割和直接精确执行的图捕获不规则行调度。

Result: 在Hy4-Preview索引器分数上，相比最快的外部精确基线在各种配置下获得1.29-1.75倍加速，几何平均1.55倍。在两个框架派生的稀疏注意力轨迹上分别获得1.36倍和1.48倍加速。

Conclusion: 样本引导方法能够在不牺牲正确性的前提下显著加速精确Top-K选择，采样控制常见路径开销的设计原则对稀疏注意力下游工作有效。

Abstract: Sparse attention bounds downstream attention work by retaining a fixed-size subset of indexed tokens, but its standalone exact Top-$K$ stage must still process materialized score rows whose length grows with context. Production radix selectors discover their first actionable boundary only after a complete-row pass, forcing another row-scale traversal before exact refinement. We observe that locating a compact upper tail requires substantially less resolution than identifying the exact rank boundary, and that fixed-stride partial views of the current row remain calibrated to the corresponding complete-row rank across ragged lengths. We present HPC-Ops Top-K, a sample-guided exact selector for ragged sparse-attention score rows. A fixed-stride view proposes a row-local coarse boundary; the mandatory complete-row pass certifies its sufficiency, forms the admitted candidate set, and initializes exact FP32 refinement over the unresolved frontier. A nested secondary boundary and exact recovery handle underfilled proposals before any output is committed, so sampling controls common-path work but never correctness. The GPU implementation fuses complete-row certification and candidate formation, and combines persistent, KV-split, and direct-exact execution behind graph-capturable ragged-row dispatch. We evaluate HPC-Ops Top-K on indexer scores from Hy4-Preview. It outperforms the fastest verified external exact baseline by $1.29$--$1.75\times$ across 20 operator configurations, with a $1.55\times$ geometric-mean speedup. It further achieves $1.36\times$ and $1.48\times$ speedups on two framework-derived sparse-attention traces. The implementation is available in HPC-Ops, Tencent's open-source high-performance operator library for LLM inference, at https://github.com/Tencent/hpc-ops.

</details>


### [54] [Measuring Sustainability in Multi-Scale High-Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08688)
*Carlos J Barrios,Frédéric Le Mouël,Yves Denneulin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多维指标框架，用于在从传统HPC向计算连续体过渡的背景下，表征多尺度混合架构系统并指导工作负载部署。该框架结合了架构性能指标（吞吐量、延迟、可扩展性）、系统利用率以及可持续性和精度指标（能效、功耗）。通过模块化混合测试平台的实验，揭示了各指标间的复杂关系，特别是精度与能耗之间的权衡，并为优化编排器和调度器（如Kubernetes）提供了指导基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HPC向计算连续体的转变带来了更复杂的多尺度混合架构，需要有效的资源管理和可持续实践。然而，缺乏一种能够全面表征这些系统并指导现代工作负载（包括AI和量子计算）部署策略的多维评估框架。

Method: 提出一个组合架构性能指标（吞吐量、延迟、可扩展性）、系统利用率以及可持续性和精度指标（能效、功耗）的多维指标框架，并在模块化混合测试平台上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验揭示了各指标之间的复杂关系，尤其是精度与能耗之间的权衡，以及混合节点的效率特性。框架提供的指导方针能够识别最优操作点。

Conclusion: 该框架为改进编排器和调度器（如Kubernetes）奠定基础，使其能够将AI、量子计算等要求苛刻的应用分配到合适的系统模块，在保证高性能的同时实现可持续性。

Abstract: The transition from traditional High Performance Computing (HPC) to the Computing Continuum emphasizes efficient resource management and sustainable practices across Multi-Scale hybrid architectures. This paper introduces a multidimensional metric framework to characterize these systems and guide deployment strategies for modern workloads. The framework combines Architectural Performance metrics (such as Throughput, Latency, Scalability), System Utilization, and key Sustainability and Accuracy indicators (such as Energy Efficiency and Power Consumption). Using a modular hybrid testbed, experiments reveal complex relationships among metrics, especially the trade-offs between accuracy and energy, and the efficiency of hybrid nodes. The guidelines help identify optimal operating points and lay the groundwork for improving orchestrators and schedulers (e.g., Kubernetes) to assign demanding applications, including AI and Quantum Computing, to suitable system modules, ensuring high performance and sustainability.

</details>


### [55] [Tools-CC-Bench: a Benchmark Suite for Collective Communication with Compression in HPC and AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08739)
*Haozhe Fan,Wei Wang,Xingchen Liu,Man Liu,Xingjian Tian,Haoquan Long,Zedong Liu,Daran Sun,Jinwu Yang,Bo Yang,Jie Liu,Yonggang Che,Hairui Zhao,Guangming Tan,Dingwen Tao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CC-Bench，一个轻量级、可扩展且面向应用的通信压缩基准测试套件，用于在真实执行条件下评估通信压缩技术，覆盖HPC和LLM负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式HPC和LLM工作负载的可扩展性依赖高效通信，但数据移动已成为主要性能瓶颈。通信压缩虽有潜力，但现有基准测试缺乏对多样化后端、真实数据集、应用特定精度指标及重叠导致资源竞争的支持，难以评估其实际收益。

Method: CC-Bench采用声明式应用-环境建模，将评测逻辑与通信库、数据集和保真度指标解耦，支持跨库移植评测。结合函数级拦截和硬件计数器监控，刻画每阶段延迟、硬件利用率、数值保真度和计算干扰，并使用HPC与LLM的代表性数据集进行评估。

Result: 在CPU和GPU集群上评估了三个支持压缩的通信库，揭示了精度-性能权衡和瓶颈，可指导实际部署与优化。

Conclusion: CC-Bench能够提供实际执行条件下通信压缩的综合评测，弥补现有基准的不足，有助于指导通信压缩在实际分布式系统中的部署与优化决策。

Abstract: Distributed HPC and LLM workloads increasingly require efficient communication for scalability, yet growing data movement has become a major performance bottleneck. Communication compression can reduce this overhead and complement execution-level optimizations, but its benefits remain difficult to assess because existing benchmarks lack support for diverse backends, realistic datasets, application-specific accuracy metrics, and overlap-induced resource contention. We present CC-Bench, a lightweight, extensible, and application-oriented benchmark suite for evaluating communication compression under realistic execution conditions. CC-Bench uses declarative application-environment modeling to decouple profiling logic from communication libraries, datasets, and fidelity metrics, enabling portable cross-library evaluation. It further combines function-level interception and hardware counter monitoring to characterize per-phase latency, hardware utilization, numerical fidelity, and computation interference. With representative datasets from HPC and LLM workloads, CC-Bench evaluates three compression-enabled communication libraries on CPU and GPU clusters, revealing accuracy-performance trade-offs and bottlenecks to guide practical deployment and optimization.

</details>


### [56] [Exploring the Genesis Platform Capabilities to Accelerate Scientific Discovery in OPAL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08844)
*Daniel Rosendo,Renan Souza,Kelsey Carter,John Lagergren,Frédéric Suter,Shelaine L. Curd,David Weston,Rafael Ferreira da Silva*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了美国能源部的Genesis Mission平台（通过美国科学云交付），为跨设施自主科学提供可复用服务，包括长期服务托管执行、模型版本化分发、大语言模型治理访问、实验数据共享基底和端到端溯源。以植物表型分析工作流为例，该平台将ORNL高级植物表型实验室与Frontier超算耦合，在40天镍处理实验中，将约12小时的人工分析替代为秒级到分钟级的交互式查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨设施自主科学需要各项目都不应独自构建的共性能力（如服务管理、模型分发、LLM治理访问、数据共享、溯源），需要一个统一的平台来提供这些基础设施服务。

Method: Genesis平台以可复用服务形式提供跨设施科学能力，通过美国科学云交付。论文以Orchestrated Platform for Autonomous Laboratories的植物表型工作流为案例，耦合ORNL先进植物表型实验室与Frontier超算。

Result: 在40天镍处理实验中，工作流将原先约12小时的人工分析替换为返回时间在秒到分钟级别的交互式查询，显著加速了科学发现过程。

Conclusion: Genesis平台展示了跨设施共享服务基础设施的可行性，能够有效支持自主实验并加速科学发现。

Abstract: Autonomous, cross-facility science requires capabilities that no individual project should have to build for itself: managed execution for long-lived services, versioned distribution of models to remote compute systems, governed access to large language models, a shared substrate for experimental data, and end-to-end provenance. The U.S. Department of Energy Genesis Mission platform, delivered through the American Science Cloud, provides these as reusable services. This paper reports how the Genesis platform enables cross-facility experiments and accelerates scientific discovery. We explore the plant phenotyping workflow of the Orchestrated Platform for Autonomous Laboratories as the exemplar: it couples Oak Ridge National Laboratory's Advanced Plant Phenotyping Laboratory with the Frontier supercomputer. In a 40-day nickel-treatment campaign, the resulting workflow replaced roughly twelve hours of manual analysis with interactive queries returning in seconds to minutes.

</details>


### [57] [ContinuumBench: Benchmarking Joint Autoscaling and Placement Across Evaluation Regimes in the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08946)
*Lanpei Li,Antonino Vaccarella,Vincenzo Lomonaco,Massimo Coppola*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ContinuumBench基准测试框架，用以严格评估云边控制器在服务放置、副本伸缩和资源预热方面的表现。通过完成率感知的计分方式、统一的比较协议以及基于ECLYPSE模拟器的实现，作者发现：在容量受限场景下，弹性容量而非放置策略的复杂性主导任务完成率；放置重规划在没有迁移的情况下无效；且不同的计分方式会改变控制器的排名结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云边控制器评估存在三个问题：放置与伸缩被分开研究；工作负载、连接与校准假设不透明；只统计完成任务指标而忽略未完成工作，导致贡献来源不明、比较不公平。

Method: 构建ContinuumBench基准：(1) 采用完成率感知会计方法，将迟到、未完成和丢弃的任务均计为截止时间违例；(2) 设计统一比较协议，在明确声明的场景和压力条件下比较仅放置控制器与可伸缩控制器；(3) 在ECLYPSE模拟器上增加到达过程、工作器弹性、间歇性传输、缓冲与故障机制以闭合控制回路。评估了九种控制器、四种场景、两种体制。

Result: 在容量受限体制下，弹性容量（而非放置策略复杂度）决定完成率；容量充足后，自动缩放策略决定任务能否按时到达。无迁移时放置重规划无实际效果，而零成本迁移构成例外。可伸缩控制器逼近过度供给的参考性能，仅放置控制器随负载劣化；放置质量仅在容量耗尽时才区分控制器。此外，仅计算完成率与完成率感知两种计分可导致控制器排名不同。

Conclusion: 评估方案本身会影响研究结论：必须采用完成率感知的度量，并在统一的容量与压力场景下同时考虑放置与伸缩，才能得到可信的控制器比较结论。在容量充足时，自动缩放策略的选择比放置优化更关键。

Abstract: Cloud-edge controllers coordinate service placement, replica scaling, and resource pre-warming to keep end-to-end latency within application deadlines. But evaluations often obscure the source of a reported gain: placement and scaling are studied separately; workload, connectivity, and calibration assumptions remain implicit; and metrics over completed tasks hide unfinished work. We present ContinuumBench, a benchmark that controls these factors. Its completion-aware accounting treats late, unfinished, and discarded tasks as deadline misses. A common protocol compares placement-only and scale-capable controllers under declared regimes and stressors. Built on the ECLYPSE simulator, ContinuumBench adds arrivals, worker elasticity, intermittent transport, buffering, and failures to close the control loop. We evaluate nine controllers across four scenarios and two regimes. The studied regimes are capacity-bound: elastic capacity, not placement sophistication, drives completion, and once capacity suffices, the choice of autoscaling policy decides how much of that work arrives on time. Placement re-planning has no measurable effect without relocation, while cost-free migration defines the observed exception. Consequently, scale-capable controllers approach an over-provisioned reference while placement-only controllers degrade with load; and placement quality separates controllers only once capacity is exhausted. Finally, the accounting choice itself changes the reported result: completion-only and completion-aware scoring can rank controllers differently.

</details>

### [58] [Memory as Infrastructure: Reliability Engineering for Persistent Agent Memory in Months-Long LLM-Assisted Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05510)
*Mike Helwig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在单一连续Claude Code会话中驱动633,000行代码库的长期项目级LLM编码代理操作经验，并提出了SIx Harness——一个开源的内存与连续性基础设施。该系统结合混合词汇-向量检索的本地长期记忆、精度门控的上下文注入、反重复决策存储、以及针对记忆子系统本身的可靠性工程（会话健康门、心跳遥测、告警疲劳预算）。文章提供了78,933次hook调用、85次记录故障（无一静默）的月度级生产运行记录，并提炼出七项设计原则与预注册消融协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理正从任务级向项目级跨越——需要在数月内、经历反复上下文压缩、在远大于任何上下文窗口的代码库上持续工作。然而，当前缺乏对持久化代理记忆子系统的可靠运行记录和可靠性工程实践，这正是作者试图填补的空白。

Method: 设计了SIx Harness记忆与连续性基础设施，包含：(1) 基于SQLite的混合词法-向量检索长期记忆（完全本地）；(2) 精度门控的上下文注入机制；(3) 决策与死胡同的反重复存储；(4) 为耐受压缩而定制的约定；(5) 记忆子系统的可靠性工程（会话启动健康门、心跳遥测、告警疲劳预算）。同时发布了预注册消融协议供其他团队复现。

Result: 基于N=1的月度级实测记录：78,933次hook调用；85次记录故障全部可追溯，其中84次发生在子系统最初三周，之后仅1次，最后20天零故障；注入层十日精度仪表显示零误报；三次生产事故从仪表读数追踪到结构性修复。

Conclusion: 从运行记录中提炼出七项设计原则，并坦承局限（N=1、无对照组、自我报告）。作者认为记忆子系统的可靠性工程是当前操作实践中缺失的关键环节，SIx Harness以MIT许可证开源发布，附带预注册消融协议供社区验证。

Abstract: LLM coding agents are crossing from task-scale to project-scale: single assisted efforts that run for months, across repeated context compactions, on codebases far larger than any context window. We report operational experience from one such effort (a research project under a single continuous Claude Code session line since January 2026, driving a 633,000-line codebase, with its memory subsystem continuously instrumented since July 2026) and describe SIx Harness, the open-source memory and continuity infrastructure that emerged from keeping it coherent.
  The harness combines per-project long-term memory (hybrid lexical-vector retrieval over SQLite, fully local), precision-gated context injection, anti-recurrence stores for decisions and dead-ends, conventions engineered to survive compaction, and, the part we argue is missing from operational practice, reliability engineering for the memory subsystem itself: a session-start health gate with discriminated failure modes, heartbeat telemetry designed so that no enumerated failure mode can pass unrecorded, and alert-fatigue budgeting borrowed from SRE practice.
  We present what we believe is the first months-scale instrumented operational record of a persistent agent-memory subsystem in production development use: 78,933 hook invocations; 85 recorded failures, none silent: 84 in the subsystem's first three weeks, one since, none in the final 20 days; an injection layer whose ten-day precision instrument shows zero false fires against an intact denominator; and three production incidents traced from instrument reading to structural fix. From the record we distill seven design principles, state our limitations plainly (N=1, no control arm, self-reported), and publish a tagged pre-registered ablation protocol that any team can run with the released MIT-licensed kit.

</details>


### [59] [Look Before You Prompt, and After: Scaffolding Human-AI Collaboration in Software Tutorial Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05563)
*Avinash Bhat,Vy Bui,Jin L. C. Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析17名技术作家的访谈并经过用户研究迭代设计，提出了一个名为dBlocks的工具，帮助LLM辅助的软件教程写作。dBlocks通过块（blocks）限定内容范围、上下文管理器编辑模型使用的上下文、内联执行验证代码等功能，在16人受试者实验中发现该工具显著提高了写作信心，并减少了验证摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具对软件教程作者（技术作家）缺乏支持，作者需要费力地引导和塑造模型输出为连贯、准确的学习资源。具体痛点包括：如何组装和结构化多个LLM响应、策划模型使用的上下文、以及验证生成内容。

Method: 采用人本设计方法：(1) 对17名技术作家进行访谈，提炼三项需求；(2) 设计dBlocks工具，包含三个核心功能——块（blocks）限定内容范围、上下文管理器（context manager）编辑上下文、内联执行（inline execution）验证代码；(3) 通过5人用户研究迭代优化设计；(4) 进行16人的组内实验，与参与者偏好工作流对比。

Result: 在16人组内实验中，使用dBlocks的参与者对所产教程的信心显著更高；验证摩擦减少——作者在起草过程中就验证代码而非推迟或跳过；块（blocks）功能帮助作者避免搜索冗长的聊天记录，将每个教程章节与其LLM对话保持在一起。

Conclusion: 该工作为支撑软件工程工作流中人类-AI协作的工具提供了启示，展示了人本设计如何指导LLM集成工具的开发。

Abstract: With LLMs, creating software tutorials now involves steering the model's output and shaping it into a coherent, accurate learning resource, yet existing LLM tools offer writers little support for this work. By analyzing interviews with technical writers ($N=17$), we identify three requirements for how they assemble and structure multiple LLM responses, curate the context the model uses, and verify the generated content. We designed a tool called dBlocks with the following features: blocks to scope content, a context manager to edit context, and inline execution to verify code. Following a human-centered design method, we iteratively refined the design through a user study ($N=5$). In a within-subjects lab study ($N=16$) comparing dBlocks with participants' preferred workflows for LLM-assisted authoring, participants reported significantly higher confidence in the tutorials they produced with dBlocks. In addition, the tool reduced friction in verification, with writers verifying code as they drafted rather than deferring or skipping it, and helped them avoid searching long chat histories by scoping their work into blocks that kept each tutorial section and its LLM conversation together. More broadly, our work offers implications for tools that scaffold human-AI collaboration in SE workflows and shows how human-centered design can guide the development of LLM-integrated tools.

</details>


### [60] [Grounded Skill Synthesis from Code at Scale for Agentic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05571)
*Yongqi Tong,Pan Wang,Hang Wang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang,Jiang-Ming Yang,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Code2Skill是一个全自动流水线，从GitHub仓库中提取源代码并将其转化为可验证、可迁移的代理技能库（CodeSkillBank），包含超过100万个被接受的技能记录，在多基准测试中将模型性能平均提升11.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能获取方法存在局限：基于轨迹的合成需要与特定环境交互，而从文档提取的技能缺乏可执行证据和验证。源代码提供了一条补充路径——无需先前的代理经验，却能提供可执行的证据来支撑抽象。

Method: Code2Skill是全自动流水线，包含两个核心阶段：(1) 将选定的代码单元转化为原子操作、复合工作流和重复模式的实现锚定记录；(2) 通过"源码盲重建"（source-body-blind reconstruction）和"源码感知比较"（source-aware comparison）验证每条记录。

Result: 在19,769个热门的活跃GitHub仓库上应用，生成了包含1,006,822条被接受记录的CodeSkillBank。在72个协议匹配的评估（覆盖9种模型设置和8个基准）中，使用检索到的CodeSkillBank技能的模型平均提升11.7%，在57个案例中优于匹配基线。在统一下游接口下，Code2Skill在全部7个共享基准上优于轨迹派生的技能库。从测试通过的AI生成代码合成的技能通过率达93.50%，略高于人类编写代码的93.00%。

Conclusion: 来自代码仓库的技能可以在代理积累足够交互经验之前，就提供有用的程序性知识。Code2Skill将仓库中嵌入的程序性知识转化为有依据、可验证且可迁移的代理技能，并且随着AI生成软件的快速增长，该流水线具有持续扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Reusable skills give agents transferable procedural knowledge, making scalable acquisition essential for extending agents beyond prior experience. Existing methods face two limitations: trajectory-based synthesis requires interactions with specific environments, while document-derived skills may lack executable evidence and verification. Source code offers a complementary path: it requires no prior agent experience yet provides executable evidence for grounding abstractions. We present Code2Skill, a fully automated pipeline that transforms selected code units into implementation-anchored records of atomic operations, composite workflows, and recurring patterns, then verifies each record through source-body-blind reconstruction and source-aware comparison. Applied to 19,769 popular, actively maintained GitHub repositories, Code2Skill produces CodeSkillBank, a grounded bank of 1,006,822 accepted records with workflow, boundary, provenance, and source-evidence metadata. Across 72 protocol-matched evaluations covering nine model settings and eight benchmarks, models augmented with retrieved CodeSkillBank skills improve by 11.7% on average over matched baselines and outperform them in 57 cases. Under a unified downstream interface, Code2Skill also outperforms trajectory-derived skill banks on all seven shared benchmarks, showing that repository-derived skills can provide useful procedural knowledge before agents accumulate sufficient interaction experience. Skills synthesized from tested AI-generated code achieve a 93.50% pass rate, compared with 93.00% for human-written code, providing initial evidence that the pipeline can expand with the growing volume of AI-generated software. Overall, Code2Skill transforms procedural knowledge embedded in repositories into grounded, verifiable, and transferable agent skills.

</details>


### [61] [The Impact of GenAI on the Future of Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05667)
*Travis Breaux,Anmol Singhal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI和大型语言模型（LLMs）如何变革需求工程（RE），涵盖从传统RE研究到提示编程和通用SE智能体的发展，并探讨了RE未来研究方向的两大维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs和通用SE智能体的发展，实现代码的成本降低，工程重心从编写正确代码转向表达、策划、验证和评估需求。作者希望通过综述现状，帮助RE研究者在这一转型中确定未来研究优先级。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法：首先调研AI用于RE的研究现状（LLM兴起之前），然后回顾LLMs的进展，接着调查两个新兴领域——提示编程（将LLM指令视为SE程序）和通用SE智能体（结合多种LLM进展形成半自动过程），最后沿两条轴展开RE未来展望。

Result: 识别出RE领域正经历根本性转型，从以编码为中心转向以需求为中心；提示编程和通用SE智能体成为关注焦点；RE未来演变沿两个维度展开：一是通过SE过程与需求交互方式的变化，二是软件开发者、利益相关者及最终用户对需求体验的变化。

Conclusion: 文章旨在为RE研究人员提供导航框架，帮助其在AI转型背景下选择未来研究优先级，领会需求工程角色从辅助编码到主导软件开发的转变。

Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), particularly large language models (LLMs), are transforming how we design and build systems by increasing access to domain knowledge and by providing automation support to software engineering (SE). As implementation becomes less expensive through generalist SE agents, engineering effort shifts away from writing correct code and toward expressing, curating, verifying, and evaluating requirements. In this paper, we survey the state of the art in AI for requirements engineering (RE) research leading up to the transformation, before reviewing advances in LLMs. We survey two subsequent research areas: prompt programming, which treats LLM instructions as a program in SE vernacular, and generalist SE agents, which combine multiple LLM advances to yield semi-autonomous processes that complete SE tasks. Finally, we explore the future of requirements engineering along two axes: matters changing how we interact with requirements through the SE process, and matters changing how requirements are experienced by software developers and stakeholders more broadly, including end-users. This article aims to inform how RE researchers can navigate this transformation in the selection of future research priorities.

</details>


### [62] [Regret Dominates Surprise: Design-Time Requirements Engineering for Agentic-AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05692)
*Nuwayyir Almohammadi,Rami Bahsoon,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于目标导向需求工程(GORE)的方法来建模与模拟智能体AI系统的安全自主性，引入名为MS-RGR的"遗憾-支配机制"，利用认知惊讶(新颖性检测)和认知遗憾(评价性风险)两个信号，解决自主体应在常规自主、反思推理或升级至人类之间如何选择的"三难问题"。通过老年护理监测和自动驾驶的随机仿真(100个种子)证明其能将近零的静默失败和约17.5倍的风险检测速度提升，并在208个AGENTHARM场景中验证了其能放大而非替代模型级安全训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向智能体AI领域的需求工程师在评估、规定和实施安全自主性方面面临挑战，而主流框架如GORE缺乏在认知不确定性下系统性处理这些挑战的机制。现有方法无法在需求工程层面显式支持智能体在不确定环境中的安全决策，特别是如何处理"自主运行-反思推理-升级人类"的时机选择问题。

Method: 在GORE基础上扩展建模与仿真能力，提出新颖的遗憾-支配机制(MS-RGR)来操作化安全自主性。MS-RGR利用两个互补信号：认知惊讶(用于新颖性检测)和认知遗憾(用于评价性风险)。在三重约束下决定智能体行为模式，并通过LTL安全属性保持形式可追溯性。在老年护理监控和自动驾驶两个领域实例化，并在AGENTHARM场景中进行回顾性代理验证。

Result: 100种子随机仿真表明，MS-RGR将静默失败降至近零，风险检测速度比仅有传感器的基线快约17.5倍。回顾性分析显示，在208个AGENTHARM场景(7个LLM)中，DRI门仅在模型基线安全表现强劲时(80%以上前置门拒绝率，如从84.1%提升至90.9%)能改善有害任务拒绝率，表明MS-RGR是放大而非替代模型层面的安全训练。

Conclusion: MS-RGR作为设计时安全约束在智能体AI需求工程中的初步可行性证据。该方法将认知不确定性的两个信号(惊讶与遗憾)纳入GORE框架，为安全自主性提供了可形式化追溯的操作化途径，但需注意其效果依赖于底层模型的安全性基础。

Abstract: Requirements engineers for agentic-AI domains face challenges in evaluating, specifying, and operationalizing safe autonomy. Mainstream frameworks, such as Goal-Oriented Requirements Engineering (GORE), lack mechanisms to systematically address these challenges under epistemic uncertainty. We contribute an approach that builds on GORE to model and simulate safe autonomy in agentic-AI systems. We introduce a novel Regret-Dominance Mechanism (MS-RGR) to operationalize safe autonomy. MS-RGR uses two signals: epistemic surprise (novelty detection) and cognitive regret (evaluative risk) to address the trilemma problem: should the agent operate in routine autonomy, undergo reflective reasoning, or escalate to human? We instantiate MS-RGR in elderly care monitoring and autonomous driving. A 100-seed stochastic simulation shows MS-RGR reduces silent failures to near-zero and detects risk approximately 17.5 times faster than a sensor-only baseline, remaining formally traceable via LTL safety properties. A retrospective proxy instantiation applying the DRI gate post-hoc over execution traces from 208 AGENTHARM scenarios across seven LLMs shows the gate improves harmful-task refusal only for models with strong baseline safety (over 80% pre-gate refusal, e.g., 84.1% to 90.9%), indicating MS-RGR amplifies rather than substitutes for model-level safety training. We discuss threats to validity, positioning MS-RGR as initial feasibility evidence for design-time safety constraints in agentic-AI requirements engineering.

</details>


### [63] [Diffs vs. Whole Files: An Empirical Comparison of Iterative Edit-Based and Direct Generation for Flutter/Dart Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05779)
*Andrej Andrejev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了代码编辑大语言模型的两种输出范式：直接生成（一次性输出整个修改文件）与基于diff的迭代生成（逐步输出局部搜索/替换编辑）。在共享的Flutter/Dart数据集上训练了两个模型（100M参数的Rainbow-Pony-100M和微调的Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B），并在约1790个保留任务上评估。结果显示直接生成在所有指标上显著优于基于diff的生成，且差距在控制任务难度后依然存在。唯一例外是短距离局部编辑的场景——即"任务局部性"（task locality）：当编辑空间上局部化时，基于diff的生成具有竞争力，尤其在重构和错误处理/边界情况修复这两类平均编辑步数最低的任务类别上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码编辑模型的训练和部署存在两种范式选择：直接生成整个文件，或迭代式的diff生成。基于diff的范式在理论上更具吸引力（更符合开发者编辑习惯、所需生成token更少、成本更低），但尚不清楚其在质量上是否真的可行或具有竞争力。作者希望系统性地回答：在相同数据和架构条件下，哪种范式更好？以及diff范式在什么条件下才能真正取胜？

Method: 作者在两个不同的模型规模/架构上（从零训练的100M参数Rainbow-Pony-100M和微调的Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B），分别用两种训练范式（直接文件生成 vs 迭代式的seek/replace diff生成）进行训练，形成4个模型。在共享的Flutter/Dart数据集上训练，用约1790个保留任务评估。评估指标包括编译/静态分析通过率、bits-per-byte（编辑密度）、与参考答案的字符级相似度、以及盲审LLM评判的达成度/正确性/代码质量。还通过matched-ID比较控制任务难度，并在两边都能编译的代码子集上进行对比分析。

Result: 直接生成在所有指标上显著优于diff-based生成——包括编译/静态分析通过率、bits-per-byte、字符级相似度和LLM盲审评分。该差距在控制任务难度（matched-ID比较）以及仅对两侧都能编译的代码进行限制后依然存在。然而发现了diff-based生成的唯一优势场景：短距离、空间局部化的编辑任务。其类别层面的胜利集中在重构和错误处理/边界修复两个类别——恰好是数据集中平均编辑步数最低的两类。

Conclusion: 作者提出了"任务局部性"（task locality）概念来解释diff-based生成的胜出条件：当编辑是空间上局部的、短距离的修改时，diff范式具有竞争力；而对于全局性的、跨代码多处的修改，直接生成更优。这为工程实践提供了清晰的指导——选择哪种训练/部署范式应根据任务的编辑局部性特征来决策，而不是一概而论。

Abstract: Large language models used for code editing can be trained and deployed in at least two output regimes: direct generation, where the model emits the entire modified file in one shot, and iterative diff-based generation ("steps"), where the model emits a sequence of localized search/replace edits applied one at a time until it signals completion or a step budget is exhausted. The diff-based regime is attractive because it mirrors how developers edit code and should require far fewer generated tokens per turn. We train two code models - a 100M-parameter model trained from scratch (Rainbow-Pony-100M) and a fine-tuned Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B - in both regimes on a shared Flutter/Dart dataset, and evaluate all four resulting models on a held-out set of approx 1,790 tasks per model. Direct generation substantially outperforms diff-based generation on every metric we measure - compilation/static-analysis pass rate, bits-per-byte, character-level similarity to the reference, and blinded LLM-judge ratings of goal fulfillment, correctness, and code quality - and the gap persists after controlling for task difficulty via a matched-ID comparison and when restricting to code that compiles on both sides.
