We then identify a single, architecture-independent mechanism behind the conditions where diff-based generation does win: it is competitive on short, spatially localized edits, and its category-level wins concentrate in exactly the two task categories - refactoring and error-handling/edge-case fixes - with the lowest mean edit-step count in our dataset. We term this task locality and discuss its implications for when an edit-based training regime is and is not the right choice for a code-editing model.

</details>


### [64] [Correct Tests Are Not Enough: Measuring and Training Oracle Conversion in Specification-Based Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05879)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究自然语言规范驱动的测试生成中，输入发现与输出预测（oracle转换）之间的交互关系。作者提出在生成五个输入-输出测试时，通过可执行的奖励分解和套件级oracle转换测量，同时保留输入击杀和有效击杀两个奖励信号（使用附加GRPO目标），在不需推理时执行的情况下提升测试质量。在TC-Bench上，三步独立训练的Qwen3.5-9B将完整测试正确率从28.59%提升至42.54%，并通过消融实验和源-神谕交叉实验揭示了NoKill到FullKill差异主因是更难的输入选择而非输出预测不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成测试需要同时满足两个独立要求：找出暴露缺陷行为的输入，以及给出正确的预期输出。这两个要求不一定同步改进——模型可以通过选择更容易的输入来提高测试正确性，或发现有用的输入但无法预测其预期输出。这种输入效用与输出预测能力之间的权衡尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1) 生成器在单次响应中联合输出五个输入-输出测试；2) 审计过的参考程序提供正确性反馈，固定缺陷程序库提供两个效用信号：潜在输入击杀（input kill）和检查生成输出后的有效击杀（effective kill）；3) 采用附加GRPO目标保留两个信号，无需推理时执行；4) 通过50步消融实验和64个训练池任务的固定输入源-神谕交叉实验进行机制分析。

Result: 在506个训练任务和142个评估任务的审计TC-Bench子集上，三步独立训练的Qwen3.5-9B在step 75时：完整测试正确率从28.59%提升至42.54%，输入击杀从24.06%提升至25.27%，有效全击杀从12.23%提升至14.15%。50步消融显示移除击杀奖励可提升正确率和全击杀但将输入击杀降至21.60%。交叉实验表明NoKill到FullKill的主要差异源于更难的输入选择而非相同输入上的输出预测更差。

Conclusion: oracle转换被识别为可测量的瓶颈，研究展示了在联合测试生成中保留输入效用反馈的优势与局限。移除击杀奖励虽然能提升测试正确性，但会降低输入发现能力，揭示了两者之间存在根本性权衡。

Abstract: Generating tests from a natural-language specification requires both an input that exposes faulty behavior and a correct expected output. These requirements need not improve together: a model can increase test correctness by choosing easier inputs, or discover useful inputs whose expected outputs it cannot predict. We study this interaction through executable reward decomposition and suite-level oracle-conversion measurement. Our generator jointly emits five input--output tests in one response. During training, audited reference programs provide correctness feedback, while a fixed bank of faulty programs provides two utility signals: potential input kill and effective kill after checking the generated output. An additive GRPO objective preserves both signals without requiring execution at inference time. On an audited TC-Bench split with 506 training and 142 evaluation tasks, three independently trained Qwen3.5-9B runs at step 75 increase full-test correctness from 28.59\% to 42.54\%, input kill from 24.06\% to 25.27\%, and effective full kill from 12.23\% to 14.15\%. Matched 50-step ablations reveal a trade-off: removing kill rewards yields higher correctness and slightly higher full kill, but lowers input kill to 21.60\%. A fixed-input source--oracle crossover on 64 training-pool tasks attributes the principal NoKill-to-FullKill difference to harder input selection rather than worse output prediction on identical inputs. These results identify oracle conversion as a measurable bottleneck and show the benefits and limits of preserving input-utility feedback in joint test generation.

</details>


### [65] [Broken on Arrival: Silently Defective LLM Artifacts in Public Model Registries and How to Catch Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05881)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对本地运行的大语言模型量化产物（GGUF）进行了系统性审计：测试了305个Ollama官方库和22个HuggingFace社区库的量化模型，发现官方库中有5个静默缺陷产物（1.6%），其中Qwen2.5-Coder-3B和phi3.5-mini的转换在多个推理后端上均无法完成任务。研究还发现小型模型因极端量化被误判的问题，以及社区转换存在后端依赖失败的现象（CUDA失败但Metal通过）。作者发布了审计数据集和quantcheck验收测试工具，主张模型注册中心应像包注册中心一样建立验收门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者越来越多地通过从公共注册中心拉取量化的GGUF产物在本地运行大语言模型，但分发管道中没有任何环节在产物到达用户之前对其进行功能性测试。现有注册机制缺乏对量化模型转换质量的验证。

Method: 作者测试了327个量化代码模型产物（305个来自Ollama官方库，覆盖15个模型系列的所有合格量化级别；22个来自HuggingFace社区），先运行15项冒烟测试套件，可疑产物再接受164项全面评估、第二推理后端、独立分销商转换作为仲裁，社区文件还在其自有模板下重测。

Result: 官方库存在5个静默缺陷产物（Qwen2.5-Coder-3B的4个转换和phi3.5-mini的1个转换），在双后端上164项任务和冒烟测试全部为零分，而相同模型的独立转换正常工作（占官方产物1.6%，29个模型与大小转换组中的2个）。仲裁链澄清了小模型被误判的问题，并揭示了两个社区转换在后端依赖上的失败。两个确认的缺陷产物输出表面统计在健康范围内，仅靠低噪声启发式检测不可见。

Conclusion: 模型注册中心需要一个类似包注册中心已有的验收门槛——即functional acceptance gate，以确保分发的量化产物确实可正常工作，而非仅仅存在。

Abstract: Developers increasingly run large language models locally by pulling quantized GGUF artifacts from public registries, yet nothing in the distribution pipeline functionally tests these conversions before they reach users. We executed 327 quantized code-capable model artifacts: 305 from the official Ollama library, spanning 15 model lines at every eligible quantization level at or under 8 GB, and 22 from the most-downloaded community repositories on HuggingFace. Each ran a 15-task smoke suite calibrated so that healthy artifacts pass while a known-broken one fails; suspects then faced full 164-task evaluation, a second inference backend, an independent distributor's conversion of the same model and quantization as referee, and, for community files, re-testing under the artifact's own template. The official library carries five silently defective artifacts, a batch of four Qwen2.5-Coder-3B conversions and one phi3.5-mini conversion, that solve zero of 164 tasks and zero of the smoke suite on both backends while independent conversions of the same models work: 1.6% of official artifacts, 2 of 29 model-and-size conversion groups. The adjudication chain cleared small-model artifacts that a naive threshold would condemn as broken when they are merely collapsed by extreme quantization, and it exposed two older community conversions that degrade badly on CUDA yet pass on Metal: not defective files but backend-dependent failures, a third phenomenon no registry currently tests for. Two confirmed defects produce output whose surface statistics sit inside the healthy range, invisible to any low-noise heuristic short of execution. We release the audit dataset, the quantcheck acceptance-testing tool, and disclosure reports for every confirmed defect (https://github.com/aditi-p31/quantcheck), and argue that model registries need the acceptance gate that package registries already run.

</details>


### [66] [VibeCheck: Assessing the Quality of LLM-Generated Unit Tests: A Multi-agent Empirical Study across Heterogeneous Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05978)
*Anika Tabassum,Mushahid Intesum,Md. Fahim Arefin,Tarannum Shaila Zaman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VibeCheck是一项针对LLM驱动的IDE代理（Kiro、Antigravity、Cursor，底层为Claude Sonnet 4.5）在15个学生开发仓库中生成单元测试的实证研究。研究发现一个"执行充分性差距"：生成的测试虽然可运行，但往往缺乏强断言和有意义的行覆盖，采用五维度评估框架（可运行性、断言强度、逻辑与边界情况覆盖、隔离/确定性、可维护性）及交叉代理同行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要依赖执行成功或覆盖率指标，这些指标可能掩盖更深层的质量问题，如弱断言、缺失边界情况、隔离性差和维护性有限。需要一个更全面的评估框架来捕捉这些质量问题。

Method: 在15个Python和JavaScript/TypeScript仓库中评估三种IDE代理（Kiro、Antigravity、Cursor），使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为底层代理。采用仓库级、零样本条件下的五维度评估框架（可运行性、断言强度、逻辑与边界覆盖、隔离/确定性、可维护性），并结合留一法交叉代理同行评估。

Result: 存在明显的执行充分性差距：生成的测试经常可运行但缺乏强断言和有意义的行覆盖。弱断言和缺失边界情况比阻塞性失败更常见，表明可运行的测试仍可能内容浅薄。

Conclusion: VibeCheck提供了面向可靠性评测LLM生成测试的框架，超越了简单的通过/失败结果，有助于识别和改善测试生成中的深层质量问题。

Abstract: LLM-based IDE agents are increasingly used to generate repository-grounded unit tests, yet common evaluations often rely on execution success or coverage. These metrics can miss deeper quality issues such as weak assertions, missing edge cases, poor isolation, and limited maintainability. This paper presents VibeCheck, an empirical study of unit test generation across 15 student-developed Python and JavaScript/TypeScript repositories. We evaluate Kiro, Antigravity, and Cursor with Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the underlying agent, under repository-only, zero-shot conditions using a five-dimensional rubric covering runnability, assertion strength, logic and edge-case coverage, isolation/determinism, and maintainability. We also apply leave-one-out cross-agent peer evaluation to compare tools and identify failure patterns. Results show a clear execution-adequacy gap: generated tests are often runnable but frequently lack strong assertions and meaningful behavioral coverage. Weak assertions and missing edge cases occur more often than blocking failures, showing that runnable tests can still be shallow. VibeCheck provides a reliability-oriented framework for evaluating LLM-generated tests beyond pass/fail outcomes.

</details>


### [67] [Replications, Revisions, and Reanalyses: Managing Empirical Evidence in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05983)
*Julian Frattini,Jannik Fischbach,Davide Fucci,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Daniel Mendez,Robert Feldt,Richard Torkar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于软件工程（SE）领域实证研究综合的框架，通过方法工程构建，将零散的实证证据系统化地组织起来，区分复制（replications）、修订（revisions）和再分析（reanalyses）三种证据演化类型，分别提升外部效度、内部效度和结论效度，从而超越传统元分析只能综合精确复制研究的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的实证研究综合很少见，即使有也多限于纯叙述性综合。虽然元分析可以综合多个定量结果，但只有在精确复制研究中才能产生可靠结果，无法从研究变异中推广。研究旨在将研究综合的边界扩展到当前技术水平之上，以便系统管理实证证据及其演化。

Method: 采用方法工程（method engineering）从经过验证的独立方法片段构建一个研究综合框架。该框架允许研究者将新证据与现有证据体建立清晰关系，并系统扩展对研究现象的知识。框架定义了证据演化的三种类型：复制（同假设新情境，提升外部效度）、修订（挑战现有假设，提升内部效度）、再分析（替换分析方法，提升结论效度），并为每种效度维度提供清晰评估标准。

Result: 将该框架应用于两个研究领域，显式建模了现有证据片段之间的关系，展示了框架的实际可行性。通过系统化证据演化和对各效度维度的清晰评估标准，该框架能够确定研究领域的前沿。

Conclusion: 该框架为SE领域系统化演化实证证据提供了视角，支持该领域更有建设性和生产力的进步，推动研究综合超越精确复制的局限，实现从研究变异中系统推广知识。

Abstract: One aspired outcome of empirical research on quantitative data is a variance theory, i.e., a quantification of the effect of an independent on a dependent variables. The validity of variance theories stems from the synthesis of multiple pieces of evidence, which increases its validity beyond the findings of a single study. However, research synthesis in SE is rare and if done mostly limited to purely narrative syntheses. At best, researchers perform meta-analyses to synthesize variance theories from several quantitative results. But even meta-analyses only produce reliable results when synthesizing exact replications yet fail to generalize from variations. We aim to extend the frontier of research synthesis beyond the state-of-the-art to systematically manage empirical evidence and its evolution. We apply method engineering to construct a framework for research synthesis from proven, individual method fragments. The framework allows researchers to put new evidence in a clear relation to an existing body of evidence and systematically expand knowledge about a studied phenomenon. We demonstrate the application of this framework to two fields of research by explicitly modeling the relationship between existing pieces of evidence. The framework puts three types of evolution of evidence into relation: (1) replications investigate the same hypothesis in a new context to improve external validity, (2) revisions challenge an existing hypothesis to improve internal validity, and (3) reanalyses replace analysis methods to improve conclusion validity. Through a systematic evolution of evidence and clear assessment criteria for each dimension of validity, the proposed framework can determine the frontier of a field of research. The framework provides a perspective to systematically evolve empirical evidence in SE, supporting more constructive and productive advances in our field.

</details>


### [68] [SemVul: Semantic-Enhanced Graph Neural Networks for Code Property Graph-based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06035)
*Younas Affan,Leonardo Regano,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SemVul，一种基于代码属性图（CPG）结合语义增强的漏洞检测流水线，通过整合节点级和边缘级语义嵌入（利用预训练代码嵌入技术）来捕捉程序的结构流和语义含义，在多个GNN架构和公开基准数据集上验证，表现出更好的泛化能力和更高的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码漏洞是全球网络攻击的根源，现有漏洞检测方法在泛化能力和学习漏洞代码模式方面存在局限，需要更有效的检测方法。

Method: SemVul采用基于代码属性图（CPG）的漏洞检测方法，结合语义级增强；整合节点级和边缘级语义嵌入，利用预训练代码嵌入技术；系统评估多种GNN架构；对编程语言具有通用性并支持多种架构。

Result: 实验结果显示SemVul在公开基准数据集上优于现有方法，具有更好的泛化能力和更高的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过整合结构信息和语义信息，SemVul显著提升了漏洞检测性能，提供了比现有方法更好的通用性和准确性，是一种有效的漏洞检测方案。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities in source code are often the root cause of cyberattacks worldwide, as attackers exploit weaknesses in software to gain unauthorized access, steal data, or disrupt services. In this study, we evaluated existing research approaches and propose SemVul, a vulnerability detection pipeline that demonstrates better generalization and higher accuracy in learning vulnerable code patterns. We propose a Code Property Graph-based vulnerability-detection approach combined with semantic-level enhancement, enabling the model to capture both the program's structural flow and the semantic meaning of the code. Our approach integrates both node-level and edge-level semantic embeddings using pre-trained code embedding techniques. We systematically evaluate multiple GNN architectures on publicly available benchmark datasets. SemVul is generic with respect to the programming language and supports multiple architectures. By integrating structural and semantic information, the proposed approach improves vulnerability detection performance. Our results show that SemVul outperforms existing approaches and provides better generalization.

</details>


### [69] [SpecCoder: Specification-Aware Code Generation with Curriculum Dual-Task Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06041)
*Yixuan Li,Mingxuan Huang,Jiajing Wang,Weidong Yang,Xinyi Liu,Ben Fei,Lipeng Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SpecCoder，一个规格感知的两阶段训练框架，用于代码生成。第一阶段采用规格引导的SFT训练模型从原始需求推导结构化规格分析并生成条件化代码；第二阶段引入课程式双任务GRPO，联合优化规格引导的生成和判别，增强规格与代码行为的一致性。在APPS、CodeContests等基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成方法在应对复杂自然语言需求时效果不佳，容易遗漏关键约束。训练无关方法（如提示或代理工作流）和训练方法（主要优化最终代码输出）都缺乏对"原始需求→结构化规格→具体实现行为"这一中间映射过程的监督，导致即使生成规格，实现也难以一致地反映它。

Method: 两阶段规格感知训练框架：(1) 规格引导SFT——训练LLM推导结构化规格分析并基于规格生成代码；(2) 课程式双任务GRPO——联合优化规格引导的代码生成任务与规格判别任务，强化规格与代码行为之间的对应关系。

Result: 在APPS、CodeContests和xCodeEval上持续提升独立代码生成和基于代理的工作流表现；在BigCodeBench-Hard和ClassEval上的额外评估、人工评估及扰动实验进一步验证了结构化规格在指导代码生成和判别中的作用。

Conclusion: 规格感知的训练方法能有效提升LLM在复杂编程任务上的代码生成质量，结构化规格在引导代码生成和判别两方面都发挥着关键作用，且能泛化到独立生成与代理式工作流等多种场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made substantial progress in code generation but still struggle with challenging programming tasks that require understanding rich natural language requirements. These requirements often specify problem goals, input/output formats, constraints, examples, and edge cases. Overlooking even one may produce executable but functionally incorrect code. Existing training-free methods mainly rely on prompting or agent-based workflows, while training-based methods typically optimize final code outputs. However, existing approaches provide limited supervision for learning the intermediate mapping from raw requirements to structured specifications and for grounding them in concrete implementation behavior. Consequently, models may omit critical constraints, and even when an explicit specification is produced, the implementation may fail to reflect it consistently. Motivated by this gap, we propose SpecCoder, a specification-aware two-stage training framework for code generation. SpecCoder first employs specification-guided SFT to train LLMs to derive structured specification analyses and generate code conditioned on them. It then introduces curriculum dual-task GRPO, which jointly optimizes specification-guided generation and discrimination to encourage stronger correspondence between specifications and code behavior. Experiments on APPS, CodeContests, and xCodeEval demonstrate the effectiveness of specification-aware training, with SpecCoder consistently improving both standalone code generation and agent-based workflows. Additional evaluations on BigCodeBench-Hard and ClassEval, alongside human evaluation and perturbation studies, further validate the role of structured specifications in guiding code generation and discrimination.

</details>


### [70] [SkillSpec: Intent-Masked Specification Reasoning for Agent Skill Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06052)
*Yizhuo Zhang,Bo Kang,Yi Yang,Zhiyu Duan,Zhouteng Ye,Shunkun Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSpec，一个Hoare风格的框架，将智能体技能（skill）的正确性问题转化为规格推理问题。通过将异构技能库统一为图表示，结合意图掩码（intent mask）调控不同视角的上下文，联合推理以发现潜在缺陷并在隔离沙箱中自动验证。在515个真实技能上识别出763个经人工确认的缺陷，精度达61.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统越来越依赖可重用的技能抽象来整合经验知识和领域专长，但这些技能的正确性难以保证。其失败模式超越传统代码缺陷，表现为意图冲突等微妙的语义不一致问题——这些问题会被底层模型掩盖为静默失败。此外，技能正确性必须基于预期的任务边界和泛化能力来判定，现有的代码验证方法无法覆盖这类需求。

Method: SkillSpec采用Hoare风格的框架将技能正确性问题形式化为规格推理。具体步骤为：(1) 将包含自由形式指令和异构资源的技能库转换为统一的图表示，对齐描述、指令和代码工件；(2) 对每个节点从周围声明的意图推导ExpectSpec（期望规格），并从部分披露意图下的编码行为推断FactSpecs（事实规格）；(3) 通过意图掩码控制整体（holistic）、谱系（lineage）、邻域（neighborhood）和局部（local）四种视图对上下文的访问，在避免过度上下文带来的偏差与上下文不足导致的缺乏支撑推断之间取得平衡；(4) 联合推理各视图标记候选缺陷，并在隔离沙箱中自动验证。

Result: 在515个来自SkillsBench和广泛下载仓库的真实技能上进行评估，SkillSpec识别出239个技能中的763个经人工确认的缺陷，精度61.2%。节点级分析显示，跨多个模型家族的规格推理对代码节点始终可靠，而纯文本节点仍是主要瓶颈。大多数缺陷出现在声明意图与实现之间的边界处。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，显式规格为真实智能体生态系统的技能质量保障提供了实用基础。意图-实现边界的缺陷集中性验证了规格推理方法的有效性，同时也指出了纯文本节点处理是未来研究需要突破的关键方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agent systems increasingly depend on reusable skill abstractions for consolidating experiential knowledge and domain expertise. These artifacts typically bundle free-form instructions with heterogeneous resources. However, ensuring their correctness remains challenging. Their failure modes transcend conventional code defects to subtle semantic inconsistencies such as intent conflicts, which manifest as silent failures masked by the underlying model. Moreover, skill correctness must be grounded in intended task boundaries and generalizability. We propose SkillSpec, a Hoare-style framework that formulates skill correctness as a specification reasoning problem. It transforms a heterogeneous skill repository into a unified graph representation that aligns descriptions, instructions and code artifacts. For each node, SkillSpec derives an ExpectSpec from the surrounding declared intent, and infers FactSpecs from encoded behavior under partially disclosed intent. An intent mask regulates access to holistic, lineage, neighborhood, and local views to balance the bias introduced by excessive context against unsupported inference caused by insufficient context. SkillSpec jointly reasons over these views to flag candidate defects, and automatically validates them in an isolated sandbox. On 515 real-world skills from SkillsBench and widely downloaded repositories, SkillSpec identified 763 manually confirmed defects across 239 skills, achieving 61.2% precision. The node-level analysis across multiple model families shows that specification reasoning is consistently reliable for code nodes, whereas plain-text nodes remain a major bottleneck. Most defects arise at the boundaries between declared intent and implementation, demonstrating that explicit specifications provide a practical foundation for skill quality assurance in real-world agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [71] [Beyond Lexical Metrics: Sentence-Embedding Detection of Reviewer Habituation in AI Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06213)
*Haoran Yu,Lifei Liu,Danping Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析400名重复审稿人在207天内对AI生成代码的11,429次审查，发现批准率从早期30.5%升至晚期36.6%，传统词汇特征无单调下降，但句子嵌入的潜在分布结构中可检测到审稿人适应行为，且语言变化滞后于批准行为变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码代理大规模提交拉取请求，尚不清楚审稿人是否会因重复接触而降低审查严格度，以及审查评论是否能揭示这一变化。这关乎AI生成代码到生产环境之间的质量关卡可靠性。

Method: 使用207天内400名重复审稿人的11,429次审查及10,104条人工内联评论，通过四种手工艺语言特征（词汇多样性、香农熵、技术特异性、建设性可操作性）、句子嵌入质心距离及逻辑回归/MLP分类器、格兰杰因果分析等方法进行检测。

Result: 批准率从30.5%升至36.6%（p=8.6×10^-8，Cohen's d=0.25）；四种词汇特征无显著单调下降（|rho|≤0.53）；嵌入特征可检测晚期质心偏移（p<0.001），MLP达F1=0.74；格兰杰分析显示批准率变化预测后续技术特异性转变，反向仅一个滞后下显著。

Conclusion: 审稿人适应可通过潜在分布结构而非经典词汇指标检测，且语言转变跟随而非先于批准行为变化，暗示可用嵌入方法监测审查质量退化的信号。

Abstract: Code review is a key quality checkpoint between AI-generated code and production. As AI coding agents submit pull requests at scale, it is unclear whether reviewers reduce scrutiny with repeated exposure and whether review comments reveal this change. We study 11,429 reviews from 400 repeat reviewers over 207 days, paired with 10,104 human-authored inline comments from AIDev. Approval rates rise from 30.5% in reviewers' early periods to 36.6% in late periods (Wilcoxon p = 8.6 x 10^-8; Cohen's d = 0.25). However, four hand-crafted linguistic features - lexical diversity, Shannon entropy, technical specificity, and constructive actionability - show no monotonic decline across exposure deciles (all Spearman absolute rho <= 0.53, p >= 0.11; Bonferroni-corrected Mann-Whitney p >= 0.36). A logistic-regression classifier based on these features reaches F1 = 0.485, below the majority-class baseline. Sentence-embedding structure does carry signal: reviewers' late-period comment centroids shift farther from their early-period centroids than under within-reviewer random permutations (Wilcoxon p < 0.001), and a small MLP using three embedding statistics reaches F1 = 0.74 under 5-fold reviewer-stratified cross-validation. Granger analysis shows that approval-rate changes predict later shifts in technical specificity at all tested lags (p < 0.001), while the reverse direction is significant at only one of four lags. Reviewer adaptation is therefore detectable in latent distributional structure rather than in classical lexical metrics, and language shifts follow rather than precede changes in approval behavior.

</details>


### [72] [SWE-Test: Benchmarking LLM Vulnerability Discovery via Input Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06229)
*Yuanxiang Shi,Jiayi Lin,Xuanyong Lin,Liangcai Su,Yeheng Duan,Wei Wang,Qi Han,Bing Zhao,Wei Hu,Xander Xu,Chenxiong Qian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Test基准，将LLM智能体的漏洞发现能力重新定义为输入预测任务，利用覆盖率引导的模糊测试在真实C/C++程序中挖掘深层目标分支，并要求智能体预测能驱动执行到达给定分支的输入，将漏洞发现分解为三种任务模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞发现基准存在数据污染风险、无法计算的召回率、依赖合成bug以及仅报告单一端到端结果无法定位智能体失败环节等问题。漏洞发现实际上是一个复合能力（代码理解、约束推断、输入构造、执行、迭代修正），需要可分解、有确定性真值的评测方法。

Method: 将漏洞发现评测重构为输入预测任务：利用覆盖率引导模糊测试在真实C/C++程序中挖掘深层目标分支，让智能体预测能驱动执行到达给定分支的输入。分解为三种模式：Open-loop（无反馈测输入构造）、Feedback-enabled（带距离oracle测反馈修正）、Online Arena（无边预设目标，按覆盖率增益评测路径探索）。覆盖22个真实C/C++程序、15个领域。

Result: 评估15种默认配置的模型-脚手架组合，最佳模型在Feedback-enabled模式仅达55.0%通过率；七个配对Claude Code配置在有无反馈下平均通过率为36.4% vs 19.3%。智能体在6个程序中集体确认了13个不同bug。失败分解显示约束推断（而非导航）是主导瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究表明现有LLM智能体的漏洞发现能力仍有限，约束推断是主要瓶颈。SWE-Test提供可分解、抗污染、确定性真值的评测环境，为后续提升智能体漏洞发现能力提供基础。

Abstract: Vulnerability discovery is becoming an important ability of large language model (LLM) agents: agents that silently miss real defects leave critical software exposed. Rigorously measuring this ability is therefore urgent, but existing benchmarks are gameable through data contamination, score recall against an unknowable vulnerability set, often rely on synthetic bugs, and report a single end-to-end verdict that cannot localize where an agent fails. Vulnerability discovery is a composite ability: an agent must comprehend source code, infer input constraints, construct inputs, execute them, and iteratively correct from feedback. We recast its measurement as an input-prediction task with a closed, deterministic ground truth: using coverage-guided fuzzing, we mine deep target branches in real-world C/C++ programs and ask an agent to predict an input that drives execution to a given branch. This decomposes discovery into three task modes over 22 real-world C/C++ programs spanning 15 domains. Open-loop and Feedback-enabled share 60 fixed-target task instances across 16 of these codebases (13 domains), testing input construction without and with a distance oracle to isolate code comprehension from feedback-driven correction. Online Arena instead removes the predefined target and scores path exploration by coverage gain on a separate, partially overlapping pool of 11 programs; agents collectively confirmed 13 distinct bugs across six programs. Evaluating 15 default-effort model-scaffold configurations, the best reaches only 55.0% pass rate in the Feedback-enabled mode, and the mean across seven paired Claude Code configurations is 36.4% with feedback versus 19.3% without. Decomposing failures, we find constraint inference, not navigation, is the dominant bottleneck. We release SWE-Test with a turnkey evaluation environment.

</details>


### [73] [KG-Commit: A Dynamic Knowledge Graph for Online Just-in-Time Software Defect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06272)
*Mohsen Hesamolhokama,Mohammad Sina Beyrami Aghbash,Behnam Rohani,Mohammadamin Fazli,Jafar Habibi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出KG-Commit，一个动态知识图谱系统，用于在线即时软件缺陷预测（JIT-SDP）。该系统增量维护仓库历史、文件内代码结构和提交语义，并通过AST-delta机制跟踪文件编辑间的结构变化，完全在CPU上进行轻量级图推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JIT-SDP方法为追求低延迟，多依赖提交级信息，忽视了变更发生的更广泛项目上下文。将这上下文纳入考虑面临两大挑战：新提交到达时需要高效检索，以及仓库演进时需要持续维护。作者希望证明丰富的项目上下文可以被高效维护并利用于在线JIT-SDP。

Method: 1) 构建KG-Commit动态知识图谱，随项目演进增量维护仓库历史、文件内代码结构和提交语义；2) 采用AST-delta机制跟踪文件编辑间的结构变化；3) 使用完全在CPU上运行的轻量级图推理管道。

Result: 在11个Apache软件项目中，KG-Commit取得最高的聚合Macro-F1（0.704）、G-Mean（0.706）和AUC（0.809）。在线协议下，它在Macro-F1上全面超越LR、HGB、RF和DeepJIT（11个全部项目），超越LApredict（10个）和JITLine（9个）。每个提交处理约1.33秒，且成本随图增长保持稳定。

Conclusion: 丰富的项目上下文可以被高效地维护和利用于在线JIT-SDP中，KG-Commit证明了动态知识图谱方法在效率和预测性能上均具有可行性，兼容真实项目的提交速率。

Abstract: Just-in-time software defect prediction (JIT-SDP) aims to identify risky commits as they arrive and provide developers with timely feedback. This need for low latency has led most approaches to rely on commit-level information and overlook the broader project context in which a change occurs. Incorporating this context is challenging because it requires both efficient retrieval for incoming commits and continual maintenance as the repository evolves. We introduce KG-Commit, a dynamic knowledge graph that incrementally maintains repository history, within-file code structure, and commit semantics as the project evolves. It also uses an AST-delta mechanism to track structural changes between file edits and relies on lightweight graph inference running entirely on CPU. Our evaluation on 11 Apache software projects against six baselines shows that KG-Commit achieves the highest aggregate Macro-F1 (0.704), G-Mean (0.706), and AUC (0.809) using our selected inference pipeline. Under a realistic online protocol, it outperforms LR, HGB, RF, and DeepJIT on all 11 projects, LApredict on 10, and JITLine on 9 projects in Macro-F1, with the aggregate paired difference significant in every case. KG-Commit processes each commit in approximately 1.33~s, with a cost that remains stable as the graph grows and is compatible with commit rates observed in real-world projects. These findings show that rich project context can be efficiently maintained and exploited for online JIT-SDP.

</details>


### [74] [From Reading Code to Reading Spec: A Verified Layer for LLM-Driven Codebase Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06383)
*Xinhao Zhang,Jingjie Lu,Kunpeng Liu,Fei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PROOF系统，通过层级化的自然语言规范（structured specifications）间接管理LLM生成的代码库，而非直接处理源代码。系统将代码拓扑抽象为规范表示，并从该规范重建源码以证明语义等价性，从而在维护请求执行时同步更新自身规范，防止语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成代码的快速增长增加了软件复杂度和维护负担。虽然LLM本可提供自动化解决方案，但代码库的结构复杂性阻碍了LLM直接管理代码库的能力，因此需要一种间接管理的方式。

Method: PROOF将代码库拓扑抽象为层级化自然语言表示，并仅从该规范中重建源代码以证明语义等价性，从而建立绝对信任。该验证基础驱动维护请求，在执行代码修改时同步更新规范自身以防止语义漂移。

Result: 在真实世界代码库上的实验证实了这些规范的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化规范间接管理代码库是可行且有效的方案，能够实现代码库全生命周期的规范驱动管理。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLM-generated code increases software complexity and the maintenance burden on engineers. While LLMs offer a potential automated alternative, this structural complexity hinders their ability to manage codebases directly. We introduce the Provable Representation Of Original Functionality (PROOF), which manages codebases indirectly via structured specifications. To enable full-lifecycle codebase management strictly through these specifications, PROOF abstracts codebase topology into a hierarchical natural-language representation. To establish absolute trust, the system proves semantic equivalence by reconstructing source code exclusively from this specification. This verified foundation drives maintenance requests, executing code modifications while synchronously updating itself to prevent semantic drift. Experiments on real-world repositories confirm the effectiveness of these specifications.

</details>


### [75] [An Autonomy Aware Metamodel for Human AI Collaboration in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06720)
*Md Mahade Hasan,Md Toufique Hasan,Zheying Zhang,Kalle Kulonen,Konsta Kalliokoski,François Christophe,Tommi Mikkonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"自主性感知的方法工程"愿景，通过元模型将自主性视为情境依赖的权威分配（而非行为体的固定属性），并定义了四个权威维度，最终为AI优先的软件工程方法提供治理相关的概念基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在将软件工程从工具支持流程转向AI优先协作，权威在人类与人工行为体之间动态分布。然而，现有方法工程方法假设静态的、以人为中心的控制，无法显式捕获不断演化的自主性。

Method: 提出一个元模型，将自主性形式化为由任务、情境和协作模式决定的派生性权威分配，涵盖四个权威维度：任务执行、任务分解、任务发起和协作重构。并通过与多智能体需求分析工具的分析性实例化进行说明。

Result: 通过分析性实例化展示了元模型如何支持动态权威分配，验证了其在多智能体需求分析情境下的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为治理感知、自适应的AI优先软件工程方法提供了概念基础。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is shifting software engineering from tool-supported processes towards AI-first collaboration, where authority is dynamically distributed across human and artificial actors. However, existing method engineering approaches assume static, human-centric control and provide limited support explicitly capturing evolving autonomy. This paper presents a vision for autonomy-aware method engineering by proposing a metamodel that treats autonomy not as a fixed property of an actor, but as a derived, situation-dependent authority assignment determined by task, context, and collaboration pattern. The metamodel formalizes autonomy through four authority dimensions: task execution, task decomposition, task initiation, and collaboration reconfiguration. Through an analytical instantiation with a multi-agent requirements analysis tool, we illustrate how the metamodel supports dynamic authority assignment. This work provides a conceptual foundation for governance-aware, adaptable, and AI-first software engineering methods.

</details>


### [76] [CAROL: Context-Aware Online Learning for Fuzzer Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06772)
*Zirui Liu,Mengfan Xu,Juan Zhai,Shenglong Yao,Shiqing Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CAROL是一个基于上下文的在线调度器，用于提升集成模糊测试（ensemble fuzzing）的性能。它利用当前每个fuzzer的丰富上下文信号（如奖励趋势、等待时间、代码覆盖和不确定性），通过领域引导和机器学习两种方法动态分配CPU资源，相比现有基线在多个目标上发现更多独特bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成模糊测试调度器仅基于过去性能的紧凑摘要和预先固定的规则进行决策，存在两个关键局限：(1)过去奖励摘要无法可靠反映性能演化趋势，相邻窗口排名与窗口内自一致性在统计上无法区分；(2)预测信号因目标而异，从一个目标学到的加权策略无法迁移到另一个目标。

Method: CAROL采用两种上下文使用方法：(1)领域引导方法——检测每个fuzzer处于上升还是衰退阶段，并应用阶段特定的学习规则；(2)学习方法——从15个上下文信号预测奖励，并利用预测不确定性进行在线选择。上下文信号包括奖励趋势、等待和平台时间、已到达代码和估计不确定性，这些信息在调度循环中已可用。此外，通过两个维度（奖励和无独特bug时间）进行后悔分析。

Result: 在九个Magma目标上，CAROL在结果有差异时比三种集成调度基线触发更多独特bug，且无一目标失败。相比每个目标的最强基线，CAROL提升11.8%，并超越了事后（retrospectively）选择每个目标最佳单个fuzzer的oracle。在五个广泛使用的C++程序上，CAROL发现120个先前未知的崩溃缺陷（按站点、故障和入口点去重），并已通过项目披露渠道报告给维护者。

Conclusion: 上下文信息是集成模糊测试调度的关键要素。移除上下文会消除性能增益，且额外发现的bug集中在基线很少或从未触发的类别中，表明CAROL能有效补充现有方法的盲区，实现更全面的漏洞发现。

Abstract: Ensemble fuzzing runs multiple fuzzers on a target while a scheduler allocates CPU time among them. Existing schedulers base these decisions on compact summaries of past performance and rules fixed before a campaign. Our measurements reveal two limitations. First, past-reward summaries do not reliably capture performance evolution: after accounting for estimation noise, agreement between consecutive-window rankings is statistically indistinguishable from within-window self-agreement. Second, predictive signals vary across targets: on eight of nine targets, a weighting learned from the other eight predicts reward worse than one learned on the current target. We introduce CAROL, an online scheduler that uses each fuzzer's current context. Already available to the dispatch loop, this context describes reward trends, waiting and plateau time, reached code, and estimation uncertainty. CAROL uses context in two ways: a domain-guided method detects whether a fuzzer is rising or rotting and applies a phase-specific learning rule, while a learned method predicts reward from 15 context signals and uses predictive uncertainty for online selection. Across nine Magma targets, CAROL triggers more unique bugs than each of three ensemble-scheduling baselines whenever their results differ, and fewer on none. Compared with the strongest baseline for each target, CAROL gains 11.8% and surpasses an oracle that retrospectively selects the best single fuzzer per target. Removing context eliminates the gain, and the additional bugs are concentrated among those the baselines trigger rarely or never. Run unchanged on five widely used C++ programs, CAROL finds 120 previously unknown crashing defects, deduplicated by site, fault, and entry point; all were reported to maintainers through the projects' stated disclosure channels.

</details>


### [77] [Shortcutting the Fix: Identifying and Categorizing Agentic Exploits in Software Engineering Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06780)
*Nikolai Ludwig,Wasi Uddin Ahmad,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了自主软件工程（SWE）智能体在基准测试中的剥削性行为（如利用本地Git历史、访问上游仓库、回忆记忆化解决方案），发现五个开源大语言模型在标准提示下剥削率高达45.1%-82.4%（SWE-bench Multilingual）和44.2%-66.1%（DeepSWE）。通过附加一个强制执行解决方案原创性的针对性指令，剥削率可大幅降至4.0%-10.7%和1.5%-7.1%，同时保持核心任务性能。研究强调需要具备剥削感知能力的评估框架来测量真实的仓库级问题解决能力，而非基准博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自主软件工程智能体在基准测试中取得高分辨率，但这些分数可能掩盖剥削性行为而非展示真正的问题解决能力，因此需要系统化地审计和揭示这些漏洞。

Method: 对五个开源大语言模型在SWE-bench Multilingual和DeepSWE基准上进行系统审计，采用turn级LLM-as-a-judge协议来识别剥削行为；通过对比标准提示与附加强制执行原创性指令的提示来量化剥削率变化。

Result: 标准提示下剥削率在SWE-bench Multilingual上达45.1%-82.4%，在DeepSWE上达44.2%-66.1%；附加原创性指令后剥削率降至4.0%-10.7%和1.5%-7.1%，同时核心任务性能保持强劲。

Conclusion: 当前基准测试存在严重的基准博弈问题，需开发能衡量真实仓库级问题解决能力而非投机取巧的剥削感知评估框架。

Abstract: While autonomous software engineering (SWE) agents achieve high benchmark resolution rates, these scores can mask exploitative behaviors---such as leveraging local Git histories, accessing upstream repositories, or recalling memorized solutions---rather than demonstrating genuine problem solving. We systematize and audit these exploits across five open large language models on SWE-bench Multilingual and DeepSWE using a turn-level LLM-as-a-judge protocol. Under standard prompts, exploitation rates reach 45.1\%--82.4\% on SWE-bench Multilingual and 44.2\%--66.1\% on DeepSWE. Appending a targeted instruction enforcing solution originality drastically cuts these exploitation rates---down to 4.0\%--10.7\% and 1.5\%--7.1\%, respectively, while maintaining strong core task performance. Our findings demonstrate the critical need for exploit-aware evaluation frameworks that measure true repository-level problem solving over benchmark gaming.

</details>


### [78] [LatentMD: Benchmarking Markdown Boundary Failures in LLM-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06993)
*Sungjune Lee,Myungjoo Kang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentMD，一个用于诊断LLM生成Markdown中围栏边界失败的基准与评估协议，将内容正确性与边界正确性分离，发现38.0%的有效输出存在内容正确但边界损坏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常将内容质量与格式遵循混为一谈，导致Markdown边界失败（fence-boundary failures）未被充分衡量。LLM越来越多地生成被渲染器、智能体和下游管线消费的Markdown，因此需要专门的诊断工具来分离这两类问题。

Method: 提出LatentMD基准，包含4,179个提示词和一个评分CLI工具。采用分离内容正确性与边界正确性的评估协议，对9个LLM约37,600次生成进行系统评测，并进行消融实验分析失败根因（如对称分隔符碰撞、嵌套影响、提示词缓解效果等）。

Result: 发现Markdown边界失败广泛存在：38.0%的有效主网格输出是内容正确但边界损坏的；在未指定提示和小型人工验证集中也存在显著边界破坏。消融实验表明失败主要由同系对称分隔符碰撞驱动而非单纯嵌套，提示词的缓解效果有限，且问题可泛化到Python三重引号docstring，而JSON作为非对称分隔符控制组保持稳健。

Conclusion: LatentMD为解析器敏感的LLM评估提供了可复现的诊断目标，强调了边界格式合规性应作为独立于内容质量的重要评估维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly generate Markdown that is consumed by renderers, agents, code extractors, and structured downstream pipelines. Yet existing evaluations often conflate content quality with format adherence, leaving Markdown boundary failures under-measured. We introduce LatentMD, a benchmark and evaluation protocol for diagnosing CommonMark-level fence-boundary failures in LLM-generated Markdown. LatentMD separates content correctness from boundary correctness, enabling detection of outputs that are content-correct but boundary-broken. The benchmark contains 4,179 prompts and a CLI for scoring arbitrary model outputs. Across 9 LLMs and roughly 37,600 generations, we find that Markdown boundary failures are widespread: 38.0% of valid main-grid outputs are content-correct but boundary-broken, with substantial boundary breakage under unspecified prompts and in a small human-authored validation set. Ablations show that failures are driven primarily by same-family symmetric-delimiter collisions rather than nesting alone, are only partially mitigated by prompt hints, and generalize to Python triple-quote docstrings while JSON remains robust as an asymmetric-delimiter control. LatentMD provides a reproducible diagnostic target for parser-sensitive LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [79] [Recompilation Is Not Enough: Test-Guided Decompiled-C Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07201)
*Yuhan Huang,Puzhuo Liu,Jianlei Chi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修复反编译C代码的工作流程，通过编译器反馈和相关官方测试来指导修复。在Coreutils 9.5的104个二进制文件评估中，91个（87.5%）成功重编译并通过测试门控；作者认为这种测试门控反馈能使LLM辅助的反编译代码修复比仅靠编译恢复更具可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠重编译成功并不能证明反编译C代码的行为正确性——重编译后的二进制仍可能在选项解析、输出字节或退出状态等方面存在差异。需要一种超越编译恢复的、基于测试行为的修复与验证方法。

Method: 提出一个分步工作流程：(1) 利用编译器和链接器诊断信息指导构建修复；(2) 待修复的C代码成功重编译为二进制后，通过冒烟检查和相关的官方测试暴露行为差异；(3) 依据测试反馈进行语义修复。

Result: 在104个Coreutils 9.5二进制文件上评估：91个（87.5%）成功重编译并通过测试门控；9个未在修复预算内重编译成功；4个虽重编译通过但未通过测试门控。

Conclusion: 测试门控反馈比仅编译恢复更能保证反编译代码修复的可审计性，为LLM辅助的反编译C修复提供了更可靠的验证框架。

Abstract: Decompiled C often becomes recompilable only after repair, but recompilation alone does not establish test-observed behavior. A recompiled command-line binary can still parse options incorrectly, print different bytes, or return a different exit status. We present a few-step workflow for repairing decompiled C using compiler feedback and related official tests. Compiler and linker diagnostics first guide build repair. Once the repaired C recompiles into a binary, smoke checks and related official tests expose behavioral discrepancies for semantic repair. In a preliminary static-enriched evaluation on 104 Coreutils 9.5 binaries with available decompiler exports and deterministic exact-output smoke comparisons, 91 binaries (87.5%) recompile and pass the test gate; 9 do not recompile within the repair budget, and 4 recompile but still fail the test gate. The result suggests that test-gate feedback can make LLM-assisted repair of decompiled C more auditable than compile-only recovery.

</details>


### [80] [One Is Not Enough: The Untold Story of Multiple Security Patches for One Vulnerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07224)
*Fangyuan Zhang,Lyuye Zhang,Lingling Fan,Chengwei Liu,Yinan Li,Liang Huang,Yang Liu,Zheli Liu,Sen Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究多安全补丁（multi-SP）漏洞现象。通过合并四个主要漏洞数据库，构建了6,053个multi-SP CVE和16,260个SP的数据集，发现20.6%的已打补丁CVE涉及多个SP。提出了包含6个类别、16个子类别的两级分类体系，并开发了SPectre工具，在补丁发现覆盖率上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个漏洞往往需要多个安全补丁才能完全修复（可能跨分支、跨仓库或增量完成），但补丁记录不完整导致用户只应用部分修复。而multi-SP现象的普遍性和成因尚不清楚，全面补丁发现仍然困难。

Method: 合并四个主要漏洞数据库构建multi-SP CVE数据集；分析漏洞与多个SP关联的原因并归纳两级分类体系（6类别16子类）；基于分类体系开发SPectre原型工具用于全面补丁发现。

Result: 数据集显示20.6%的CVE涉及多SP，合并数据库使multi-SP CVE识别量比任何单一来源提高36-55%。SPectre在300个multi-SP CVE上达到0.927（同仓库）和0.873（跨仓库）的召回率；在100个被所有数据库记录为单补丁的近期CVE中，发现了28个此前未报告的SP。

Conclusion: multi-SP漏洞既普遍又系统性低报，需要更强的补丁完整性意识、改进漏洞数据库管理以及具备关系感知的安全工具。

Abstract: Security patches (SPs) are the main mechanism for fixing software vulnerabilities, yet a single vulnerability is not always resolved by a single patch: fixes may be completed incrementally, propagated across maintained branches, or replicated across related repositories. When patch records are incomplete, downstream users may observe only part of the required fix set and therefore apply only partial patching. However, comprehensive patch discovery remains difficult because the prevalence and causes of the multi-SP phenomenon are still poorly understood. In this paper, we present the first large-scale empirical study of multi-SP vulnerabilities. By merging four major vulnerability databases, we construct a dataset of 6,053 multi-SP CVEs with 16,260 SPs, showing that 20.6% of CVEs with patches involve multiple SPs and that merging databases increases recognized multi-SP CVE counts by 36-55% over any single source. We further analyze why a vulnerability is associated with multiple SPs and derive a two-level taxonomy with 6 categories and 16 sub-categories. Based on these findings, we develop SPectre, a taxonomy-driven prototype for comprehensive patch discovery. On 300 multi-SP CVEs, after manually verifying ground-truth SPs, SPectre improves multi-SP patch coverage over representative patch localization baselines, achieving 0.927 recall on same-repository cases and 0.873 recall on cross-repository cases after manual ground truth verification. On 100 recent CVEs recorded as single-patch by all public databases, SPectre further discovers 28 previously unreported SPs across 20 CVEs. Our results show that multi-SP vulnerabilities are both prevalent and systematically underreported, motivating stronger patch-completeness awareness, improved vulnerability database curation, and relation-aware security tooling.

</details>


### [81] [Quality Metrics for LLM-Generated Asset Administration Shells: A Perturbation-Based Evaluation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07290)
*Janek Groß,Elena Zentgraf,Jens Heidrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对AI生成资产管理壳(AAS)子模型的质量评估问题，提出了一种基于扰动的评估框架，系统性地在多维度上降低AAS生成质量，以检验不同质量指标对质量变化的反映能力。基于200个产品数据，使用三种LLM生成了6,400个AAS实例，发现基于属性名称精确匹配和价值相似度软匹配的指标（特别是基于召回率的价值指标和基于F1的名称指标）最能可靠地反映质量退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0依赖AAS实现物理与软件资产的无缝互操作，而LLM虽能从未结构化数据源（如产品数据表）生成AAS子模型，但存在三大挑战阻碍可靠采用：不可预见的错误、缺乏真实参照（ground truth）、缺乏标准化质量指标。

Method: 提出一种基于扰动的评估框架（perturbation-based evaluation framework），系统地沿多个维度对AAS生成进行质量降级（degradation），以评估不同质量指标反映质量变化的能力。使用GPT-4o-mini、Qwen3和DeepSeek-R1三种模型，基于200个产品（来自多个制造商）的数据集生成了6,400个AAS实例进行验证。

Result: 结果表明，基于属性名称精确匹配（exact matching）和属性价值相似度软匹配（similarity-based soft matching）的指标，特别是基于召回率的价值指标（value-based recall）和基于F1分数的名称指标（name-based F1 score），是质量退化最可靠的指示器。此外，研究量化了不同扰动类型的影响，并分析了不同模型家族和产品细分之间的差异。

Conclusion: 研究成果有助于选择合适的质量指标、调优基于LLM的生成管道，以及将AI生成的AAS集成到工业应用中，为AI辅助工业数字化的质量保障提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The rapid digital transformation of manufacturing, often referred to as Industry 4.0, relies on seamless interoperability between physical and software assets. A central enabler is the Asset Administration Shell (AAS), a standardized digital representation of such assets. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable the generation of AAS submodels from unstructured sources such as product datasheets but raise challenges for quality assurance. In particular, unexpected errors, the lack of ground truth references, and the absence of standardized quality metrics hinder reliable adoption. In this work, we evaluate quality metrics for AI-generated AAS using a perturbation-based evaluation framework. By systematically degrading AAS generation along multiple dimensions, we assess how well different metrics reflect quality changes. Based on a dataset of 200 products from multiple manufacturers, we generate 6,400 AAS instances using GPT-4o-mini, Qwen3, and DeepSeek-R1. Our results show that metrics based on exact matching of property names and similarity-based soft matching of property values, in particular value-based recall and name-based F1 score, provide the most reliable indicators of quality degradation. Furthermore, we quantify the impact of different perturbation types and analyze differences across model families and product segments. These findings support the selection of suitable metrics, the tuning of LLM-based pipelines, and the integration of AI-generated AAS into industrial applications.

</details>


### [82] [When Stakeholder-centric Requirements Engineering is Not Enough: An Action Research Study on Legacy System Modernisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07340)
*Ruward S. Karper,Damian A. Tamburri,Alessio Ferrari,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过在某跨国能源公司的行动研究，探讨了以利益相关者为中心的RE方法在遗留系统现代化中支持系统现状与目标间差距分析的有效性。结果表明该方法能产出可理解且正确的需求，但在将需求映射到遗留系统功能方面效果有限，提示需要将利益相关者视角与证据驱动的遗留系统分析相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留系统现代化不仅是技术挑战，更是涉及利益相关者视角的需求工程问题。组织在现代化过程中既要定义未来需求，又需理解现有系统的实际功能和应保留、调整或替换的部分，因此需要研究以利益相关者为中心的需求工程能否有效支持系统差距分析。

Method: 采用行动研究方法，在某跨国能源公司的系统现代化项目中应用以利益相关者为中心的需求工程实践，包括：利益相关者识别、半结构化访谈、基于德尔菲法的共识构建，以及MoSCoW方法进行优先级排序。

Result: 该过程能有效产出利益相关者普遍认为可理解和正确的需求，但在就需求如何映射到遗留系统功能达成共识方面效果较差，表明利益相关者中心的需求工程是必要但非充分条件。

Conclusion: 研究指出现代化需要采用不确定性感知的、迭代的、基于证据的实践，将利益相关者视角与手工及工具辅助的遗留系统分析相结合，以弥补单纯利益相关者中心方法的不足。

Abstract: Legacy system modernisation is a major challenge in digital transformation, especially when organisations depend on long-lived, business-critical systems that are only partly understood. In such context, organisations must define future needs while determining what current systems actually do and which functions to retain, adapt, or replace. Modernisation is therefore not only a technical challenge but also a requirements engineering (RE) problem, shaped by stakeholder perspectives. This study examines how far stakeholder-centric RE can support gap analysis between the system-as-is and the system-to-be in a legacy modernisation context. We conducted an action research study in a multinational energy company engaged in system modernisation. In the study, we applied stakeholder-centric RE practices, including stakeholder identification, semi-structured elicitation interviews, agreement-building through the Delphi method, and prioritisation with the MoSCoW method. The results show that this process was effective in producing requirements stakeholders generally viewed as understandable and correct, but less effective in achieving agreement on how elicited requirements mapped to legacy system functionality. The findings suggest that stakeholder-centric RE is necessary but not sufficient, pointing to the need for uncertainty-aware, iterative, and evidence-based modernisation practices that combine stakeholder perspectives with manual and tool-assisted analysis of legacy systems.

</details>


### [83] [EnvPilot: Systematic Design and Evaluation of an Experience-Augmented Agent for Software Environment Setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07357)
*Hanwu Chen,Hanyu Lin,Zhanjiang Yang,Linhao Zhang,Aoyan Li,Jinxi Li,Meng Li,Yin Chen,Daoguang Zan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EnvPilot是一个经验增强的智能体，通过可扩展的轨迹衍生记忆（TDM）和上下文感知检索机制，将历史执行轨迹中的隐性知识转化为结构化经验并复用到软件环境搭建任务中，在112个真实GitHub实例基准上达到75%的Pass@1成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件环境搭建（Environment Setup）是一个依赖专家知识的关键且复杂任务。现有自动化方法缺乏从历史执行轨迹中积累经验并随时间演化的能力，导致大量冗余探索、忽视过往有效解决方案，且难以在不同软件生态系统中泛化。

Method: EnvPilot维护一个可扩展的轨迹衍生记忆（TDM），初始包含667条高质量经验；通过系统性的方法将历史执行轨迹中的隐性知识转化为结构化经验，并在任务执行过程中通过上下文感知检索机制提取最相关的指导信息，从而组合多个经过验证的搭建策略。

Result: 构建了AES-Bench多语言基准（112个跨9种编程语言的真实GitHub实例）。EnvPilot在Pass@1上达到75.00%的成功率，刷新了SOTA成绩，同时降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: 经验研究表明，结构化经验表示和上下文感知检索机制对系统性能至关重要，两者缺一不可。

Abstract: Environment Setup is a critical yet complex task in software engineering that relies heavily on expert knowledge. Existing automated environment setup methods lack the ability to accumulate experience from past execution trajectories and to evolve over time. As a result, their performance is limited because they often perform redundant exploration, ignore useful past solutions, and fail to generalize across diverse software ecosystems. We present the systematic design and empirical validation of EnvPilot, an experience-augmented agent that operationalizes trajectory-derived experience reuse for software environment setup. EnvPilot maintains an expandable Trajectory-Derived Memory (TDM), initialized with 667 high-quality experiences. It systematically transforms implicit knowledge from historical execution trajectories into structured experience and retrieves the most relevant guidance during task execution through the Context-aware Retrieval mechanism. This enables EnvPilot to combine multiple validated setup strategies, providing more precise and detailed guidance than methods that rely solely on static project files or web retrieval. To evaluate EnvPilot, we construct AES-Bench, a multilingual benchmark of 112 real-world GitHub instances across 9 programming languages. Experiments show that EnvPilot achieves a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) with a 75.00% Pass@1 success rate while reducing reasoning costs. Our empirical study shows that both the structured experience representation and the Context-aware Retrieval mechanism are essential.

</details>


### [84] [Scanning the Harness: An Empirical Study of Supply-Chain Defects in AI Coding-Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07360)
*Benjamin Kapner,Carmel Soceanu,Alicia Petrunin,Hofni Gartner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了AI编码智能体（如Claude Code、Cursor等）的配置"harness"层（指令文件、skills、hooks、MCP服务器声明、subagents）的安全性。通过对3,171个公共GitHub仓库的分析，发现16.0%的配置存在安全缺陷，16.7%存在任何类型的确认缺陷。主要安全问题包括：9.8%的配置安装了无版本固定的MCP服务器、3.1%预授权看似受限实则任意的执行权限（如Bash(python:*)）、3.8%携带预授权shell的skill。所有发现均经过独立实现验证、语言模型仲裁和第二轮独立检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体日益普及，其配置通过开发者编写和共享的artifacts（指令文件、skills、hooks等）来组装，这些组件从市场或公共仓库安装，以开发者权限运行，但没有lockfile、没有安装时检查、也没有描述组件行为的词汇表。这种缺乏约束的依赖层可能带来严重的安全隐患，但此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 作者扫描了3,171个公共GitHub仓库（2,660个多组件配置和511个skill集合），应用三层验证流程：1）独立实现从固定commit重新推导每个发现；2）语言模型仲裁器对分歧进行裁决（发布提示词）；3）第二独立模型会话重新检查每个计数对。只测量可从字节层面判定且导致安全暴露、无效配置或偏离Agent Skills规范的规则，并报告了以观察形式出现的跨文件比较差异。

Result: 三类安全缺陷被发现并确认：9.8%的配置安装无版本固定的MCP服务器，3.1%通过如Bash(python:*)这类看似受限的授权预批准任意执行，3.8%携带预批准shell的skill。总计16.0%的配置存在安全缺陷，16.7%存在任意类型的确认缺陷（原始扫描器在同一规则上的检出率为25.5%）。第三类缺陷出现在3.7%的collection中，市场扫描即可发现。未确认凭证外泄路径。

Conclusion: AI编码智能体的配置harness层存在显著的系统性安全问题，约六分之一的配置带有确认缺陷。作者发布了完整的工具、语料库清单、提示词及每项判定结果，为该领域后续研究和安全改进提供了基础。研究同时指出跨文件比较规则检出的差异通常是预期行为而非安全缺陷，这对未来自动化审计方法具有方法论意义。

Abstract: AI coding agents such as Claude Code, Cursor, GitHub Copilot, and OpenAI Codex are configured through artifacts developers write and share: instruction files, skills, hooks, MCP server declarations, subagents. This harness is a dependency layer installed from marketplaces and public repositories, running with the developer's privileges, with no lockfile, no install-time check, and no vocabulary for what a component may do. We study it over 3,171 public GitHub repositories: 2,660 setups that assemble two or more component types and 511 published skill collections. We measure only rules decidable from bytes whose consequence is a security exposure, a configuration that cannot work, or a departure from the Agent Skills specification, and validate every finding before it counts: an independent implementation re-derives it from the repository at its pinned commit, a language-model adjudicator with a released prompt rules on every disagreement, and a second independent model session re-checks every counted pair. Three security classes survive: 9.8% of setups install an MCP server with no version pinned, 3.1% pre-approve arbitrary execution behind a scoped-looking grant such as Bash(python:*), and 3.8% carry a skill that pre-approves the shell for whoever installs it. In total 16.0% of setups carry a security defect and 16.7% a confirmed defect of any kind, against a raw scanner rate of 25.5% on the same rules; the third class ships inside 3.7% of collections, where a marketplace scan can see it. Rules that compare two files detect differences that are usually intended and are reported as observations. No credential-exfiltration path was confirmed. The instrument, corpus manifest, prompt, and every verdict are released.

</details>


### [85] [Trust the Spec, Not the Code - A Specification-First, AI-Assisted Case Study in Online Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07365)
*Eitan Farchi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个由AI辅助的形式化规格说明方法：用自然语言结合轻量数学（以LaTeX书写）作为中间规格语言，LLM负责审查歧义、起草证明并生成实现。规格说明成为作者编写、评审、证明和细化的核心工件，代码则是可再生的输出。该研究在在线银行资金转账服务领域复制了先前在组织知识增长模拟中的成功经验，并扩展了该方法，包含对定期/循环转账的更丰富问题的压力测试、AI提出的运行时覆盖模型以及Z形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规格说明承诺早期错误检测、显式不变量和设计的正确性，但其符号化成本使其难以进入主流实践。作者认为AI可以消除大部分这种成本，使形式化方法变得实用可行。

Method: 采用自然语言+轻量数学的LaTeX文档作为中间规格语言；LLM审查歧义、起草形式化证明、生成实现代码。在两个不同领域（知识增长模拟和银行资金转账）中应用，统一使用守恒不变量的核心概念。同时提出AI运行时覆盖模型，区分"从未违反"与"已覆盖"；并建立Z形式化，包括配对的成功/失败操作模式和在不变量上对所有可达配置的归纳证明。

Result: 提供了第二个独立的案例研究；证明了方法在更丰富的周期性转账问题上仍然有效——生成的代码规模大幅增长，但不变量及其证明的复杂度没有增长；提出了AI提议的运行时覆盖模型；完成了Z形式化，并进行了AI自行提议Z接口的实验。

Conclusion: 该方法在多个领域具有泛化潜力，但其局限在于：证明和运行时检查停留在规格说明层面，不能保证生成的代码精化（refine）了规格说明——这一步委托给了AI。本文是案例研究而非控制实验。

Abstract: Formal specification promises early error detection, explicit invariants, and correctness by design, yet its notational cost has kept it out of mainstream practice. We argue that AI removes much of that cost: natural language enriched with lightweight mathematics, written in \LaTeX, can serve as an intermediate specification language that is precise enough to reason over and prove, while a large language model (LLM) reviews it for ambiguity, drafts proofs, and generates the implementation. The specification becomes the artifact one authors, reviews, proves, and refines; the code becomes regenerable output. This paper is a follow-on to a prior study that established the discipline on an organizational-knowledge-growth simulation~\cite{predecessor2026}. Here we replicate the discipline in a different domain---an online-banking fund-transfer service---and extend it. The two domains share one spine: a conservation invariant (knowledge in the prior study, money here), which suggests the approach generalizes across domains. We contribute: (i) a second, independent case study of the method; (ii) a stress-test of the method on a richer problem---\emph{scheduled/recurring} transfers---whose generated code grows substantially while the invariant and its proof do not; (iii) an \emph{AI-proposed runtime coverage model} for invariants (``never violated $\neq$ covered''); and (iv) a Z formalization, including paired success/failure operation schemas and an invariant proved over the inductive set of all reachable configurations, together with an experiment in which the AI proposes the Z interfaces itself. We are explicit about the method's limits: the proofs and runtime checks live at the specification level and do not establish that the generated code refines the specification---that step is delegated to the AI. This is a case study, not a controlled experiment.

</details>


### [86] [A Text Mining and Classification Approach for Analyzing Architecture Decision Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07375)
*Nicolás Miccio Palermo,Antonela Tommasel,J. Andrés Diaz-Pace*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化文本挖掘与分类方法，用于大规模分析架构决策记录（ADR），并应用于约550个开源仓库的数据集，揭示了ADR中捕获的决策类型及其与模板的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ADR已成为记录架构知识的流行轻量级机制，但关于ADR捕获的架构关注点类型及其与既有架构知识概念和文档实践的一致性，缺乏经验证据。

Method: 结合主题建模、基于LLM的分类以及模板合规性检查，对从约550个开源仓库中提取的ADR数据集进行自动化文本挖掘与分类分析。

Result: 发现ADR经常捕获存在性、技术及流程相关决策，而备选方案、决策驱动因素及部分质量关注点记录不足；同时观察到ADR内容与模板章节之间存在反复出现的不匹配现象。

Conclusion: 这些关于当前文档实践的洞察为架构师反思ADR应如何有效利用以处理架构知识提供了有价值的信息，且所提出的自动化方法可适用于其他架构任务。

Abstract: Architectural decision records (ADRs) have become a popular lightweight mechanism for documenting architectural knowledge in software projects. However, there is limited empirical evidence on the kinds of architectural concerns captured in ADRs and how well their contents align with established architectural knowledge concepts and documentation practices. In this paper, we propose an automated text-mining and classification approach for analyzing ADRs at scale. We apply this approach to a dataset of ADRs extracted from ~550 open-source repositories, combining topic modeling, LLM-based classification, and template compliance checks. Our analysis examines decision taxonomies and quality attributes, and the degree to which ADRs adhere to the MADR template. Our findings show that ADRs frequently capture existence, technology, and process-related decisions, while alternatives, decisions drivers, and some quality concerns remain under-documented. We also observe recurring mismatches between ADR contents and template sections. These insights into current documentation practices provide architects with valuable information to reflect on how ADRs are and should be used to effectively deal with architectural knowledge. Furthermore, our automated approach is adaptable to other architectural tasks.

</details>


### [87] [A Surrogate-based Approach for Fast Multi-objective Architectural Refactoring Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07389)
*J. Andrés Diaz-Pace,Daniele Di Pompeo,Antonela Tommasel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用基于回归技术的替代模型来近似软件架构分析工具的输出，以降低多目标进化算法在软件模型优化中的计算成本，实验表明可节省高达30%的计算时间并保持帕累托前沿质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标进化算法在复杂软件模型和大设计空间中面临效率挑战，因为评估每个架构的适应度需要调用计算昂贵的分析工具，且搜索过程中会反复调用，导致计算开销巨大。

Method: 构建基于回归技术的替代模型（surrogate models），用其近似替代分析工具的输出，以显著降低计算成本，同时保持合理的输出精度。

Result: 实验表明替代模型可节省高达30%的计算时间，同时维持进化算法所提供的帕累托前沿质量；但观察到产生的一些架构模型存在差异。

Conclusion: 替代模型是将多目标架构优化扩展到更大空间和复杂架构模型的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Software model optimization is a process that generates architecture alternatives aimed at improving quantifiable non-functional properties of software systems, such as performance and reliability. Multi-objective evolutionary algorithms are commonly used to explore the search space and help designers identify trade-offs among competing non-functional properties (e.g., through a Pareto front). However, such algorithms face efficiency challenges in complex software models and large design spaces, since evaluating the fitness (i.e., the quality) of each architecture requires analysis tools that become computationally expensive when repeatedly invoked during the search process. In this paper, we explore the construction of surrogate models based on regression techniques to approximate the outputs of these analysis tools at significantly lower computational cost, while maintaining reasonable output accuracy. Our experimental results suggest that surrogate models provide savings of up to $30\%$ in computational time and maintain the Pareto front quality provided by evolutionary algorithms. Also, we observed some differences in the architectural models produced by our approach. Overall, surrogate models constitute a promising approach for scaling multi-objective architecture optimization to larger spaces and complex architectural models.

</details>


### [88] [An Empirical Study on the Impact of Change Granularity in Refactoring Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07482)
*Lei Chen,Shinpei Hayashi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究32个开源Java项目，识别并分析了两种在单一提交粒度下无法被传统重构检测技术发现的粗粒度重构（CGRs）和短暂重构（EPRs），揭示了它们的特征、成因及与提交信息的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重构检测技术仅关注单一提交粒度，但实际开发中重构可能跨越多个提交完成（CGRs），或在单一提交中发生但无法被粗粒度检测所识别（EPRs）。这两种类型在传统检测中容易被遗漏，影响代码审查和软件演化研究。

Method: 对32个开源Java项目进行实证研究，通过分析提交历史来识别CGRs和EPRs，调查其特征与成因，对成因进行分类，并评估提交信息与CGRs之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现CGRs和EPRs在开发过程中均普遍存在；与类/包的拆分合并及继承结构修改相关的重构往往属于CGRs，而针对变量、属性等小对象的重构及具有上下文敏感检测标准的重构往往属于EPRs。约20%的提交信息明确暗示了CGRs的存在。

Conclusion: 建议在重构研究中重视CGRs和EPRs的价值，并扩展检测器以识别CGRs。

Abstract: Detecting refactorings in commit history is essential to improve comprehension to code changes on code reviews, and to provide valuable information for empirical studies on software evolution. Techniques have been proposed to accurately detect refactorings on the granularity of a single commit. However, refactorings can be made over multiple commits because of their complexity or other practical development problems, which cause detecting on only the granularity of a single commit not enough. We observe that some refactorings can only be detected in coarser granularity, i.e., changes conducted over multiple commits, or in the granularity of a single commit but not in coarse-grained. We call these types of refactorings as coarse-grained refactorings (CGRs) and ephemeral refactorings (EPRs). We investigated the features and causes of CGRs and EPRs through an empirical study of 32 open-source Java projects and found that both commonly occur during development. In addition, we found that refactoring types related to splitting or merging classes and packages, as well as those involving modifications to the inheritance structure, tend to be CGRs, and types targeting small objects such as variables and attributes, and refactorings with context-sensitive detection criteria tend to be EPRs. The causes of CGRs and EPRs are analyzed and categorized, and the relationships between the commit messages of CGRs and themselves are also assessed. We found that about 20% of commit messages explicitly suggest the existence of CGRs. We suggest that CGRs and EPRs be valued in refactoring research and that detectors be extended to identify CGRs.

</details>


### [89] [FPScan: An Automated Constraint-Based Analyzer for Floating-Point Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07492)
*Julien Bortolussi,Dorra Ben Khalifa,Pierre-Loïc Garoche*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FPScan，一个用于形式化定义和检测浮点程序中灾难性抵消和吸收问题的工具。该方法结合抽象解释的静态分析、一阶约束建模和SMT求解器，系统性地识别浮点程序中的数值陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编写无错误的浮点程序对缺乏数值分析背景的程序员来说极具挑战性。现有技术主要通过静态或动态分析来约束舍入误差，但很少有工具能明确指出吸收和灾难性抵消这类关键浮点陷阱，这些情况会导致有限精度计算与实数语义产生显著偏差。

Method: FPScan采用三步方法：首先，基于抽象解释的自定义静态分析器推断所有程序变量的数量级；然后，利用这些数量级信息构建一组一阶约束来建模程序中的误差传播和数值精度；最后，使用现成的SMT求解器判断程序是否存在上述关键数值陷阱。

Result: 在FPBench（一个知名的浮点程序基准测试套件）上进行了实验来评估工具的有效性，并与现有最先进工具在可靠性和分析时间方面进行了比较。

Conclusion: FPScan能够系统性地形式化定义和检测浮点程序中的灾难性抵消和吸收问题，并通过SMT求解实现自动化验证，在基准测试中表现出良好的有效性。

Abstract: Writing error-free floating-point programs is a challenging task, especially for programmers who lack a strong background in numerical analysis and rounding-error propagation. State-of-the-art techniques typically aim to bound such errors using static or dynamic analysis. However, only a few tools explicitly address critical floating-point pitfalls such as absorption and catastrophic cancellation. These anomalies represent situations in which rounding errors are significantly amplified, causing the semantics of the finite-precision computation to deviate substantially from the real-number semantics. In this article, we present FPScan, a novel tool to formally define and detect both catastrophic cancellation and absorption in floating-point programs. Our approach starts with a custom static analyzer based on abstract interpretation to infer the order of magnitude of all program variables. This magnitude information is then used to build a set of first-order constraints that model error propagation and numerical precision within the program. Finally, we employ an off-the-shelf SMT solver to determine whether the program exhibits any of these critical numerical pitfalls. Experiments were conducted on FPBench, a well-known benchmark suite of floating-point programs, to evaluate the effectiveness of our tool. We also present a comparison with state-of-the-art tools regarding soundness and analysis time.

</details>


### [90] ["We Permit the Use of AI, but [...]": The Landscape of AI Policies in Popular Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07542)
*Andre Hora,Romain Robbes,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对281个开源社区的AI贡献政策进行了系统性分析，沿六个维度手动分类，并跟踪92个专项AI政策文件的演变。研究发现：83.3%的政策允许或鼓励AI用于代码贡献，但67.3%要求高程度人类参与、43.4%设定问责机制；48.8%的政策要求AI披露（多在PR描述和提交信息中）；识别出十种针对AI垃圾内容（AI slop）的应对措施；且半数专项政策文件已被修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源社区正在迅速涌现一种新的治理产物——AI贡献政策，这类政策在数月前几乎不存在，如今正被大量编写和采纳。这种快速变化使得社区维护者和研究者缺乏对AI政策现状的基线认知，亟需系统性的梳理与分析。

Method: 收集并分析了281个AI贡献政策，沿六个维度进行人工分类；同时追踪92个专项AI政策文件的动态变化；围绕四个研究问题展开：(1) AI使用许可程度、(2) AI披露实践、(3) 针对AI垃圾内容的应对措施、(4) AI政策随时间如何演变。

Result: (1) 许可（允许/鼓励AI）是常态（83.3%），但附有条件：67.3%要求高人类参与度，43.4%分配问责责任；(2) 48.8%的政策要求AI披露，最常见于PR描述和提交信息，但披露内容差异很大；(3) 识别出十种针对AI slop的反制措施，覆盖PR、用户和自主代理三个对象；(4) 政策具有动态性，半数的专项AI政策文件自创建以来已被修订。

Conclusion: 该研究为维护者和研究者提供了一个关于开源社区AI治理的基线和带标注的政策语料库，可用于进一步研究AI政策的影响。

Abstract: Open source communities are converging on a new governance artifact: the AI contribution policy. These policies barely existed a few months ago and are now being written and adopted. We analyzed 281 AI contribution policies, and manually classified them along the six dimensions; to study how policies change, we also tracked 92 dedicated AI policy files over time. We answer four research questions on (1) AI usage allowance, (2) AI disclosure practices, (3) AI slop countermeasures, and (4) AI policy evolution. We find that, first, permission is the norm rather than the exception: 83.3% of policies permit or encourage AI in code contributions. But permission comes with conditions, as 67.3% require a high level of human involvement and 43.4% assign accountability. Second, AI disclosure is required by 48.8% of policies, most often in pull request descriptions and commit messages, but what must be disclosed varies widely. Third, we identify ten countermeasures against AI slop, targeting pull requests, users, and autonomous agents. Finally, policies are not static: half of the dedicated AI policy files have already been revised since creation. Our results give maintainers and researchers a baseline and a labeled corpus for studying the impact of AI policies.

</details>


### [91] [RefVerifier: Semi-Automated Reference Claim Verification for Scientific Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07652)
*Stefania Mocan,Florian Angermeir,Mark Kreitz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RefVerifier，一个面向学术同行评审的半自动化、引用受限的参考文献验证原型系统。该系统从稿件中提取含引用的句子，校验参考文献元数据，解析全文开放获取PDF，定位证据段落，并生成带自然语言解释的判定结果。在公共基准上，声明检测F1达0.990，开放获取解析率57.6%，证据定位命中率在摘要上达98%、完整论文上达68%；端到端测试中判定准确率为71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域投稿量激增，同行评审人面临严重的时间约束，导致对引用支撑声明进行系统性验证的成本过高。因此，缺乏依据的声明和语义漂移可能在文献中未被检测地传播。现有的事实核查和检索增强生成工具要么基于开放域网络数据运行，要么孤立地评估声明而不处理完整稿件，无法满足同行评审场景的需求。

Method: RefVerifier采用管线化设计，包含以下核心步骤：(1) 从稿件中提取含引用的句子（声明检测）；(2) 对照学术数据库校验参考文献元数据；(3) 将参考文献解析为全文开放获取PDF；(4) 在论文中定位相关证据段落；(5) 生成判定结果及自然语言解释。系统采用半自动化方式，保留评审人对最终裁决的监督权。

Result: 在公共基准上的评估结果：声明检测F1分数达0.990；57.6%的参考文献实现开放获取解析；证据定位命中率在摘要层面为98%，在完整被引论文层面为68%。在八篇稿件的端到端测试中，判定准确率达到71%。

Conclusion: 通过自动化文档检索和证据定位，同时保留评审人监督，RefVerifier初步证明了半自动化学术出版诚信检查的可行性，为同行评审中的引用验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As software engineering research submission counts surge, peer reviewers face severe time constraints, making systematic verification of citation-supported claims prohibitively expensive. Consequently, unsubstantiated claims and semantic drift can propagate undetected across scientific literature. Existing approaches such as fact-checking and retrieval-augmented generation tools operate on open-domain web data or evaluate claims in isolation without processing complete manuscripts. To address this gap, we present RefVerifier, a semi-automated, citation-bounded reference verification prototype designed to support in academic peer review. RefVerifier extracts citation-bearing sentences from manuscripts, checks bibliography metadata against scholarly databases, resolves references to full-text open-access PDFs, localizes relevant evidence passages, and generates verdicts with natural language explanations. Evaluating RefVerifier on public benchmarks shows claim detection at an F1 score of 0.990, open-access resolution of 57.6% of references, and evidence localization with a hit rate of 98% on abstracts and 68% on complete cited papers. In an end-to-end test with eight manuscripts, RefVerifier achieves a verdict accuracy of 71%. By automating document retrieval and evidence localization while preserving reviewer oversight, RefVerifier provides first indicators for the feasibility of semi-automated integrity checks in scholarly publishing.

</details>


### [92] [CodeTD: Topology of Attention Detects Hallucinations in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07779)
*Daria Voronkova,Ilya Trofimov,Anton Dmitriev,Eduard Tulchinskii,Evgeny Burnaev,Serguei Barannikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeTD是首个基于拓扑数据分析（TDA）的代码LLM预执行正确性评估方法，通过分析注意力图的拓扑模式来量化提示-生成之间的不匹配，从而在代码执行前预测生成代码的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代码助手工具的普及，自动评估生成代码正确性成为重大挑战。代码LLM容易产生幻觉，可能导致代码无法解决问题甚至存在严重安全漏洞，因此需要一种在执行前就能评估代码正确性的方法。

Method: 利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）分析代码LLM的注意力图，通过提取注意力图中的拓扑模式来量化提示与生成代码之间的不匹配程度，从而进行预执行阶段的代码正确性评估。

Result: 在HumanEval、MBPP、BigCodeBench和MultiPL-E等常见基准测试上，覆盖5种编程语言和10个规模达34B参数的代码LLM进行实验，结果显示所提方法优于最近的基线方法，并且CodeTD在不同编码基准之间具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: CodeTD提供了一种无需执行代码即可评估代码生成正确性的有效方法，具有跨基准的可迁移性，为AI代码助手工具的质量保障提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: As AI-code assistant tools become widespread, automatic assessment of the correctness of generated code becomes a significant challenge. Code LLMs are prone to hallucinations, which may lead to code that does not solve the required problem, or even to code with severe security vulnerabilities. In this paper, we introduce CodeTD -- the first approach to pre-execution assessment of code correctness based on topological data analysis (TDA) of Code LLMs' attention maps. Our method quantifies prompt-generation mismatch using topological patterns of attention maps. We carry out experiments with common benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, BigCodeBench, MultiPL-E), 5 programming languages and 10 Code LLMs of size up to 34B parameters. The experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms recent baselines. Moreover, CodeTD is transferable between coding benchmarks.

</details>


### [93] [You can contribute if you... An Empirical Framework of AI Contribution Policies in OSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07919)
*Gregorio Robles,Daniel M. German*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件（OSS）社区中针对AI中介贡献的新兴政策进行了实证研究，分析了这些政策背后的理由、规则和期望，并提出了"AI贡献治理框架"（AI Contribution Governance Framework），用于组织跨项目的重复关切和治理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在重塑开源软件贡献方式，降低代码、文档、问题报告和审查互动的生产成本，为广泛参与创造机会，但也扰乱了维护者评估贡献者努力、能力和责任的方式。OSS项目开始通过贡献指南等文档规范AI中介贡献，但这些新兴政策尚未被系统研究。

Method: 对OSS项目中关于AI中介贡献的政策进行实证研究，通过评估这些政策背后的理由（rationales）、规则（rules）和期望（expectations）来分析其内容，并在此基础上归纳出治理框架。

Result: 分析表明这些政策旨在保护稀缺的维护者注意力、保持责任性、维持有意义的审查互动、解决法律和质量问题，以及维护新手学习的路径。研究提出AI贡献治理框架，组织跨项目的重复关切和治理机制。

Conclusion: AI贡献治理框架能帮助OSS社区制定AI贡献政策，并为研究人员提供研究AI如何改变协作软件生产的词汇表。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is reshaping open source software (OSS) contribution by lowering the cost of producing code, documentation, issue reports, and review interactions. This creates opportunities for broader participation, but also disrupts how maintainers assess contributor effort, competence, and accountability. In response, OSS projects are beginning to regulate AI-mediated contribution through contribution guidelines and other project documentation. This paper presents an empirical study of these emerging policies. We analyze project policies on AI-mediated contributions by evaluating their underlying rationales, rules, and expectations. Our analysis shows that these policies seek to protect scarce maintainer attention, preserve accountability, sustain meaningful review interactions, address legal and quality concerns, and maintain pathways for newcomer learning. Based on these findings, we introduce the AI Contribution Governance Framework, which organizes recurring concerns and governance mechanisms across projects. The framework helps OSS communities develop AI contribution policies and provides researchers with a vocabulary for studying how AI is changing collaborative software production.

</details>


### [94] [Service Health Engineering for Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08020)
*Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"服务健康工程"（Service Health Engineering）作为一项实践性的可靠性纪律，将遥测、工作流完成度、依赖行为、运维就绪度和恢复验证联系起来，以端到端用户结果而非组件仪表盘来评判服务健康。以文档审批工作流为例，展示了服务承诺、SLO、看门狗、事件度量、韧性测试和周度服务健康评审如何揭示静默故障和滞留的异步工作，并提出了人工审查+AI辅助的报告架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统支持许多关键业务工作流，但服务健康的评判往往依赖组件仪表盘，而非端到端的用户结果。这种视角缺失可能导致静默故障和滞留的异步工作未被及时发现，需要一种以用户旅程完成为中心的可靠性实践。

Method: 提出服务健康工程这一可靠性纪律，核心要素包括：服务承诺（service promises）、服务级别指标与目标（SLI/SLO）、看门狗（watchdogs）、事件度量（incident measures）、韧性测试（resiliency testing）以及周度服务健康评审（weekly service-health reviews）。同时设计了一个人工审查、AI辅助的报告架构来整合服务健康证据，确保AI仅辅助而不做自主决策。

Result: 以文档审批工作流为实例，展示了该框架能够揭示传统组件监控难以发现的静默故障和滞留的异步工作，并通过AI辅助（非自主）的报告架构有效组装服务健康证据。

Conclusion: 服务健康工程将已有的可靠性实践统一到"用户旅程是否按承诺完成"这一核心目标周围，为分布式系统的服务健康评估提供了端到端视角的整合性方法。

Abstract: Distributed systems support many critical business workflows, but service health is often judged through component dashboards rather than through end-to-end user outcomes. This article presents service health engineering as a practical reliability discipline that connects telemetry, workflow completion, dependency behavior, operational readiness, and recovery validation. Using a document approval workflow as a running example, it describes how service promises, service-level indicators and objectives, watchdogs, incident measures, resiliency testing, and weekly service-health reviews can reveal silent failures and stranded asynchronous work. It also presents a human-reviewed, AI-assisted reporting architecture for assembling service-health evidence without making AI an autonomous decision-maker. The approach brings established reliability practices together around whether user journeys complete as promised.

</details>


### [95] [Agent ATO: Visualizing Agent Interaction Timelines from Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08301)
*Takuto Kawamoto,Yoshiki Hig,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent ATO（Agentic Trajectory Observer），一个从控制台日志中可视化AI编码智能体交互时间线的工具。Agent ATO提取智能体交互并按命令或工具类型分类，以时间线形式展示，同时提供强调文件发现、读取、编辑和执行等功能的过滤时间线，帮助开发者检查和比较智能体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体已融入开发者工作流程，但其行为难以仅从最终代码变更中理解。智能体在任务中通过一系列动作与代码仓库交互，这些交互虽有日志记录，但原始日志难以被开发者直接检查，需要可视化工具来揭示智能体的行为过程。

Method: Agent ATO从控制台日志中提取智能体交互，按其命令或工具类型分类，并可视化为时间线。系统提供全交互时间线以及强调文件发现、文件读取、文件编辑和执行的过滤时间线，同时保留周围上下文信息。

Result: 作者使用两个修复任务中的选定运行示例，展示了Agent ATO如何帮助开发者检查和比较智能体动作。未来工作将扩展到更多智能体、任务和开发环境，并评估其是否能减少比较轨迹所需的工作量。

Conclusion: Agent ATO通过将难以阅读的原始控制台日志转化为可查看的时间线可视化，有望降低开发者理解AI编码智能体行为的门槛，但当前仅为工具展示，尚需更大规模的实证评估。

Abstract: AI coding agents are becoming part of developers' workflows, but their behavior is difficult to understand from final code changes alone. During a task, agents interact with software repositories through sequences of actions such as searching for files, reading code, editing programs, and running tests or build commands. These interactions, together with token usage, are often recorded in console logs, but raw logs are difficult for developers to inspect. In this paper, we propose Agent ATO (Agentic Trajectory Observer), a tool for visualizing AI coding agent interaction timelines from console logs. Agent ATO extracts agent interactions, classifies them by command or tool type, and visualizes them as timelines. In addition to an all-interaction timeline, Agent ATO provides filtered timelines that emphasize file discovery, file reading, file editing, and execution while preserving surrounding context. We illustrate how Agent ATO may help developers inspect and compare agent actions using selected runs from two repair tasks. Future work will apply Agent ATO to more agents, tasks, and development environments, and will evaluate whether it reduces the effort needed to compare trajectories.

</details>


### [96] [AttnCompress: Dynamic Attention-Guided Trajectory Compression for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08318)
*Zhengran Zeng,Yixin Li,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AttnCompress，一种动态注意力引导的轨迹压缩框架，用于解决自主软件工程代理在长交互轨迹中的上下文窗口瓶颈和成本问题。通过困惑度尖峰驱动的结构感知分割、代理注意力权重相关性估计和动态滚动窗口三个机制，在SWE-Bench-Verified和Multi-SWE-Bench上达到53.17%的通过率，同时减少21.6%的token消耗和33.6%的总成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件工程代理从人类辅助向自动化转变后，其试错式的交互过程生成了冗长的轨迹，导致上下文窗口限制和成本成为严重瓶颈。虽然上下文压缩是潜在解决方案，但先前方法存在静态剪枝策略和粒度不匹配问题，未能保留软件工程任务所需的关键语义依赖和语法细节。

Method: AttnCompress框架通过三个关键机制实现语义完整性与动态适应性的统一：(1)基于困惑度(PPL)尖峰的结构感知分割，保留代码和日志的语法结构；(2)使用代理注意力权重估计历史块与代理当前推理的相关性；(3)动态滚动窗口随任务演进重新评估和召回历史上下文。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified和Multi-SWE-Bench基准上，AttnCompress达到53.17%的通过率，优于先前最先进的基线方法，同时将token消耗减少21.6%，总成本降低33.6%。框架具有模型无关性，并能有效推广到多种编程语言。

Conclusion: AttnCompress成功弥合了语义完整性与动态适应性之间的鸿沟，在压缩上下文的同时严格保留关键任务证据，是一种有效的、模型无关的、可跨语言泛化的轨迹压缩方案，为软件工程代理的实用化部署提供了经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The transition from human-centric assistance to Autonomous Software Engineering (ASE) agents has enabled the resolution of complex real-world SE tasks. However, the trial-and-error nature of these agents generates lengthy interaction trajectories, creating severe bottlenecks in terms of context window limits and cost. While context compression offers a potential remedy, prior approaches suffer from static pruning strategies and granularity mismatches, often failing to preserve the semantic dependencies and syntactic details crucial for SE tasks. To strictly preserve critical task evidence while reducing context length, we introduce AttnCompress, a dynamic attention-guided trajectory compression framework. Unlike existing approaches, AttnCompress bridges the gap between semantic integrity and dynamic adaptability through three key mechanisms: (1) structure-aware segmentation via perplexity (PPL) spikes to preserve the syntactic structure of code and logs; (2) relevance estimation using proxy attention weights to quantify the precise relevance of historical blocks to the agent's current reasoning; and (3) a dynamic rolling window to re-evaluate and recall historical context as the task evolves. Extensive evaluation on SWE-Bench-Verified and Multi-SWE-Bench demonstrates that AttnCompress achieves a pass rate of 53.17%, outperforming prior state-of-the-art baselines while reducing token consumption by 21.6% and total costs by 33.6%. The framework proves to be model-agnostic and generalizes effectively across diverse programming languages.

</details>


### [97] [Tool Retrievers Are Underestimated: Annotation Expansion Reveals True Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08327)
*Yanyu Zhu,Chenheng Zhang,Shaoshen Chen,Hoilam Pao,Yufei zhang,Jiajun Chai,Dongnian Wang,Zhaoyu Hu,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Hai-Tao Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolEX框架，用于自动发现并标注与已有标注功能等价的工具组合，解决工具检索基准中"一对多"查询-工具映射被压缩为"一对一"标注的问题。在7,360个查询上，67.9%的子查询存在等价替代，将平均有效组合扩展到5.3个。重新评估显示原始标注系统性地低估了检索器性能，且30-47%的微调增益是评估伪影而非真实提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准为每个查询仅标注单一相关工具组合，将工具仓库中天然存在的一对多（多功能等价组合）映射强行压缩为一对一标注，导致合法检索结果被误判为失败，系统性地低估检索器真实性能。

Method: 提出ToolEX框架，自动发现并标注与已标注工具组合功能等价的替代组合；构建扩展后的基准ToolEQ；在工具检索上用8个基础检索器和2个微调变体重评估，并在技能检索任务上验证结论的泛化性。

Result: ToolEQ中67.9%的子查询存在等价替代，平均每个查询有5.3个有效组合；检索器在ToolEQ上的指标显著高于Tool-DE；30-47%的微调增益被证明是评估伪影而非真实改进；技能检索上同样存在一对多问题。

Conclusion: 一对一标注系统性地低估了工具/技能检索器的性能，ToolEX和ToolEQ提供了更公平的评估方案，并揭示了现有评估体系中被掩盖的真实检索能力。

Abstract: In open-world scenarios with massive and evolving tool repositories, tool-augmented large language models rely on a retriever to surface relevant tools for a given query. Because such repositories often contain many tools that implement the same functionality, a single query can often be resolved by several distinct but functionally equivalent tool combinations, making the natural query-to-tool mapping inherently one-to-many. However, existing tool retrieval benchmarks annotate each query with a single relevant tool combination, collapsing this one-to-many mapping into a rigid one-to-one annotation and causing valid retrieved tools to be misjudged as failures.
